===== docs/scratch/arrival-break/sdk-conformance-two-observer-2026-09-06.md =====
1: # Public SDK conformance: two observers and descriptor relocation
2: 
3: Status: implemented and natively validated; Fable-low review pending on
4: `arrival/finish`, based on
5: `a36487dc`. This is the first workload-conformance slice preceding C9's
6: SDK/minimal-CLI transition and deliberate legacy retirement.
7: 
8: ## Contract under test
9: 
10: An application prepares two explicit mapped bindings, initializes Arrival with
11: one observer, introduces the other's public key, and emits a mixed-author batch.
12: The write receipts, public fact reads and prefix
13: verification must describe the same lineage and accepted history. Observer
14: labels select the corresponding public authorization; the explicit local
15: namespace selects custody material independently of the descriptor filename.
16: 
17: Moving and renaming the descriptor while retaining an absolute store location
18: must preserve the selected residence, lineage, history and mapped keys. A newly
19: constructed provider must support a subsequent authorized write from the moved
20: descriptor. This does not imply that moving a descriptor relocates its store,
21: rewrites relative locations or adopts another lineage.
22: 
23: `verify_target` verifies grammar, dense ordinals, lineage and the hash chain
24: through its returned prefix. It does not establish signature validity, key
25: trust or projection agreement. The workflow compares read and write evidence
26: explicitly; existing mapped-signing tests separately cover domain-separated
27: signatures and the engine's captured authorization checks.
28: 
29: ## Scope and evidence
30: 
31: Sol owns the executable SDK workflow, Terra independently audits relocation and
32: updates public guidance, Luna validates coverage and package behavior, and root
33: integrates the result and triages the frozen Fable 5.1 LOW review. Every test
34: process isolates XDG state/config and `LOOPS_HOME`; all stores and keys are
35: disposable fixtures. No live migration or legacy retirement is part of this
36: slice.
37: 
38: The executable
39: [workload](../../../libs/sdk/tests/test_arrival_mapped_workload.py)
40: chains initialization, grant, batch and final-write heads. It checks current
41: projection evidence against captured custody, exact returned authors/payloads,
42: and the unchanged history and persisted key references after descriptor
43: relocation. Batch receipts share the enclosing result's captured head, witness
44: and projection evidence; the shared commit belongs to the batch result.
45: 
46: Three refusal controls exercise the same lifecycle: Bob's binding before his
47: grant, another namespace's Alice key, and an independently bound `Alice` label
48: that was never granted. Refusals retain the available typed evidence and leave
49: the verified head unchanged. A final successful write and exact fact listing
50: also check that those attempted facts never entered the query result.
51: 
52: No production defect has been found by this pass. The changes are executable
53: conformance coverage and public SDK guidance.
54: 
55: ## Validation and review
56: 
57: - Final full SDK suite: **579 passed**, 20.88 seconds.
58: - Architecture suite: **101 passed**, 5.49 seconds.
59: - Existing mapped-credential tests plus the new workload: **13 passed**.
60: - Scoped Ruff and maintained-source whitespace checks: passed.
61: 
62: The workload uses public SDK operations and the public credential-request
63: types. It opens no engine ledger or private custody file directly. A descriptor
64: rename and byte comparison express the caller's relocation, while custody
65: preparation remains explicit. This bounded flow exercises fact reads and prefix
66: verification, not every SDK read surface or concurrent/multi-machine relocation.
67: Existing domain-signature tests supply complementary coverage, rather than a
68: claim that `Full` cryptographically authenticates authorship.
69: 
70: Frozen Fable-low review and primary disposition will be linked here when
71: complete. No source or test change is committed or pushed by this slice.
72: 
73: ## Next bounded workflows
74: 
75: 1. Interrupt a declaration edit, recover, inspect, and continue writing.
76: 2. Run sources with partial failure, inspect persisted tiers, and identify what
77:    can safely resume.
78: 3. Export a captured prefix, restore forward, verify, explicitly sync, and read.
79: 
80: Maintenance/transfer completion, adoption and C9 retirement remain separate
81: roadmap work; passing this workflow does not close them.

===== libs/sdk/README.md =====
1: # sdk — Loops Apex Composition Library
2: 
3: `sdk` is the headless composition layer uniting `engine`, `custody`, `lang`, `store`, `atoms`, and `sign` into unified, typed operations. It serves as the single foundation for presentation layers (`apps/loops`, TUI, external agents, Python scripts) without leaking presentation logic into the core substrate.
4: 
5: ---
6: 
7: ## Architectural Guarantees
8: 
9: - **Zero Presentation Logic**: Returns pure typed dataclasses; never formats terminal escape codes or parses CLI flags.
10: - **Descriptor-First Arrival Reads**: An explicitly declared backend and instance role select the adapter; store locations remain opaque to the SDK.
11: - **Visible Legacy Boundary**: Bare `.jsonl`/SQLite inputs remain transitional and every read result labels its path as `legacy` with `basis=None`.
12: - **Witness-Axis Honesty**: Fact listing and pagination use append-order cursors (`WitnessPosition`) ensuring deterministic, non-lossy walks.
13: - **Declared Admission & Attestation**: Fact emission enforces declared observer and kind admission policies, signing with operation-fresh custody keys.
14: - **AST-Verified Ceremonies**: Declarative vertex modifications are verified against the language grammar and executed via transactional plan/apply ceremonies.
15: 
16: ---
17: 
18: ## API Reference
19: 
20: ### 1. Target Resolution & Discovery
21: 
22: ```python
23: from sdk import resolve_arrival_target, resolve_target, discover_targets, TargetInfo
24: 
25: # Supported Arrival target: the descriptor carries backend, opaque location,
26: # optional lineage pin, and mandatory instance role.
27: arrival = resolve_arrival_target("path/to/target.vertex")
28: # -> ArrivalTarget(store=StoreDescriptorInfo(backend='file', location='...', role='replica'))
29: 
30: # Single target probe
31: info = resolve_target("path/to/target.vertex")
32: # -> TargetInfo(target_type='vertex', canonical_mode='sqlite', canonical_path=..., index_path=..., exists=True, ...)
33: 
34: # Multi-target workspace discovery
35: targets = discover_targets(".", recursive=True)
36: ```
37: 
38: - **`resolve_target(target: Path | str) -> TargetInfo`**:
39:   Transitional filesystem probe for legacy targets. Raises `TargetNotFound` or `TargetUnsupported`.
40: - **`resolve_arrival_target(target: Path | str) -> ArrivalTarget`**:
41:   Parses an explicit vertex descriptor without probing or normalizing its backend-owned location. It refuses suffix inference and an omitted instance role.
42: - **`discover_targets(root_path=".", *, recursive=True, include_bare=True) -> list[TargetInfo]`**:
43:   Discovers all Loops vertices and bare stores in a directory tree.
44: 
45: ---
46: 
47: ### 2. Read Operations
48: 
49: ```python
50: from sdk import (
51:     read_summary,
52:     read_facts,
53:     read_state,
54:     read_ticks,
55:     read_fact_by_id,
56:     search_facts,
57:     resolve_entity,
58:     read_timeline,
59:     sync_target,
60: )
61: 
62: # Target statistical summary & kind inventory
63: summary = read_summary("target.vertex")
64: # -> ReadSummary(read_path='arrival', basis=ReadBasis(...), store=StoreDescriptorInfo(...), ...)
65: 
66: # Bounded, cursor-bearing fact pagination
67: page = read_facts("target.vertex", limit=10, order="newest")
68: # -> FactPageResult(items=[...], next_cursor=..., truncated=True, order='newest')
69: 
70: # Arrival continuations are process-local capabilities. Pass next_cursor back
71: # to read_facts() directly; as_dict() reports has_continuation=True and redacts
72: # the engine value instead of emitting an unprotected wire token.
73: 
74: # Folded vertex state
75: state = read_state("target.vertex", kind="note")
76: # -> FoldStateResult(vertex_name='target', sections={'note': {'count': 10, 'items': [...]}})
77: 
78: # Full-text search across observation payloads
79: search_res = search_facts("target.vertex", query="refactor auth", limit=10)
80: # -> SearchResult(query='refactor auth', matches=[...], total_matches=1)
81: 
82: # Domain entity fold-key resolution
83: fact_id = resolve_entity("target.vertex", kind="task", key="task_id", value="T-100")
84: 
85: # Interleaved chronological timeline (facts + ticks)
86: timeline = read_timeline("target.vertex", limit=50)
87: 
88: # Explicit projection synchronization. Arrival sync first attests custody and
89: # catches a missing or behind projection up through the captured head.
90: sync_res = sync_target("target.vertex")
91: # -> SyncResult(read_path='arrival', captured_head=..., target=...,
92: #               projected_before=..., projected_after=..., changed=True, ...)
93: 
94: # Tick records
95: ticks = read_ticks("target.vertex", name="default")
96: for tick in ticks.items:
97:     print(tick["name"])
98: 
99: # Fact lookup by full ID or prefix
100: lookup = read_fact_by_id("target.vertex", "01M01...")
101: fact = lookup.fact
102: ```
103: 
104: `read_summary`, `read_facts`, `read_state`, `read_ticks`, and
105: `read_fact_by_id` always return typed result models. Descriptor-first results
106: carry the custody head and represented projection prefix in `basis`; legacy
107: results carry `read_path="legacy"` and `basis=None`. Descriptor reads require a
108: current projection and never materialize, repair, or catch it up. Search,
109: entity resolution, and timelines carry the same captured basis. Aggregate
110: state, summary and timeline results retain per-occurrence member bases rather
111: than inventing one shared head. `sync_target` is the
112: explicit maintenance boundary: it may catch up a missing or behind Arrival
113: projection, while inconsistent existing state and rebuild requests refuse.
114: 
115: Arrival read operations, including `resolve_entity`, `read_timeline`, and
116: `inspect_declaration`, accept an optional keyword-only `registry=` for custom
117: `engine.arrival_registry.BackendRegistry` instances. Omission selects the
118: built-in backends. A supplied registry handles every supported root and member
119: Arrival open; unknown backends refuse without fallback, and non-file locations reach
120: the named adapter unchanged. Timeline reuses its captured root when the
121: effective declaration selects aggregation. Entity resolution and declaration
122: inspection retain their current Arrival aggregate restrictions.
123: Legacy targets keep their existing behavior and do not consult the registry.
124: 
125: `export_target("target.vertex", "snapshot.jsonl")` writes an exact captured
126: wire prefix and returns its manifest, selected/captured heads and byte count.
127: It refuses to replace an existing output. A late source failure publishes no
128: partial artifact; a directory-sync failure after publication raises
129: `ExportPublicationError` with `published=True` and the complete artifact's
130: identity. Export requires no current query projection. Import and replication
131: are still pending their explicit initialization/restoration boundary.
132: 
133: ---
134: 
135: ### 3. Fact Emission
136: 
137: ```python
138: from sdk import BatchEmitResult, EmitReceipt, emit_batch, emit_fact, preview_emission
139: from atoms import Fact
140: 
141: # Emitting by kind and payload
142: receipt = emit_fact(
143:     "target.vertex",
144:     kind_or_fact="note",
145:     payload={"title": "Meeting Notes", "body": "Discussed SDK API"},
146:     observer="alice",
147: )
148: # Descriptor-first Arrival receipts carry the captured head, durable Commit,
149: # witness state, and post-commit projection status.
150: 
151: # Or preflight dry-run simulation
152: preview = preview_emission("target.vertex", "note", {"title": "Test"}, observer="alice")
153: 
154: # A descriptor-first batch is planned from one snapshot and uses one append.
155: batch = emit_batch(
156:     "target.vertex",
157:     [fact, ("note", {"title": "Batch item"})],
158:     observer="alice",
159: )
160: for receipt in batch.items:
161:     print(receipt.id, receipt.stored)
162: print(batch.commit, batch.atomicity, batch.projection)
163: ```
164: 
165: - **`emit_fact(target, kind_or_fact, payload=None, *, observer=None, origin="", ts=None, id_override=None, credentials=None, admit_undeclared=False, dry_run=False, registry=None) -> EmitReceipt`**:
166:   Emits a fact (or `Fact` atom) under declared admission rules. Descriptor-first Arrival writes prepare from one CURRENT snapshot, append once against its captured full head, witness the commit, then explicitly synchronize the projection. Bypassing strict kind admission requires `admit_undeclared=True`. Deterministic IDs can be specified with `id_override`. `dry_run=True` simulates emission without storage.
167: - **`preview_emission(target, kind_or_fact, payload=None, *, observer=None, origin="", ts=None, admit_undeclared=False, credentials=None, registry=None) -> EmitPreviewResult`**:
168:   Simulates emission against declared policies, checking fold key requirements without disk side effects. Arrival policy and fold metadata come from the same bounded effective declaration used for planning; a missing projection refuses without materializing it.
169: - **`emit_batch(target, facts, *, observer=None, origin="", credentials=None, admit_undeclared=False, registry=None) -> BatchEmitResult`**:
170:   Normalizes every item before resolving the target. A descriptor-first Arrival batch derives all per-item admission decisions from one current snapshot, appends the packed records under one head comparison, witnesses one shared commit, and explicitly synchronizes the projection. Each mapping item may set its own boolean `admit_undeclared`; duplicate receipts retain `stored=False`. Empty input is an explicit zero-write result. Legacy targets return the same wrapper with `atomic=False`, `atomicity="legacy-sequential"`, and no invented shared commit; `LegacyBatchPartialFailure` retains receipts if that sequential loop stops after an observable prefix.
171: 
172: `CustodyCredentialProvider()` and `CustodyCredentialProvider(key_dir=None)` use
173: the existing custody resolver. A non-`None` `key_dir` now raises
174: `SdkValueError` during construction because directory overrides were previously
175: accepted but ignored. This applies before either Arrival or legacy operations
176: can use the provider. Callers that need another credential source can continue
177: to supply a custom `CredentialProvider`; that interface does not itself create
178: an observer mapping or register public keys.
179: 
180: For an explicit persisted namespace, use `MappedCredentialProvider` with an
181: Arrival target. Prepare the binding separately from initializing the store:
182: 
183: ```python
184: from pathlib import Path
185: from sdk import MappedCredentialProvider, init_vertex, emit_fact
186: 
187: credentials = MappedCredentialProvider(
188:     Path("/absolute/path/to/private-custody"),
189:     namespace="personal",
190:     receipt_observer="alice",  # explicit local receipt-signing selection
191: )
192: binding = credentials.create_binding("alice", token="bootstrap-alice-1")
193: init_vertex(
194:     Path("observations.vertex"), store_type="arrival", observer="alice",
195:     credentials=credentials,
196: )
197: emit_fact(
198:     Path("observations.vertex"), "item", {"value": 1},
199:     observer="alice", credentials=credentials,
200: )
201: ```
202: 
203: Mapped selection uses the exact namespace and observer, independently of the
204: vertex filename or lineage. The engine checks author keys against verified
205: history through captured H, for use after H, and verifies each signature in its
206: own domain. Receipt signing uses the explicitly selected local observer and
207: the effective declaration's current receipt-key policy; the unsigned outer tick
208: still names the physical genesis custodian. Omitting `receipt_observer` permits
209: unsigned ticks before the signed-tick era, and refuses a required signed tick.
210: A missing author binding may remain unsigned where the existing policy allows;
211: a contradictory or unauthorized binding refuses before append.
212: 
213: Mapped initialization only loads a pre-created binding. For another observer,
214: prepare its binding and pass `key=binding.public_key` to `grant_observer` with
215: the existing author's credentials. Mapped `grant_observer(key=None)` refuses
216: before creating keys. Recovery of an existing Arrival initialization intent
217: signs nothing and does not require private custody to remain available.
218: Mapped providers are supported on Arrival targets; legacy writers explicitly
219: refuse them. The default `CustodyCredentialProvider` remains unchanged.
220: 
221: The runnable [two-observer mapped workload](tests/test_arrival_mapped_workload.py)
222: shows the complete public lifecycle: create Alice and Bob bindings under one
223: persisted provider root and exact namespace; initialize with Alice; grant Bob
224: using Bob's pre-created public key; then emit with both authors. For a locator
225: move that keeps the same physical file-backed store, initialize with an
226: **absolute** `location` and move only the `.vertex` file while retaining its
227: store clause, lineage, and role. Recreate `MappedCredentialProvider` with the
228: same provider root, namespace, and receipt observer before continuing work.
229: The absolute store location keeps the same store residence, while
230: mapped key selection comes only from the provider namespace and exact observer
231: label; neither the vertex filename nor its new directory chooses a key.
232: 
233: After relocation, `verify_target(relocated_vertex)` provides a `Full`
234: verification of the captured prefix's grammar, dense ordinals, lineage, and
235: record-hash chain. It does not establish projection agreement, signature
236: authorship, key trust, or the identity of an observer across lineages. The
237: mapped workload exercises public writes with captured authorization; the
238: [mapped signing tests](tests/test_arrival_mapped_credentials.py) separately
239: verify domain-specific signatures and key-history behavior.
240: 
241: `import_legacy(vertex_path, observer, token=...)` explicitly copies a verified
242: legacy key into managed storage without changing the original. It preserves
243: flat/nested ambiguity and alias refusals. After import, locator moves do not
244: change mapped selection. No read, preview, or signer load imports or creates a
245: binding. `bind_existing_ref(observer, key_ref, expected_public_key, token=...)`
246: permits deliberate reuse of a completed managed key in another namespace;
247: equal observer labels alone never select another namespace's key.
248: See [custody binding recovery](../custody/README.md#explicit-mapped-bindings)
249: for interruption handling and filesystem scope.
250: 
251: Supported Arrival execution reserves the vertex name from its loop names.
252: Every exact name collision refuses, including passive loops, reset/carry loops,
253: and count- or event-triggered boundaries. The runtime also adds an implicit
254: `cite` loop, so a vertex named `cite` cannot execute under this restriction.
255: Runtime capture checks the historized effective declaration and expanded
256: template loop names before hydration and planning; editing only the local cache
257: cannot bypass it. New and proposed declarations check their declared loops and
258: the implicit `cite` loop. The SDK initializer also refuses the name `item`,
259: because its scaffold declares an `item` loop, before creating custody keys.
260: External template sources are expanded and checked at runtime; declaration
261: editing does not load them solely to enforce this rule.
262: 
263: This is an Arrival execution compatibility restriction. Existing histories
264: remain available through evidence reads, declaration inspection and export.
265: A collision-free declaration can be appended to correct an ambiguous current
266: declaration; prior facts and ticks retain their original names and bytes.
267: This does not establish which role an already-recorded ambiguous tick had;
268: historical boundary continuity remains a separate limitation.
269: Legacy runtime behavior and the wire format retain their existing rules.
270: 
271: ---
272: 
273: ### 4. Arrival Source Execution
274: 
275: ```python
276: from sdk import run_sources
277: 
278: result = await run_sources("target.vertex", observer="alice", force=False)
279: for tier in result.tiers:
280:     print(tier.outcome, tier.commit, tier.fact_ids, tier.tick_ids)
281: ```
282: 
283: - **`await run_sources(target, *, observer, force=False, credentials=None, registry=None, collector_factory=None, dispatcher=None, evaluated_at=None) -> SourceRunResult`**:
284:   Captures a CURRENT Arrival Authority before collection, fixes cadence and dependency tiers, and commits each collected tier with one exact-head batch append plus explicit projection synchronization. The lifecycle observer is always explicit. Existing custody keys are loaded when present; the operation never creates keys.
285: 
286:   A source error after yielding observations returns `status="error"` after its error lifecycle fact becomes durable. Interrupted results retain prior commits and distinguish `known_uncommitted` collection from an append whose durability is `unknown`; collectors are not rerun. When `dispatcher` is omitted, captured tick run intents remain visible with `dispatch_status="not-requested"` and `attempted=False`. This operation supports explicit single-store Arrival Authority descriptors only and never falls through to legacy source execution.
287: 
288:   Coordinator failures during ID allocation, lifecycle construction, or otherwise successful collector cleanup return an incomplete result with terminal category `collection-failed`. `terminal.details.collection` contains the current tier's basis, `custody="not-attempted"`, completed siblings, and paired partial observations from failed or cancelled siblings. Incomplete attempts have no lifecycle fact; prior tiers keep their commits. These partial observations live in terminal evidence, not `known_uncommitted`, which describes a fully collected tier. Owned iterators and distinct iterable owners receive `aclose()` when available; caller cancellation propagates after sibling cleanup. Closing a collector does not undo its external effects or guarantee subprocess termination.
289: 
290: ### 5. Declaration, Scaffolding & Ceremonies
291: 
292: ```python
293: from sdk import (
294:     init_vertex,
295:     inspect_declaration,
296:     add_kind,
297:     edit_kind,
298:     remove_kind,
299:     grant_observer,
300:     revoke_observer,
301:     plan_kind_mutation,
302:     recover_ceremony,
303: )
304: from lang.ast import LoopDef, FoldDecl, FoldCollect
305: 
306: # Scaffold a new vertex declaration
307: init_res = init_vertex("target.vertex", name="my_app", store_type="sqlite")
308: 
309: # Deep structural inspection & syntax validation
310: info = inspect_declaration("target.vertex")
311: 
312: # Plan declaration update (dry-run diff)
313: plan = plan_kind_mutation("target.vertex", "add", "todo")
314: 
315: # Add new loop kind with default 'items collect 100'
316: result = add_kind("target.vertex", "todo", observer="admin")
317: 
318: # Edit existing kind definition
319: result = edit_kind(
320:     "target.vertex",
321:     "note",
322:     LoopDef(folds=(FoldDecl("items", FoldCollect(50)),)),
323:     observer="admin",
324: )
325: 
326: # Remove kind definition
327: result = remove_kind("target.vertex", "deprecated_kind", observer="admin")
328: 
329: # Manage observer admission grants
330: grant_observer("target.vertex", "alice", grants=["todo", "note"], observer="admin")
331: revoke_observer("target.vertex", "alice", observer="admin")
332: 
333: # Recover interrupted ceremony
334: recovery = recover_ceremony("target.vertex.intent")
335: ```
336: 
337: ---
338: 
339: ## Result Models
340: 
341: All models are frozen, immutable dataclasses providing `.as_dict()` conversion:
342: 
343: Arrival fact and tick items include receipt-order coordinates. Legacy items
344: retain their established serialized fields; use `read_path`, `basis`, and
345: `store` on the enclosing result to identify the source and read boundary.
346: 
347: | Model | Schema | Purpose |
348: | :--- | :--- | :--- |
349: | **`ArrivalTarget`** | `loops.sdk/arrival-target/v1` | Vertex path plus its serializable explicit store descriptor. |
350: | **`InitVertexResult`** | `loops.sdk/init-vertex/v1` | Outcome of vertex scaffolding operation. |
351: | **`DeclarationInspectionResult`** | `loops.sdk/declaration-inspection/v1` | Deep structural inspection of a `.vertex` file. |
352: | **`DeclarationPlanResult`** | `loops.sdk/declaration-plan/v1` | Dry-run preview of a proposed declaration update. |
353: | **`ReadSummary`** | `loops.sdk/read-summary/v2` | Basis-bearing inventory of facts, ticks, and kinds. |
354: | **`FactPageResult`** | `loops.sdk/facts-page/v2` | Basis-bearing bounded page with continuation metadata. |
355: | **`FactLookupResult`** | `loops.sdk/fact-lookup/v1` | Basis-bearing exact/prefix lookup; a miss retains its basis. |
356: | **`TickReadResult`** | `loops.sdk/tick-read/v1` | Basis-bearing chronological tick records. |
357: | **`FoldStateResult`** | `loops.sdk/fold-state/v2` | State folded from facts and declarations in one bounded snapshot. |
358: | **`SearchResult`** | `loops.sdk/search-result/v1` | Full-text search matches, snippets, and rankings. |
359: | **`TimelineResult`** | `loops.sdk/timeline-result/v1` | Interleaved chronological stream of facts and tick seals. |
360: | **`SyncResult`** | `loops.sdk/sync-result/v2` | Legacy index status or Arrival captured/target/before/after projection basis. |
361: | **`EmitReceipt`** | `loops.sdk/emit-receipt/v2` | Legacy receipt fields plus Arrival store, captured head, commit, witness, and projection outcome. |
362: | **`SourceRunResult`** | `loops.sdk/source-run/v1` | Captured cadence, collected bodies, tier commits, and interrupted projection/dispatch evidence. |
363: | **`EmitPreviewResult`** | `loops.sdk/emit-preview/v2` | Preflight admission/fold result with descriptor and captured-head provenance. |
364: | **`KindMutationResult`** | `loops.sdk/kind-mutation/v1` | Outcome of a declaration update ceremony and generation diffs. |
365: 
366: ---
367: 
368: ## Exception Taxonomy
369: 
370: ```mermaid
371: graph TD
372:     SdkError --> TargetError
373:     TargetError --> TargetNotFound
374:     TargetError --> TargetUnsupported
375:     TargetError --> TargetNotWritable
376:     SdkError --> AdmissionFailed
377:     SdkError --> EmissionFailed
378:     SdkError --> SdkValueError
379:     SdkValueError --> InvalidEmissionRequest
380:     EmissionFailed --> InvalidEmissionRequest
381:     EmissionFailed --> CommittedEmissionError
382:     SdkError --> CeremonyFailed
383: ```
384: 
385: - **`SdkError`**: Base class for all high-level SDK exceptions.
386: - **`SdkValueError`**: Base class for SDK parameter and validation errors (inherits from `ValueError`).
387: - **`TargetNotFound`**: Target path does not exist on disk.
388: - **`TargetUnsupported`**: Path exists but is not a recognized Loops artifact.
389: - **`TargetNotWritable`**: Target or derived index cannot be written to.
390: - **`AdmissionFailed`**: Declared admission policy (strict kinds or observer grants) refused the operation.
391: - **`EmissionFailed`**: Fact emission failed prior to committing.
392: - **`InvalidEmissionRequest`**: Invalid emission parameters (missing required observer or invalid shape).
393: - **`CommittedEmissionError`**: Fact was written to storage, but a post-commit task failed (carries `.fact_id`).
394: - **`CeremonyFailed`**: Declaration update AST generation or ceremony apply was refused.
395: 
396: Descriptor-first reads also surface engine contract refusals (for example, a
397: missing or behind projection or an invalid continuation) and declaration
398: resolution errors. These keep the adapter's evidence-bearing refusal intact.
399: 
400: Arrival `sync_target` and `sync_search_index` normalize maintenance failures as
401: `ProjectionOutcomeUnknown`. Their optional `details.evidence` object uses
402: `loops.sdk/evidence/v1`: it records the coordinator phase, resource effects,
403: available head/coverage coordinates, and up to two explicit causal exceptions.
404: Search failures before the build call report derived state as
405: `not-entered`/`not-attempted`; failures after entering build or projection
406: catch-up report `entered`/`unknown`. A nested refusal does not prove that an
407: adapter left derived state unchanged. Existing error outcomes, source types,
408: and operation-specific `details.phase` remain intact. Cause messages are capped
409: at 512 characters; record bodies and implicit exception context are excluded.
410: Declaration preparation refusals carry preparation evidence without claiming a
411: custody append. Cancellation and process-control exceptions propagate unchanged.
412: 
413: ## Restore an existing exact copy
414: 
415: ```python
416: from sdk import restore_forward
417: 
418: restored = restore_forward("source.vertex", "replica.vertex")
419: print(restored.before, restored.after)
420: ```
421: 
422: Both vertices require explicit Arrival descriptors. The receiver must already
423: be an exact prefix of the verified source. Restoration appends the complete
424: missing suffix atomically through at least the witnessed head, preserving its
425: declared role. `restored.commit` is an actual receipt, or `None` when the exact
426: selected head is already present. Projection catch-up remains explicit.
427: 
428: Ordinary opening still refuses rollback. `CommittedOutcomeUnknown` retains
429: before/target heads when a replication receipt was lost;
430: `CommittedIncomplete` retains the commit if later witnessing or comparison
431: failed. Empty receiver installation is a separate, pending import operation.

===== libs/sdk/tests/test_arrival_mapped_credentials.py =====
1: """Public SDK checks for explicitly mapped Arrival credentials.
2: 
3: These tests intentionally use the public custody provider and SDK operations.
4: They do not inspect the provider's private slot layout; the temporary root is
5: only a test-owned custody namespace.
6: """
7: 
8: from __future__ import annotations
9: 
10: import asyncio
11: from dataclasses import replace
12: from pathlib import Path
13: 
14: import pytest
15: from atoms import Fact, SourceError
16: from engine.credentials import CredentialPurpose, CredentialRequest, SigningDomain
17: 
18: from sdk import (
19:     MappedCredentialProvider,
20:     SdkError,
21:     SdkValueError,
22:     edit_declaration,
23:     emit_batch,
24:     emit_fact,
25:     grant_observer,
26:     init_vertex,
27:     preview_emission,
28:     run_sources,
29: )
30: 
31: 
32: @pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
33: def _isolated_process_roots(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
34:     """Keep custody, config, and legacy home state inside this test."""
35: 
36:     monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_STATE_HOME", str(tmp_path / "state"))
37:     monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", str(tmp_path / "config"))
38:     monkeypatch.setenv("LOOPS_HOME", str(tmp_path / "loops"))
39: 
40: 
41: def _provider(tmp_path: Path, *, namespace: str = "tenant-a") -> MappedCredentialProvider:
42:     return MappedCredentialProvider(
43:         tmp_path / "mapped-custody", namespace=namespace, receipt_observer="alice"
44:     )
45: 
46: 
47: def _create(provider: MappedCredentialProvider, observer: str, token: str):
48:     return provider.create_binding(observer, token=token)
49: 
50: 
51: def _file_state(root: Path) -> dict[Path, bytes]:
52:     return {
53:         path.relative_to(root): path.read_bytes()
54:         for path in root.rglob("*")
55:         if path.is_file()
56:     }
57: 
58: 
59: def _init_mapped(
60:     tmp_path: Path, provider: MappedCredentialProvider, *, observer: str = "alice"
61: ) -> tuple[Path, object]:
62:     target = tmp_path / "declared-name.vertex"
63:     _create(provider, observer, f"create-{observer}")
64:     result = init_vertex(
65:         target,
66:         name="declared-name",
67:         store_type="arrival",
68:         observer=observer,
69:         location=str(tmp_path / "records.arrival"),
70:         credentials=provider,
71:     )
72:     return target, result
73: 
74: 
75: def test_mapped_provider_separates_domains_and_keeps_key_ref_stable(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
76:     provider = _provider(tmp_path)
77:     created = _create(provider, "alice", "create-alice")
78: 
79:     fact = provider.resolve(
80:         CredentialRequest(
81:             provider.namespace,
82:             "alice",
83:             SigningDomain.FACT,
84:             CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP,
85:         )
86:     )
87:     arrival = provider.resolve(
88:         CredentialRequest(
89:             provider.namespace,
90:             "alice",
91:             SigningDomain.ARRIVAL,
92:             CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP,
93:         )
94:     )
95:     tick = provider.resolve(
96:         CredentialRequest(
97:             provider.namespace,
98:             "alice",
99:             SigningDomain.TICK,
100:             CredentialPurpose.RECEIPT,
101:         )
102:     )
103: 
104:     assert fact is not None and arrival is not None and tick is not None
105:     assert fact.evidence.key_ref == created.key_ref
106:     assert arrival.evidence.key_ref == created.key_ref
107:     assert tick.evidence.key_ref == created.key_ref
108:     assert fact.evidence.public_key == created.public_key
109:     assert fact.evidence.request.domain is SigningDomain.FACT
110:     assert tick.evidence.request.observer == "alice"
111:     signature = fact.sign_digest("mapped-digest")
112:     assert provider.verify(
113:         SigningDomain.FACT, created.public_key, signature, "mapped-digest"
114:     )
115:     assert not provider.verify(
116:         SigningDomain.ARRIVAL, created.public_key, signature, "mapped-digest"
117:     )
118:     tick_signature = tick.sign_digest("tick-digest")
119:     assert provider.verify(
120:         SigningDomain.TICK, created.public_key, tick_signature, "tick-digest"
121:     )
122:     assert not provider.verify(
123:         SigningDomain.FACT, created.public_key, tick_signature, "tick-digest"
124:     )
125: 
126:

128:     provider = _provider(tmp_path)
129:     target, result = _init_mapped(tmp_path, provider)
130:     assert result.lineage

133:         CredentialRequest(
134:             provider.namespace,
135:             "alice",

139:     )

141:     assert f'key "{binding.evidence.public_key}"' in target.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
142:

144:     relocated.parent.mkdir()
145:     target.rename(relocated)
146:

148:         relocated,
149:         "item",
150:         {"value": 7},

158: def test_mapped_source_tiers_use_bound_author_for_each_append(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
159:     class CountingProvider(MappedCredentialProvider):
160:         def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
161:             super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
162:             self.requests = []
163: 
164:         def resolve(self, request):
165:             self.requests.append(request)
166:             return super().resolve(request)
167: 
168:     provider = CountingProvider(
169:         tmp_path / "mapped-custody", namespace="tenant-a", receipt_observer="alice"
170:     )
171:     target, _ = _init_mapped(tmp_path, provider)
172:     for kind in ("item", "other"):
173:         cadence = 'on "_sync.item"\n' if kind == "other" else ""
174:         (tmp_path / f"{kind}.loop").write_text(
175:             f'kind "{kind}"\nobserver "alice"\nsource "collect-{kind}"\n{cadence}',
176:             encoding="utf-8",
177:         )
178:     original = target.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
179:     prefix = original.split("loops {", 1)[0]
180:     proposal = prefix + (
181:         'sources {\n'
182:         '  path "./item.loop"\n'
183:         '  path "./other.loop"\n'
184:         '}\n\n'
185:         'loops {\n'
186:         '  item {\n    fold { items "collect" 100 }\n  }\n'
187:         '  other {\n    fold { items "collect" 100 }\n  }\n'
188:         '}\n'
189:     )
190:     edited = edit_declaration(target, proposal, observer="alice", credentials=provider)
191:     assert edited.commit is not None
192:     requests_before_sources = len(provider.requests)
193: 
194:     async def collect(source):
195:         yield Fact(source.kind, 5.0, {"source": source.kind}, observer="alice")
196: 
197:     result = asyncio.run(
198:         run_sources(
199:             target,
200:             observer="alice",
201:             force=True,
202:             credentials=provider,
203:             collector_factory=collect,
204:             evaluated_at=4.0,
205:         )
206:     )
207:     assert result.status == "ok"
208:     assert len(result.tiers) == 2
209:     assert all(tier.commit is not None for tier in result.tiers)
210:     assert len(provider.requests) - requests_before_sources >= 2
211: 
212: 
213: def test_mapped_init_rejects_relabelled_binding_evidence_before_intent(
214:     tmp_path: Path,
215: ) -> None:
216:     class RelabelledProvider(MappedCredentialProvider):
217:         def resolve(self, request):
218:             resolved = super().resolve(request)
219:             if resolved is None:
220:                 return None
221:             evidence = replace(
222:                 resolved.evidence,
223:                 request=replace(resolved.evidence.request, observer="mallory"),
224:             )
225:             return replace(resolved, evidence=evidence)
226: 
227:     provider = RelabelledProvider(
228:         tmp_path / "mapped-custody", namespace="tenant-a", receipt_observer="alice"
229:     )
230:     _create(provider, "alice", "create-alice")
231:     before = _file_state(tmp_path)
232: 
233:     with pytest.raises(SdkError):
234:         init_vertex(
235:             tmp_path / "relabelled.vertex",
236:             name="relabelled",
237:             store_type="arrival",
238:             observer="alice",
239:             location=str(tmp_path / "records.arrival"),
240:             credentials=provider,
241:         )
242: 
243:     assert _file_state(tmp_path) == before
244:     assert not (tmp_path / "relabelled.vertex").exists()
245: 
246: 
247: def test_mapped_two_author_batch_preserves_inner_and_outer_domains(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
248:     provider = _provider(tmp_path)
249:     target, initialized = _init_mapped(tmp_path, provider)
250:     bob = _create(provider, "bob", "create-bob")
251:     grant_observer(
252:         target,
253:         "bob",
254:         observer="alice",
255:         credentials=provider,
256:         key=bob.public_key,
257:     )
258: 
259:     from engine.admission import fact_commitment_hash
260:     from engine.arrival import ArrivalLog, content_commitment
261: 
262:     result = emit_batch(
263:         target,
264:         [
265:             {"kind": "item", "payload": {"value": 1}, "observer": "alice", "ts": 3.0},
266:             {"kind": "item", "payload": {"value": 2}, "observer": "alice", "ts": 4.0},
267:             {"kind": "item", "payload": {"value": 3}, "observer": "bob", "ts": 5.0},
268:             {"kind": "item", "payload": {"value": 4}, "observer": "bob", "ts": 6.0},
269:         ],
270:         credentials=provider,
271:     )
272:     assert result.commit is not None
273:     assert initialized.store is not None
274:     records = list(ArrivalLog(initialized.store.location).walk())
275:     batches = [record for record in records if record["k"] == "batch"]
276:     assert [batch["observer"] for batch in batches[-2:]] == ["alice", "bob"]
277:     for batch in batches[-2:]:
278:         outer_observer = str(batch["observer"])
279:         outer = provider.resolve(
280:             CredentialRequest(
281:                 provider.namespace,
282:                 outer_observer,
283:                 SigningDomain.ARRIVAL,
284:                 CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP,
285:             )
286:         )
287:         assert outer is not None
288:         outer_digest = content_commitment(
289:             batch["k"], batch["at"], batch["observer"], batch["origin"], batch["body"]
290:         )
291:         assert provider.verify(
292:             SigningDomain.ARRIVAL,
293:             outer.evidence.public_key,
294:             batch["sig"],
295:             outer_digest,
296:         )
297: 
298:         for row in batch["body"]["rows"]:
299:             inner = provider.resolve(
300:                 CredentialRequest(
301:                     provider.namespace,
302:                     row["observer"],
303:                     SigningDomain.FACT,
304:                     CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP,
305:                 )
306:             )
307:             assert inner is not None
308:             inner_digest = fact_commitment_hash(
309:                 row["kind"], row["ts"], row["observer"], row["origin"], row["payload"]
310:             )
311:             assert provider.verify(
312:                 SigningDomain.FACT,
313:                 inner.evidence.public_key,
314:                 row["signature"],
315:                 inner_digest,
316:             )

===== libs/sdk/src/sdk/verify.py =====
1: """Custody-prefix verification for explicit Arrival descriptors."""
2: 
3: from __future__ import annotations
4: 
5: from contextlib import suppress
6: from pathlib import Path
7: 
8: from engine.arrival_contract import Full, Head, HeadMismatch, NotAuthority, Watermark
9: from engine.arrival_head_seam import AttestedLedger, Compared, Indeterminate, PreGenesis
10: from engine.arrival_registry import BackendRegistry
11: 
12: from .errors import normalize_exception
13: from .target import _arrival_descriptor
14: from .types import StoreDescriptorInfo, VerifyResult
15: 
16: __all__ = ["verify_target"]
17: 
18: 
19: def _close_quietly(handle: object) -> None:
20:     close = getattr(handle, "close", None)
21:     if callable(close):
22:         with suppress(Exception):
23:             close()
24: 
25: 
26: def _verified_prefix(ledger: AttestedLedger, captured: Head, target: Head) -> Head:
27:     """Verify one target already bounded by the comparison established at open."""
28:     if target.lineage != captured.lineage:
29:         raise NotAuthority(
30:             f"verification target names lineage {target.lineage}, but the "
31:             f"attested ledger presents {captured.lineage}"
32:         )
33:     if target.ordinal > captured.ordinal:
34:         raise HeadMismatch(
35:             f"verification target ordinal {target.ordinal} exceeds the attested "
36:             f"captured ordinal {captured.ordinal}"
37:         )
38:     resolved = ledger.head_at(Watermark(target.lineage, target.ordinal))
39:     if resolved != target:
40:         raise HeadMismatch("verification target no longer resolves to its recorded hash")
41:     verified = ledger.verify(Full(through=resolved))
42:     if verified != resolved:
43:         raise HeadMismatch("full verification returned a different head")
44:     return verified
45: 
46: 
47: def verify_target(
48:     target: Path | str,
49:     *,
50:     through: Head | None = None,
51:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
52: ) -> VerifyResult:
53:     """Fully verify one attested Arrival prefix without reading its projection.
54: 
55:     ``Full`` establishes grammar, dense ordinals, one lineage, and the record
56:     hash chain through the returned head. It does not verify record signatures,
57:     authorship, key trust, or projection agreement.
58:     """
59:     ledger: object | None = None
60:     query: object | None = None
61:     try:
62:         resolved = _arrival_descriptor(target)
63:         if resolved is None:
64:             from .types import TargetUnsupported
65: 
66:             raise TargetUnsupported("verify_target requires an explicit Arrival descriptor")
67:         _path, _ast, descriptor = resolved
68:         ledger, query = (registry or BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()).open(descriptor)
69:         if not isinstance(ledger, AttestedLedger):
70:             raise TypeError("BackendRegistry.open must return an AttestedLedger")
71:         comparison = ledger.opened.comparison
72:         if isinstance(comparison, PreGenesis):
73:             if comparison.ledger_refusal is not None:
74:                 raise comparison.ledger_refusal
75:             raise HeadMismatch("a pre-genesis ledger has no full verification head")
76:         if isinstance(comparison, Indeterminate):
77:             raise comparison.refusal
78:         if not isinstance(comparison, Compared):
79:             raise TypeError(f"unsupported head comparison {comparison!r}")
80:         captured = comparison.presented
81:         verified = _verified_prefix(ledger, captured, captured if through is None else through)
82:         return VerifyResult(
83:             store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
84:             captured_head=captured,
85:             verified_through=verified,
86:         )
87:     except BaseException as exc:
88:         normalized = normalize_exception(exc)
89:         if normalized is exc:
90:             raise
91:         raise normalized from exc
92:     finally:
93:         _close_quietly(query)
94:         _close_quietly(ledger)

===== libs/sdk/src/sdk/types.py =====
1: """SDK types, models, and exceptions for the Loops composition library."""
2: 
3: from __future__ import annotations
4: 
5: import json
6: import math
7: from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
8: from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field, fields
9: from datetime import UTC, datetime
10: from typing import Any
11: 
12: from engine.arrival_contract import Commit, Continuation, Head, ReadBasis, StoreDescriptor
13: 
14: 
15: def _as_serializable(value: Any) -> Any:
16:     """Turn nested aggregate evidence values into JSON-shaped SDK output."""
17:     if hasattr(value, "__dataclass_fields__"):
18:         return _as_serializable(asdict(value))
19:     if isinstance(value, Mapping):
20:         return {str(key): _as_serializable(item) for key, item in value.items()}
21:     if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
22:         return [_as_serializable(item) for item in value]
23:     return value
24:

26: # Exceptions
27: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

29: 
30: class SdkError(Exception):
31:     """Base exception for all high-level SDK operations."""
32: 
33: 
34: class TargetError(SdkError):
35:     """Target resolution or probe error."""
36: 
37: 
38: class TargetNotFound(TargetError):
39:     """The requested target path does not exist."""
40: 
41: 
42: class TargetUnsupported(TargetError):
43:     """The target exists but is not a recognized loops artifact."""
44: 
45: 
46: class TargetNotWritable(TargetError):
47:     """The target or its store is not writable for the requested operation."""
48: 
49: 
50: class AdmissionFailed(SdkError):
51:     """Declared admission policy rejected the operation."""
52: 
53:     def __init__(
54:         self,
55:         message: str,
56:         *,
57:         observer: str | None = None,
58:         kind: str | None = None,
59:         vertex: str | None = None,
60:     ) -> None:
61:         super().__init__(message)
62:         self.observer = observer
63:         self.kind = kind
64:         self.vertex = vertex
65: 
66: 
67: class EmissionFailed(SdkError):
68:     """Fact emission failed before committing to the store."""
69: 
70: 
71: class SdkValueError(SdkError, ValueError):
72:     """Invalid input parameter or missing required argument for an SDK operation."""
73: 
74: 
75: class InvalidEmissionRequest(SdkValueError, EmissionFailed):
76:     """Invalid parameters supplied for fact emission (e.g. missing observer)."""
77: 
78: 
79: class LegacyBatchPartialFailure(EmissionFailed):
80:     """A sequential legacy batch failed after storing an observable prefix."""
81: 
82:     outcome = "legacy-partial"
83: 
84:     def __init__(
85:         self,
86:         message: str,
87:         *,
88:         failed_index: int,
89:         items: list[EmitReceipt],
90:         cause: BaseException,
91:         committed_fact_id: str | None = None,
92:     ) -> None:
93:         super().__init__(message)
94:         self.failed_index = failed_index
95:         self.items = tuple(items)
96:         self.cause = cause
97:         self.committed_fact_id = committed_fact_id
98: 
99:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
100:         return {
101:             "schema": "loops.sdk/error/v1",
102:             "type": type(self).__name__,
103:             "message": str(self),
104:             "outcome": self.outcome,
105:             "failed_index": self.failed_index,
106:             "items": [item.as_dict() for item in self.items],
107:             "committed_fact_id": self.committed_fact_id,
108:             "cause_type": type(self.cause).__name__,
109:         }

112: class CommittedEmissionError(EmissionFailed):
113:     """The fact was successfully committed to the canonical store, but a
114:     compound post-commit operation (e.g. tick computation or index sync) failed.
115: 
116:     The fact ID is preserved so callers know not to blindly retry.
117:     """
118: 
119:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, fact_id: str) -> None:
120:         super().__init__(message)
121:         self.fact_id = fact_id

202: @dataclass(frozen=True)
203: class FactPageResult:
204:     """Bounded, cursor-bearing page of facts."""
205: 
206:     schema: str = "loops.sdk/facts-page/v2"
207:     read_path: str = "legacy"
208:     basis: ReadBasis | None = None
209:     store: StoreDescriptorInfo | None = None
210:     items: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
211:     next_cursor: str | Continuation | None = None
212:     prev_cursor: str | Continuation | None = None
213:     truncated: bool = False
214:     order: str = "newest"
215: 
216:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
217:         result = asdict(self)
218:         # Engine Continuation is deliberately an in-memory value, not a wire
219:         # token. Never make an unprotected, non-round-trippable token appear in
220:         # the SDK's process-boundary serialization.
221:         result["has_continuation"] = isinstance(self.next_cursor, Continuation)
222:         if isinstance(self.next_cursor, Continuation):
223:             result["next_cursor"] = None
224:         if isinstance(self.prev_cursor, Continuation):
225:             result["prev_cursor"] = None
226:         return result

282: 
283: def _commit_dict(commit: Commit | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
284:     """Serialize commit identity without copying backend record bodies."""
285:     if commit is None:
286:         return None
287:     return {
288:         "before": asdict(commit.before),
289:         "after": asdict(commit.after),
290:         "record_count": len(commit.records),
291:         "durability": {
292:             "profile": commit.durability.profile.value,
293:             "mechanism": commit.durability.mechanism,
294:         },
295:     }
296: 
297: 
298: def _payload_issues(value: Any, path: str = "$") -> list[dict[str, str]]:
299:     """Describe values json cannot encode without replacing them with repr/str."""
300:     if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, bool)):
301:         return []
302:     if isinstance(value, float):
303:         return [] if math.isfinite(value) else [
304:             {"path": path, "type": "float", "issue": "non-finite"}
305:         ]
306:     if isinstance(value, Mapping):
307:         issues: list[dict[str, str]] = []
308:         for key, item in value.items():
309:             if not isinstance(key, str):
310:                 issues.append({"path": path, "type": type(key).__name__})
311:                 continue
312:             issues.extend(_payload_issues(item, f"{path}.{key}"))
313:         return issues
314:     if isinstance(value, Sequence) and not isinstance(value, (str, bytes, bytearray)):
315:         issues = []
316:         for index, item in enumerate(value):
317:             issues.extend(_payload_issues(item, f"{path}[{index}]"))
318:         return issues
319:     return [{"path": path, "type": type(value).__name__}]
320: 
321: 
322: def _payload_dict(value: Any) -> tuple[Any | None, bool, list[dict[str, str]]]:
323:     """Return exact JSON-shaped payload plus typed shape/encoding evidence."""
324:     issues = []
325:     if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
326:         issues.append(
327:             {"path": "$", "type": type(value).__name__, "issue": "expected-object"}
328:         )
329:     try:
330:         encoded = json.dumps(
331:             value,
332:             ensure_ascii=False,
333:             separators=(",", ":"),
334:             allow_nan=False,
335:         )
336:         decoded = json.loads(encoded)
337:     except (TypeError, ValueError):
338:         issues.extend(_payload_issues(value))
339:         return None, False, issues
340:     return decoded, True, issues
341: 
342: 
343: def _wire_float(value: Any, field_name: str) -> tuple[float | None, list[dict[str, str]]]:
344:     if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and math.isfinite(float(value)):
345:         return float(value), []
346:     return None, [
347:         {"field": field_name, "type": type(value).__name__, "issue": "non-finite"}
348:     ]
349: 
350: 
351: @dataclass(frozen=True)
352: class EmitReceipt:
353:     """Persisted receipt for an emitted fact or dry-run simulation."""
354: 
355:     schema: str = "loops.sdk/emit-receipt/v2"
356:     write_path: str = "legacy"
357:     store: StoreDescriptorInfo | None = None
358:     id: str = ""
359:     stored: bool = True
360:     signed: bool | None = None
361:     observer: str = ""
362:     tick_mark: str | None = None
363:     tick_id: str | None = None
364:     state_change: bool | None = False
365:     affected_sections: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
366:     delta_count: int | None = 0
367:     predicted_state_change: bool = False
368:     captured_head: Head | None = None
369:     commit: Commit | None = None
370:     witnessed: bool | None = None
371:     projection: str | None = None
372: 
373:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
374:         # Do not call asdict on the full Commit: third-party backend record
375:         # mappings need not support deepcopy, and record bodies are not part
376:         # of this process receipt's identity contract.
377:         result = {item.name: getattr(self, item.name) for item in fields(self)}
378:         result["store"] = None if self.store is None else asdict(self.store)
379:         result["affected_sections"] = list(self.affected_sections)
380:         result["captured_head"] = None if self.captured_head is None else asdict(self.captured_head)
381:         result["commit"] = _commit_dict(self.commit)
382:         return result
383: 
384: 
385: @dataclass(frozen=True)
386: class BatchEmitResult:
387:     """Receipts and shared custody evidence for one SDK batch request."""
388: 
389:     schema: str = "loops.sdk/batch-emit/v2"
390:     write_path: str = "legacy"
391:     store: StoreDescriptorInfo | None = None
392:     items: list[EmitReceipt] = field(default_factory=list)
393:     atomic: bool = False
394:     atomicity: str = "legacy-sequential"
395:     captured_head: Head | None = None
396:     commit: Commit | None = None
397:     witnessed: bool | None = None
398:     projection: str | None = None
399: 
400:     def __iter__(self):
401:         return iter(self.items)
402: 
403:     def __len__(self) -> int:
404:         return len(self.items)
405: 
406:     def __getitem__(self, index: int) -> EmitReceipt:
407:         return self.items[index]
408: 
409:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
410:         return {
411:             "schema": self.schema,
412:             "write_path": self.write_path,
413:             "store": None if self.store is None else asdict(self.store),
414:             "items": [item.as_dict() for item in self.items],
415:             "item_count": len(self.items),
416:             "stored_count": sum(item.stored for item in self.items),
417:             "atomic": self.atomic,
418:             "atomicity": self.atomicity,
419:             "captured_head": (None if self.captured_head is None else asdict(self.captured_head)),
420:             "commit": _commit_dict(self.commit),
421:             "witnessed": self.witnessed,
422:             "projection": self.projection,
423:         }

426: @dataclass(frozen=True)
427: class SourceFactResult:
428:     """One exact collected fact identity and body retained for reconciliation."""
429: 
430:     id: str = ""
431:     kind: str = ""
432:     ts: float = 0.0
433:     observer: str = ""
434:     origin: str = ""
435:     payload: Any = field(default_factory=dict)
436:     payload_snapshot: bool = True
437: 
438:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
439:         payload, serializable, payload_issues = _payload_dict(self.payload)
440:         ts, envelope_issues = _wire_float(self.ts, "ts")
441:         result: dict[str, Any] = {
442:             "id": self.id,
443:             "kind": self.kind,
444:             "ts": ts,
445:             "observer": self.observer,
446:             "origin": self.origin,
447:             "payload": payload,
448:             "payload_type": type(self.payload).__name__,
449:             "payload_serializable": serializable,
450:             "payload_valid_object": isinstance(self.payload, Mapping),
451:             "payload_snapshot": self.payload_snapshot,
452:         }
453:         if payload_issues:
454:             result["payload_issues"] = payload_issues
455:         if envelope_issues:
456:             result["envelope_issues"] = envelope_issues
457:         return result
458:

460: @dataclass(frozen=True)
461: class SourceCadenceResult:
462:     """Serializable evidence for one source qualification decision."""
463: 
464:     source_index: int = 0
465:     kind: str = ""
466:     mode: str = ""
467:     evaluated_at: float = 0.0
468:     latest_success_id: str | None = None
469:     latest_success_ts: float | None = None
470:     trigger_fact_ids: tuple[str, ...] = ()
471:     interval_seconds: float | None = None
472:     forced: bool = False
473:     qualified: bool = False
474: 
475:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
476:         evaluated_at, evaluated_issues = _wire_float(
477:             self.evaluated_at, "evaluated_at"
478:         )
479:         latest, latest_issues = (
480:             (None, [])
481:             if self.latest_success_ts is None
482:             else _wire_float(self.latest_success_ts, "latest_success_ts")
483:         )
484:         interval, interval_issues = (
485:             (None, [])
486:             if self.interval_seconds is None
487:             else _wire_float(self.interval_seconds, "interval_seconds")
488:         )
489:         return {
490:             "source_index": self.source_index,
491:             "kind": self.kind,
492:             "mode": self.mode,
493:             "evaluated_at": evaluated_at,
494:             "latest_success_id": self.latest_success_id,
495:             "latest_success_ts": latest,
496:             "trigger_fact_ids": list(self.trigger_fact_ids),
497:             "interval_seconds": interval,
498:             "forced": self.forced,
499:             "qualified": self.qualified,
500:             "value_issues": [
501:                 *evaluated_issues,
502:                 *latest_issues,
503:                 *interval_issues,
504:             ],
505:         }

546: @dataclass(frozen=True)
547: class SourceDispatchResult:
548:     """A frozen tick run intent and whether this invocation attempted it."""
549: 
550:     tick_id: str = ""
551:     tick_name: str = ""
552:     command: str = ""
553:     attempted: bool = False
554:     succeeded: bool | None = None
555:     ts: float = 0.0
556:     since: float | None = None
557:     origin: str = ""
558:     payload_text: str = ""
559:     payload: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
560:     fact_cursor: str | None = None
561:     window_start: str | None = None
562:     window_hash: str | None = None
563:     vertex_path: str | None = None
564: 
565:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
566:         payload, serializable, issues = _payload_dict(self.payload)
567:         ts, ts_issues = _wire_float(self.ts, "ts")
568:         since, since_issues = (
569:             (None, [])
570:             if self.since is None
571:             else _wire_float(self.since, "since")
572:         )
573:         result: dict[str, Any] = {
574:             "tick_id": self.tick_id,
575:             "tick_name": self.tick_name,
576:             "command": self.command,
577:             "attempted": self.attempted,
578:             "succeeded": self.succeeded,
579:             "ts": ts,
580:             "since": since,
581:             "origin": self.origin,
582:             "payload_text": self.payload_text,
583:             "payload": payload,
584:             "payload_serializable": serializable,
585:             "fact_cursor": self.fact_cursor,
586:             "window_start": self.window_start,
587:             "window_hash": self.window_hash,
588:             "vertex_path": self.vertex_path,
589:         }
590:         if issues:
591:             result["payload_issues"] = issues
592:         if ts_issues or since_issues:
593:             result["envelope_issues"] = [*ts_issues, *since_issues]
594:         return result

938:     """Outcome of legacy scaffolding or registry-backed Arrival bootstrap.
939: 
940:     Arrival initialization fills ``lineage``, ``head`` and ``phase``;
941:     aggregate and legacy scaffolding leaves those optional fields empty.
942:     """
943: 
944:     schema: str = "loops.sdk/init-vertex/v2"
945:     read_path: str = "legacy"
946:     store: StoreDescriptorInfo | None = None
947:     target_path: str = ""
948:     name: str = ""
949:     store_path: str | None = None
950:     store_type: str = "sqlite"
951:     is_root: bool = False
952:     file_written: bool = True
953:     lineage: str | None = None
954:     head: dict[str, Any] | None = None
955:     phase: str | None = None
956: 
957:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
958:         return asdict(self)
959: 
960: 
961: @dataclass(frozen=True)
962: class DeclarationInspectionResult:
963:     """Root declaration evidence without aggregate-member capture.
964: 
965:     An Arrival result has one root ``basis`` only; unexpanded combine/discover
966:     fields describe that captured effective root and never assert member
967:     custody. ``read_path="local-frozen"`` has no adopted store or basis and
968:     reports the one parsed storeless aggregate definition instead.
969:     """
970: 
971:     schema: str = "loops.sdk/declaration-inspection/v2"
972:     read_path: str = "legacy"
973:     store: StoreDescriptorInfo | None = None
974:     basis: ReadBasis | None = None
975:     target_path: str = ""
976:     name: str = ""
977:     status: str = ""
978:     local_status: str | None = None
979:     effective_status: str | None = None
980:     local_fingerprint: str | None = None
981:     effective_fingerprint: str | None = None
982:     store_mode: str | None = None
983:     store_path: str | None = None
984:     declared_kinds: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
985:     declared_observers: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
986:     cadence_ticks: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
987:     strict: bool = False
988:     is_aggregate: bool = False
989:     syntax_valid: bool = True
990:     errors: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
991:     local_combine: list[dict[str, str | None]] | None = None
992:     local_discover: str | None = None
993:     effective_combine: list[dict[str, str | None]] | None = None
994:     effective_discover: str | None = None
995: 
996:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
997:         return asdict(self)
998: 
999: 
1000: @dataclass(frozen=True)
1001: class DeclarationPlanResult:
1002:     """Dry-run preview of a proposed declaration update ceremony."""
1003: 
1004:     schema: str = "loops.sdk/declaration-plan/v1"
1005:     applicable: bool = True
1006:     reason: str = ""
1007:     mode: str = ""
1008:     vertex_path: str = ""
1009:     generation_before: dict[str, Any] | None = None
1010:     changes: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
1011: 
1012:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
1013:         return asdict(self)

===== libs/sdk/src/sdk/emit.py =====
1: """Headless fact and batch emission operations over Loops vertices."""
