76: def _arrival_semantic_preview(
77:     vertex_path: Path,
78:     proposed_text: str,
79:     *,
80:     registry: Any | None,
81: ) -> DeclarationPlanResult:
82:     """Preview a splice only after confirming the cache matches CURRENT."""
83:     from engine.arrival_boundary_continuity import (
84:         BoundaryContinuityConflict,
85:         analyze_boundary_continuity,
86:         collect_verified_parameter_rows,
87:     )
88:     from engine.arrival_contract import TickRequest
89:     from engine.arrival_declarations import DeclarationPreparationRefused
90:     from engine.declaration import validate_arrival_runtime_identity
91:     from lang import diff_documents, parse_vertex, validate_vertex, vertex_to_documents
92: 
93:     from .declare import DeclarationPreviewResult
94:     from .errors import normalize_exception
95:     from .read import _arrival_declaration, _open_arrival_read
96: 
97:     resolved = _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path)
98:     if resolved is None:
99:         raise TargetUnsupported(f"not an explicit Arrival target: {vertex_path}")
100:     _path, locator_ast, descriptor = resolved
101:     active = registry or BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
102:     try:
103:         local_ast = parse_vertex(vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), path=vertex_path)
104:         with _open_arrival_read(resolved, registry=active) as (_locator, _descriptor, opened):
105:             effective_ast, _status, _facts, _lineage = _arrival_declaration(
106:                 locator_ast, vertex_path, opened
107:             )
108:             local_docs = [doc.as_json() for doc in vertex_to_documents(local_ast)]
109:             effective_docs = [doc.as_json() for doc in vertex_to_documents(effective_ast)]
110:             if local_docs != effective_docs:
111:                 return DeclarationPreviewResult(
112:                     applicable=False,
113:                     reason="declaration cache differs from CURRENT Arrival history",
114:                     mode="arrival-semantic-preview",
115:                     vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
116:                     generation_before={"captured_head": opened.basis.captured_head},
117:                     changes=[],
118:                     store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
119:                     basis=asdict(opened.basis),
120:                     captured_head={
121:                         "lineage": opened.basis.captured_head.lineage,
122:                         "ordinal": opened.basis.captured_head.ordinal,
123:                         "record_hash": opened.basis.captured_head.record_hash,
124:                     },
125:                     lineage=opened.basis.lineage,
126:                 )
127:             proposed_ast = parse_vertex(proposed_text, path=vertex_path)
128:             validate_vertex(proposed_ast)
129:             validate_arrival_runtime_identity(
130:                 proposed_ast.name, proposed_ast.loops, refusal=DeclarationPreparationRefused
131:             )
132:             proposed_docs = [
133:                 doc.as_json() for doc in vertex_to_documents(proposed_ast)
134:             ]
135:             changes = diff_documents(local_docs, proposed_docs)
136:             try:
137:                 verified_params = collect_verified_parameter_rows(
138:                     opened.snapshot.declaration_anchor,
139:                     _facts,
140:                     target_documents=proposed_docs,
141:                     base_dir=vertex_path.parent,
142:                 )
143:                 analyze_boundary_continuity(
144:                     opened.snapshot.declaration_anchor,
145:                     _facts,
146:                     opened.snapshot.ticks(TickRequest(since=float("-inf"))),
147:                     target_documents=proposed_docs,
148:                     verified_params=verified_params,
149:                 )
150:             except BoundaryContinuityConflict as conflict:
151:                 refusal = DeclarationPreparationRefused(
152:                     f"boundary continuity refuses declaration preview: {conflict}",
153:                     coordinator_phase="prepare",
154:                     effects={
155:                         "custody": {
156:                             "attempt": "not-entered",
157:                             "state": "not-attempted",
158:                         }
159:                     },
160:                     captured_head=opened.basis.captured_head,
161:                 )
162:                 raise refusal from conflict
163:             return DeclarationPreviewResult(
164:                 applicable=True,
165:                 reason="semantic preview only; signed execution requires observer credentials",
166:                 mode="arrival-semantic-preview",
167:                 vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
168:                 generation_before={"captured_head": opened.basis.captured_head},
169:                 changes=[c.as_dict() if hasattr(c, "as_dict") else str(c) for c in changes],
170:                 store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
171:                 basis=asdict(opened.basis),
172:                 captured_head={
173:                     "lineage": opened.basis.captured_head.lineage,
174:                     "ordinal": opened.basis.captured_head.ordinal,
175:                     "record_hash": opened.basis.captured_head.record_hash,
176:                 },
177:                 lineage=opened.basis.lineage,
178:             )
179:     except Exception as exc:
180:         normalized = normalize_exception(exc)
181:         if normalized is exc:
182:             raise
183:         raise normalized from exc
184: 
185: 
186: def plan_kind_mutation(
187:     target: Path | str,
188:     op: str,
189:     kind_name: str,
190:     definition: LoopDef | None = None,
191:     *,
192:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
193: ) -> DeclarationPlanResult:
194:     """Dry-run preview of a proposed kind declaration mutation.
195: 
196:     Parameters:
197:         target: Path to the .vertex file.
198:         op: Operation type ('add', 'edit', or 'remove').
199:         kind_name: The name of the kind to mutate.
200:         definition: LoopDef AST node (for add/edit).
201: 
202:     Returns:
203:         DeclarationPlanResult with proposed changes and applicability status.
204:     """
205:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
206:     if _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path) is not None:
207:         from .declare import DeclarationPreviewResult
208: 
209:         loop_def = definition or _default_loop_def()
210:         current_text = vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
211:         try:
212:             if op == "add":
213:                 new_text = add_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name, loop_def)
214:             elif op == "edit":
215:                 new_text = edit_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name, loop_def)
216:             elif op == "remove":
217:                 new_text = remove_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name)
218:             else:
219:                 raise SdkValueError(
220:                     f"unsupported mutation op '{op}', expected 'add', 'edit', or 'remove'"
221:                 )
222:         except SdkValueError:
223:             raise
224:         except ValueError as exc:
225:             return DeclarationPreviewResult(
226:                 applicable=False, reason=str(exc), mode="refused",
227:                 vertex_path=str(vertex_path), generation_before=None, changes=[],
228:             )
229:         return _arrival_semantic_preview(vertex_path, new_text, registry=registry)
230: 
231:     info = resolve_target(target)
232:     if info.target_type != "vertex":
233:         raise TargetUnsupported(
234:             f"plan_kind_mutation requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}"
235:         )
236: 
237:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
238:     current_text = vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
239:     loop_def = definition or _default_loop_def()
240: 
241:     try:
242:         if op == "add":
243:             new_text = add_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name, loop_def)
244:         elif op == "edit":
245:             new_text = edit_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name, loop_def)
246:         elif op == "remove":
247:             new_text = remove_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name)
248:         else:
249:             raise SdkValueError(
250:                 f"unsupported mutation op '{op}', expected 'add', 'edit', or 'remove'"
251:             )
252:     except SdkValueError:
253:         raise
254:     except ValueError as exc:
255:         # lang refused the mutation (duplicate add, missing edit/remove target, ...).
256:         # A plan is a preview of applicability, so a describable refusal is a
257:         # non-applicable plan, not an exception — the apply paths raise CeremonyFailed.
258:         return DeclarationPlanResult(
259:             applicable=False,
260:             reason=str(exc),
261:             mode="refused",
262:             vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
263:             generation_before=None,
264:             changes=[],
265:         )
266: 
267:     preview = plan_declaration_update(vertex_path, proposed_text=new_text)
268:     return DeclarationPlanResult(
269:         applicable=preview.applicable,
270:         reason=preview.reason,
271:         mode=preview.mode,
272:         vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
273:         generation_before=preview.generation,
274:         changes=[c.as_dict() if hasattr(c, "as_dict") else str(c) for c in preview.changes],
275:     )
276: 
277: 
278: def add_kind(
279:     target: Path | str,
280:     kind_name: str,
281:     definition: LoopDef | None = None,
282:     *,
283:     observer: str,
284:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None = None,
285:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
286: ) -> KindMutationResult:
287:     """Add a new loop-kind definition to a vertex and orchestrate declaration persistence.
288: 
289:     Parameters:
290:         target: Path to the .vertex file.
291:         kind_name: The name of the kind to add.
292:         definition: LoopDef AST node (defaults to items 'collect' 100).
293:         observer: Authorship identity performing the declaration update.
294:         credentials: Key provider for signing the ceremony.
295: 
296:     Returns:
297:         KindMutationResult detailing the preview, apply status, and updated generation.
298:     """
299:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
300:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path)
301:     if arrival is None:
302:         _refuse_legacy_mapped_credentials(credentials, vertex=vertex_path)
303:         info = resolve_target(target)
304:         if info.target_type != "vertex":
305:             raise TargetUnsupported(f"add_kind requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}")
306:     loop_def = definition or _default_loop_def()
307:     cred_provider = credentials or CustodyCredentialProvider()
308: 
309:     current_text = vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
310:     try:
311:         new_text = add_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name, loop_def)
312:     except Exception as exc:
313:         if arrival is not None:
314:             raise SdkValueError(f"could not generate Arrival kind mutation: {exc}") from exc
315:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"could not generate kind mutation: {exc}") from exc
316: 
317:     if arrival is not None:
318:         from .declare import _edit_arrival_declaration
319: 
320:         return _edit_arrival_declaration(
321:             vertex_path, new_text, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider,
322:             registry=registry,
323:             source_cache_bytes=current_text.encode("utf-8"),
324:             require_cache_basis=True,
325:         )
326: 
327:     preview = plan_declaration_update(vertex_path, proposed_text=new_text)
328:     if not preview.applicable:
329:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update not applicable: {preview.reason}")
330: 
331:     result = apply_declaration_update(preview, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider)
332:     if result.status not in ("applied", "noop"):
333:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update failed ({result.status}): {result.reason}")
334: 
335:     from engine.declaration import declaration_generation
336: 
337:     gen_after = declaration_generation(vertex_path)
338: 
339:     return _legacy_result(KindMutationResult(
340:         status=result.status,
341:         reason=result.reason,
342:         mode=preview.mode,
343:         vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
344:         generation_before=preview.generation,
345:         generation_after=gen_after,
346:         changes=[c.as_dict() if hasattr(c, "as_dict") else str(c) for c in preview.changes],
347:         file_written=result.file_written,
348:     ))
349:

351: def edit_kind(
352:     target: Path | str,
353:     kind_name: str,
354:     definition: LoopDef,
355:     *,
356:     observer: str,
357:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None = None,
358:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
359: ) -> KindMutationResult:
360:     """Edit an existing loop-kind definition in a vertex and orchestrate declaration persistence."""
361:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
362:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path)
363:     if arrival is None:
364:         _refuse_legacy_mapped_credentials(credentials, vertex=vertex_path)
365:         info = resolve_target(target)
366:         if info.target_type != "vertex":
367:             raise TargetUnsupported(f"edit_kind requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}")
368:     cred_provider = credentials or CustodyCredentialProvider()
369: 
370:     current_text = vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
371:     try:
372:         new_text = edit_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name, definition)
373:     except Exception as exc:
374:         if arrival is not None:
375:             raise SdkValueError(f"could not generate Arrival kind mutation: {exc}") from exc
376:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"could not generate kind mutation: {exc}") from exc
377: 
378:     if arrival is not None:
379:         from .declare import _edit_arrival_declaration
380: 
381:         return _edit_arrival_declaration(
382:             vertex_path, new_text, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider,
383:             registry=registry,
384:             source_cache_bytes=current_text.encode("utf-8"),
385:             require_cache_basis=True,
386:         )
387: 
388:     preview = plan_declaration_update(vertex_path, proposed_text=new_text)
389:     if not preview.applicable:
390:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update not applicable: {preview.reason}")
391: 
392:     result = apply_declaration_update(preview, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider)
393:     if result.status not in ("applied", "noop"):
394:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update failed ({result.status}): {result.reason}")
395: 
396:     from engine.declaration import declaration_generation
397: 
398:     gen_after = declaration_generation(vertex_path)
399: 
400:     return _legacy_result(KindMutationResult(
401:         status=result.status,
402:         reason=result.reason,
403:         mode=preview.mode,
404:         vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
405:         generation_before=preview.generation,
406:         generation_after=gen_after,
407:         changes=[c.as_dict() if hasattr(c, "as_dict") else str(c) for c in preview.changes],
408:         file_written=result.file_written,
409:     ))

412: def remove_kind(
413:     target: Path | str,
414:     kind_name: str,
415:     *,
416:     observer: str,
417:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None = None,
418:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
419: ) -> KindMutationResult:
420:     """Remove a loop-kind definition from a vertex and orchestrate declaration persistence."""
421:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
422:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path)
423:     if arrival is None:
424:         _refuse_legacy_mapped_credentials(credentials, vertex=vertex_path)

427:             raise TargetUnsupported(

429:             )

431: 
432:     current_text = vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
433:     try:
434:         new_text = remove_vertex_kind(current_text, kind_name)
435:     except Exception as exc:
436:         if arrival is not None:
437:             raise SdkValueError(f"could not generate Arrival kind mutation: {exc}") from exc
438:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"could not generate kind mutation: {exc}") from exc
439: 
440:     if arrival is not None:
441:         from .declare import _edit_arrival_declaration
442: 
443:         return _edit_arrival_declaration(
444:             vertex_path, new_text, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider,
445:             registry=registry,
446:             source_cache_bytes=current_text.encode("utf-8"),
447:             require_cache_basis=True,
448:         )
449: 
450:     preview = plan_declaration_update(vertex_path, proposed_text=new_text)
451:     if not preview.applicable:
452:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update not applicable: {preview.reason}")
453: 
454:     result = apply_declaration_update(preview, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider)
455:     if result.status not in ("applied", "noop"):
456:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update failed ({result.status}): {result.reason}")
457: 
458:     from engine.declaration import declaration_generation
459: 
460:     gen_after = declaration_generation(vertex_path)
461: 
462:     return _legacy_result(KindMutationResult(
463:         status=result.status,
464:         reason=result.reason,
465:         mode=preview.mode,
466:         vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
467:         generation_before=preview.generation,
468:         generation_after=gen_after,
469:         changes=[c.as_dict() if hasattr(c, "as_dict") else str(c) for c in preview.changes],
470:         file_written=result.file_written,

474: def grant_observer(
475:     target: Path | str,
476:     observer_name: str,
477:     *,
478:     grants: Sequence[str] | None = None,
479:     identity: str | None = None,
480:     key: str | None = None,
481:     observer: str,
482:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None = None,
483:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
484: ) -> KindMutationResult:
485:     """Add or update an observer in the vertex's admission block via ceremony.
486: 
487:     Parameters:
488:         target: Path to the .vertex file.
489:         observer_name: Name of the observer to declare/grant.
490:         grants: List of kind names this observer is allowed to emit.
491:         identity: Optional backing vertex identity store name.
492:         key: Optional base64 public key (if omitted, ensured via custody).
493:         observer: Authorship identity performing the declaration update.
494:         credentials: Key provider for signing the ceremony.
495: 
496:     Returns:
497:         KindMutationResult detailing the applied ceremony outcome.
498:     """
499:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
500:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path)
501:     if arrival is None:
502:         _refuse_legacy_mapped_credentials(credentials, vertex=vertex_path)
503:         info = resolve_target(target)
504:         if info.target_type != "vertex":
505:             raise TargetUnsupported(
506:                 f"grant_observer requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}"
507:             )
508:     cred_provider = credentials or CustodyCredentialProvider()
509: 
510:     if key is None:
511:         mapped = getattr(cred_provider, "mapped", False)
512:         if arrival is not None and not mapped:
513:             # A wrapper need not expose a provider-level marker. Classify its
514:             # actual write configuration before the legacy custody helper can
515:             # mint a key for this requested observer.
516:             from .declare import _arrival_credentials
517: 
518:             mapped = _arrival_credentials(vertex_path, cred_provider).mapped
519:         if mapped:
520:             raise SdkValueError(
521:                 "mapped observer grants require an explicitly pre-created binding public key"
522:             )
523:         from custody import ensure_signing_key
524: 
525:         try:
526:             keypair = ensure_signing_key(vertex_path, observer=observer_name)
527:         except ValueError as exc:
528:             if arrival is not None:
529:                 raise SdkValueError(f"could not prepare Arrival observer grant: {exc}") from exc
530:             raise CeremonyFailed(f"could not splice observer grant: {exc}") from exc
531:         pub_key = keypair.public_b64
532:     else:
533:         pub_key = key
534: 
535:     current_text = vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
536:     try:
537:         new_text = upsert_vertex_observer(
538:             current_text,
539:             observer_name,
540:             identity=identity,
541:             key=pub_key,
542:             grants=tuple(grants) if grants else (),
543:         )
544:     except Exception as exc:
545:         if arrival is not None:
546:             raise SdkValueError(f"could not generate Arrival observer grant: {exc}") from exc
547:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"could not splice observer grant: {exc}") from exc
548: 
549:     if arrival is not None:
550:         from .declare import _edit_arrival_declaration
551: 
552:         return _edit_arrival_declaration(
553:             vertex_path, new_text, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider,
554:             registry=registry,
555:             source_cache_bytes=current_text.encode("utf-8"),
556:             require_cache_basis=True,
557:         )
558: 
559:     preview = plan_declaration_update(vertex_path, proposed_text=new_text)
560:     if not preview.applicable:
561:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update not applicable: {preview.reason}")
562: 
563:     result = apply_declaration_update(preview, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider)
564:     if result.status not in ("applied", "noop"):
565:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update failed ({result.status}): {result.reason}")
566: 
567:     from engine.declaration import declaration_generation
568: 
569:     gen_after = declaration_generation(vertex_path)
570: 
571:     return _legacy_result(KindMutationResult(
572:         status=result.status,
573:         reason=result.reason,
574:         mode=preview.mode,
575:         vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
576:         generation_before=preview.generation,
577:         generation_after=gen_after,
578:         changes=[c.as_dict() if hasattr(c, "as_dict") else str(c) for c in preview.changes],
579:         file_written=result.file_written,
580:     ))
581: 
582: 
583: def revoke_observer(
584:     target: Path | str,
585:     observer_name: str,
586:     *,
587:     observer: str,
588:     credentials: CredentialProvider | None = None,
589:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
590: ) -> KindMutationResult:
591:     """Remove an observer from the vertex's declared admission block via ceremony."""
592:     vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
593:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor_for(vertex_path)
594:     if arrival is None:
595:         _refuse_legacy_mapped_credentials(credentials, vertex=vertex_path)
596:         info = resolve_target(target)
597:         if info.target_type != "vertex":
598:             raise TargetUnsupported(
599:                 f"revoke_observer requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}"
600:             )
601:     cred_provider = credentials or CustodyCredentialProvider()
602: 
603:     current_text = vertex_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
604:     try:
605:         new_text = remove_vertex_observer(current_text, observer_name)
606:     except ValueError as exc:
607:         if arrival is not None:
608:             raise SdkValueError(f"could not generate Arrival observer removal: {exc}") from exc
609:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"could not remove observer '{observer_name}': {exc}") from exc
610: 
611:     if arrival is not None:
612:         from .declare import _edit_arrival_declaration
613: 
614:         return _edit_arrival_declaration(
615:             vertex_path, new_text, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider,
616:             registry=registry,
617:             source_cache_bytes=current_text.encode("utf-8"),
618:             require_cache_basis=True,
619:         )
620: 
621:     preview = plan_declaration_update(vertex_path, proposed_text=new_text)
622:     if not preview.applicable:
623:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update not applicable: {preview.reason}")
624: 
625:     result = apply_declaration_update(preview, observer=observer, credentials=cred_provider)
626:     if result.status not in ("applied", "noop"):
627:         raise CeremonyFailed(f"declaration update failed ({result.status}): {result.reason}")
628: 
629:     from engine.declaration import declaration_generation
630: 
631:     gen_after = declaration_generation(vertex_path)
632: 
633:     return _legacy_result(KindMutationResult(
634:         status=result.status,
635:         reason=result.reason,
636:         mode=preview.mode,
637:         vertex_path=str(vertex_path),
638:         generation_before=preview.generation,
639:         generation_after=gen_after,
640:         changes=[c.as_dict() if hasattr(c, "as_dict") else str(c) for c in preview.changes],
641:         file_written=result.file_written,
642:     ))
643: 
644: 
645: def recover_ceremony(
646:     intent_path: Path | str,
647:     *,
648:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
649: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
650:     """Recover an interrupted legacy or explicit Arrival ceremony."""
651:     try:
652:         import json
653: 
654:         intent = Path(intent_path).resolve()
655:         data = json.loads(intent.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
656:         if data.get("schema") == "loops.engine/arrival-declaration-intent/v1":
657:             from .declare import recover_declaration
658: 
659:             return recover_declaration(intent, registry=registry).as_dict()
660:     except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):
661:         pass
662:     outcome = recover_declaration_update(intent_path)
663:     return {
664:         "classification": outcome.classification,
665:         "finished": outcome.finished,
666:         "reason": outcome.reason,
667:         "intent_path": str(outcome.intent_path),
668:     }

===== libs/sdk/src/sdk/target.py =====
1: """Target resolution and probing."""
2: 
3: from __future__ import annotations
4: 
5: import os
6: from pathlib import Path
7: from typing import Any
8: 
9: from engine.arrival_contract import StoreDescriptor
10: from engine.arrival_registry import descriptor_for
11: from engine.probe import TargetInfo, probe_target
12: from lang import parse_vertex_file, resolve_vertex
13: 
14: from .types import (
15:     ArrivalTarget,
16:     SdkValueError,
17:     StoreDescriptorInfo,
18:     TargetNotFound,
19:     TargetUnsupported,
20: )
21: 
22: __all__ = ["resolve_target", "resolve_arrival_target", "discover_targets", "TargetInfo"]
23: 
24: _IGNORE_DIRS = frozenset(
25:     {
26:         ".git",
27:         ".venv",
28:         "__pycache__",
29:         ".pytest_cache",
30:         ".ruff_cache",
31:         ".hypothesis",
32:         ".gemini",
33:         ".claude",
34:         ".subtask",
35:         ".uv-cache",
36:         "node_modules",
37:     }
38: )
39: 
40: 
41: def resolve_target(target: Path | str) -> TargetInfo:
42:     """Resolve and probe a target path without modifying it or opening SQLite.
43: 
44:     Returns `TargetInfo` describing target type, canonical path, and mode.
45:     Raises `TargetNotFound` if the path doesn't exist, or `TargetUnsupported`
46:     if the target cannot be classified as a loops artifact.
47:     """
48:     path = Path(target).resolve()
49:     if not path.exists():
50:         raise TargetNotFound(f"target path does not exist: {path}")
51: 
52:     info = probe_target(path)
53:     if info.target_type == "unknown":
54:         raise TargetUnsupported(
55:             f"target {path} is not a recognized loops artifact "
56:             "(accepted: .vertex, .jsonl, .db, .sqlite)"
57:         )
58: 
59:     return info
60: 
61: 
62: def _arrival_definition(
63:     target: Path | str, *, allow_aggregate: bool = False
64: ) -> tuple[Path, Any, StoreDescriptor | None] | None:
65:     """Parse one Arrival-capable root without probing or opening its residence.
66: 
67:     ``TargetInfo`` is a legacy filesystem classification and necessarily turns
68:     a store spelling into a ``Path``. Descriptor resolution instead parses the
69:     vertex locator directly, so another backend's DSN or service URL remains
70:     byte-for-byte opaque until that named adapter receives it. Aggregate shape
71:     remains refused by default; declaration inspection may opt in because it
72:     reports bounded root structure without claiming one basis for the aggregate.
73: 
74:     A valid storeless vertex retains its parsed AST with ``descriptor=None`` so
75:     a structural caller can make its descriptor/storeless decision from these
76:     same bytes. Malformed and non-vertex targets retain the legacy ``None``
77:     classification.
78:     """
79:     path = Path(target).resolve()
80:     if path.suffix.lower() != ".vertex":
81:         return None
82:     if not path.exists():
83:         raise TargetNotFound(f"target path does not exist: {path}")
84:     try:
85:         ast = parse_vertex_file(path)
86:     except Exception:
87:         return None  # the legacy resolver owns malformed-target classification
88:     descriptor = descriptor_for(ast, path)
89:     if descriptor is None:
90:         return path, ast, None
91:     if descriptor.role is None:
92:         raise SdkValueError(
93:             f"Arrival target {path} must declare store role explicitly"
94:         )
95:     if not allow_aggregate and (ast.combine is not None or ast.discover is not None):
96:         raise TargetUnsupported(
97:             f"Arrival target {path} is an aggregate; member-basis reads are not "
98:             "implemented in this single-store stage"
99:         )
100:     return path, ast, descriptor
101: 
102: 
103: def _arrival_descriptor(
104:     target: Path | str, *, allow_aggregate: bool = False
105: ) -> tuple[Path, Any, StoreDescriptor] | None:
106:     """Resolve and require an explicitly declared Arrival descriptor."""
107:     resolved = _arrival_definition(target, allow_aggregate=allow_aggregate)
108:     if resolved is None:
109:         return None
110:     path, ast, descriptor = resolved
111:     if descriptor is None:
112:         return None
113:     return path, ast, descriptor
114: 
115: 
116: def _refuse_arrival_aggregate_members(target: Path | str) -> None:
117:     """Keep legacy aggregate composition from opening descriptor members."""
118:     root = Path(target).resolve()
119:     if root.suffix.lower() != ".vertex" or not root.exists():
120:         return
121: 
122:     config_home = Path(
123:         os.environ.get(
124:             "LOOPS_HOME",
125:             Path(os.environ.get("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", Path.home() / ".config")) / "loops",
126:         )
127:     )
128:     visited: set[Path] = set()
129: 
130:     def visit(path: Path) -> None:
131:         path = path.resolve()
132:         if path in visited or not path.exists():
133:             return
134:         visited.add(path)
135:         try:
136:             ast = parse_vertex_file(path)
137:         except Exception:
138:             return  # legacy resolver reports malformed targets in its own taxonomy
139: 
140:         if path != root and ast.store_backend is not None:
141:             raise TargetUnsupported(
142:                 f"aggregate target {root} includes descriptor-backed member {path}; "
143:                 "member-basis Arrival reads are not implemented"
144:             )
145: 
146:         members: list[Path] = []
147:         if ast.discover is not None:
148:             members.extend(
149:                 match.resolve()
150:                 for match in sorted(path.parent.glob(ast.discover))
151:                 if match.suffix.lower() == ".vertex" and match.resolve() != path
152:             )
153:         elif ast.combine is not None:
154:             for entry in ast.combine:
155:                 member = resolve_vertex(entry.name, config_home)
156:                 if not member.is_absolute():
157:                     member = (path.parent / member).resolve()
158:                 members.append(member)
159:         for member in members:
160:             visit(member)
161: 
162:     visit(root)
163: 
164: 
165: def resolve_arrival_target(target: Path | str) -> ArrivalTarget:
166:     """Resolve the supported descriptor-first Arrival target.
167: 
168:     Bare stores and vertices without ``backend=`` remain legacy inputs. They
169:     are deliberately refused here instead of being inferred from a suffix.
170:     """
171:     resolved = _arrival_descriptor(target)
172:     if resolved is None:
173:         path = Path(target).resolve()
174:         raise TargetUnsupported(
175:             f"target {path} does not declare an Arrival backend and role"
176:         )
177:     path, ast, descriptor = resolved
178:     return ArrivalTarget(
179:         target_path=str(path),
180:         vertex_name=ast.name,
181:         store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
182:     )
183: 
184: 
185: def discover_targets(
186:     root_path: Path | str = ".",
187:     *,
188:     recursive: bool = True,
189:     include_bare: bool = True,
190: ) -> list[TargetInfo]:
191:     """Discover all Loops targets (vertices and bare stores) within a directory.
192: 
193:     Parameters:
194:         root_path: Root directory path to start scanning from.
195:         recursive: Whether to search subdirectories recursively.
196:         include_bare: Whether to include bare .jsonl / .db stores.
197: 
198:     Returns:
199:         Sorted list of TargetInfo descriptors for all discovered artifacts.
200:     """
201:     root = Path(root_path).resolve()
202:     if not root.exists() or not root.is_dir():
203:         return []
204: 
205:     discovered: list[TargetInfo] = []
206:     seen_paths: set[Path] = set()
207: 
208:     def _scan_dir(dir_path: Path) -> None:
209:         try:
210:             entries = sorted(dir_path.iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.name)
211:         except OSError:
212:             return
213: 
214:         for entry in entries:
215:             if entry.is_dir():
216:                 if entry.name in _IGNORE_DIRS or entry.name.startswith("."):
217:                     continue
218:                 if recursive:
219:                     _scan_dir(entry)
220:             elif entry.is_file():
221:                 suffix = entry.suffix.lower()
222:                 if suffix == ".vertex":
223:                     try:
224:                         info = resolve_target(entry)
225:                         if entry not in seen_paths:
226:                             seen_paths.add(entry)
227:                             discovered.append(info)
228:                     except Exception:
229:                         continue
230:                 elif include_bare and suffix in (".jsonl", ".db", ".sqlite"):
231:                     # Avoid adding .loops/data derived indices
232:                     if ".loops" in entry.parts:
233:                         continue
234:                     try:
235:                         info = resolve_target(entry)
236:                         if entry not in seen_paths:
237:                             seen_paths.add(entry)
238:                             discovered.append(info)
239:                     except Exception:
240:                         continue
241: 
242:     _scan_dir(root)
243:     discovered.sort(key=lambda t: str(t.canonical_path) if t.canonical_path else "")
244:     return discovered

===== libs/engine/src/engine/credentials.py =====
1: """Operation-fresh signing credentials for engine write coordinators.
2: 
3: This module contains values only.  It has no store, projection, or custody
4: dependency, so supported Arrival coordinators can accept signer injection
5: without importing the legacy SQLite handle implementation.
6: """
7: 
8: from __future__ import annotations
9: 
10: from collections.abc import Callable
11: from dataclasses import dataclass
12: from enum import StrEnum
13: from pathlib import Path
14: from typing import Protocol
15: 
16: from .arrival_contract import Head
17: 
18: __all__ = [
19:     "BindingResolver",
20:     "CredentialBindingEvidence",
21:     "CredentialBindingRefused",
22:     "CapturedSigningContext",
23:     "CredentialProvider",
24:     "CredentialPurpose",
25:     "CredentialRequest",
26:     "ResolvedCredential",
27:     "SignatureVerifier",
28:     "SigningDomain",
29:     "WriteCredentials",
30: ]
31: 
32: 
33: class SigningDomain(StrEnum):
34:     """The protocol domain whose commitment is being signed."""
35: 
36:     FACT = "fact"
37:     ARRIVAL = "arrival"
38:     TICK = "tick"
39: 
40: 
41: class CredentialPurpose(StrEnum):
42:     """Why one operation needs a signing credential."""
43: 
44:     AUTHORSHIP = "authorship"
45:     KEY_INTRODUCTION = "key-introduction"
46:     RECEIPT = "receipt"
47:     INITIALIZATION = "initialization"
48: 
49: 
50: @dataclass(frozen=True)
51: class CredentialRequest:
52:     """An exact local namespace, protocol observer, domain and purpose."""
53: 
54:     namespace: str
55:     observer: str
56:     domain: SigningDomain
57:     purpose: CredentialPurpose
58: 
59:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
60:         if not isinstance(self.namespace, str) or not self.namespace:
61:             raise ValueError("credential namespace must be an exact nonempty string")
62:         if not isinstance(self.observer, str) or not self.observer:
63:             raise ValueError("credential observer must be an exact nonempty string")
64:         if not isinstance(self.domain, SigningDomain):
65:             raise ValueError("credential domain must be a SigningDomain")
66:         if not isinstance(self.purpose, CredentialPurpose):
67:             raise ValueError("credential purpose must be a CredentialPurpose")
68: 
69: 
70: @dataclass(frozen=True)
71: class CredentialBindingEvidence:
72:     """Serializable public evidence for one resolved local binding."""
73: 
74:     request: CredentialRequest
75:     key_ref: str
76:     algorithm: str
77:     public_key: str
78:     provenance: str
79: 
80: 
81: @dataclass(frozen=True)
82: class ResolvedCredential:
83:     """Public binding evidence paired with its private signing operation."""
84: 
85:     evidence: CredentialBindingEvidence
86:     sign_digest: Callable[[str], str]
87: 
88: 
89: BindingResolver = Callable[[CredentialRequest], ResolvedCredential | None]
90: SignatureVerifier = Callable[[SigningDomain, str, str, str], bool]
91: 
92: 
93: class CredentialBindingRefused(ValueError):
94:     """A mapped binding could not safely authorize one planned signature."""
95: 
96:     def __init__(
97:         self,
98:         reason: str,
99:         *,
100:         request: CredentialRequest | None = None,
101:         captured_head: Head | None = None,
102:         use_position: int | None = None,
103:         evidence: CredentialBindingEvidence | None = None,
104:     ) -> None:
105:         super().__init__(reason)
106:         self.reason = reason
107:         self.request = request
108:         self.captured_head = captured_head
109:         self.use_position = use_position
110:         self.evidence = evidence
111: 
112: 
113: @dataclass(frozen=True)
114: class CapturedSigningContext:
115:     """Immutable authorization evidence derived from one verified prefix."""
116: 
117:     captured_head: Head
118:     author_keys: tuple[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]], ...]
119:     receipt_keys: tuple[str, ...]
120: 
121:     @property
122:     def use_position(self) -> int:
123:         return self.captured_head.ordinal + 1
124: 
125:     def keys_for_author(self, observer: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
126:         return next(
127:             (keys for name, keys in self.author_keys if name == observer),
128:             (),
129:         )
130: 
131: 
132: class CredentialResolutionSession:
133:     """Operation-local mapped resolution with cross-domain binding coherence."""
134: 
135:     __slots__ = ("_bindings", "_evidence")
136: 
137:     def __init__(self) -> None:
138:         self._bindings: dict[tuple[str, str], tuple[str, str]] = {}
139:         self._evidence: list[CredentialBindingEvidence] = []
140: 
141:     @property
142:     def evidence(self) -> tuple[CredentialBindingEvidence, ...]:
143:         return tuple(self._evidence)
144: 
145:     def sign(
146:         self,
147:         credentials: WriteCredentials,
148:         request: CredentialRequest,
149:         digest: str,
150:         *,
151:         context: CapturedSigningContext,
152:         authorized_keys: tuple[str, ...],
153:         required: bool,
154:     ) -> str | None:
155:         """Resolve, authorize and independently verify one mapped signature."""
156:         resolver = credentials.binding_resolver
157:         verifier = credentials.signature_verifier
158:         if not credentials.mapped or resolver is None or verifier is None:
159:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
160:                 "mapped signing requires a complete credential configuration",
161:                 request=request,
162:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
163:                 use_position=context.use_position,
164:             )
165:         try:
166:             resolved = resolver(request)
167:         except CredentialBindingRefused as exc:
168:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
169:                 exc.reason,
170:                 request=request,
171:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
172:                 use_position=context.use_position,
173:                 evidence=exc.evidence,
174:             ) from exc
175:         except Exception as exc:
176:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
177:                 "credential resolver failed",
178:                 request=request,
179:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
180:                 use_position=context.use_position,
181:             ) from exc
182:         if resolved is None:
183:             if not required:
184:                 return None
185:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
186:                 "missing-required",
187:                 request=request,
188:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
189:                 use_position=context.use_position,
190:             )
191:         if not isinstance(resolved, ResolvedCredential):
192:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
193:                 "resolver returned an invalid credential value",
194:                 request=request,
195:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
196:                 use_position=context.use_position,
197:             )
198:         evidence = resolved.evidence
199:         if (
200:             not isinstance(evidence, CredentialBindingEvidence)
201:             or evidence.request != request
202:             or not all(
203:                 isinstance(value, str) and bool(value)
204:                 for value in (
205:                     evidence.key_ref,
206:                     evidence.algorithm,
207:                     evidence.public_key,
208:                     evidence.provenance,
209:                 )
210:             )
211:             or not callable(resolved.sign_digest)
212:         ):
213:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
214:                 "resolver returned contradictory binding evidence",
215:                 request=request,
216:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
217:                 use_position=context.use_position,
218:                 evidence=evidence
219:                 if isinstance(evidence, CredentialBindingEvidence)
220:                 else None,
221:             )
222:         if evidence.public_key not in authorized_keys:
223:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
224:                 "resolved public key is not authorized at the captured position",
225:                 request=request,
226:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
227:                 use_position=context.use_position,
228:                 evidence=evidence,
229:             )
230:         binding_key = (request.namespace, request.observer)
231:         binding_value = (evidence.key_ref, evidence.public_key)
232:         prior = self._bindings.get(binding_key)
233:         if prior is not None and prior != binding_value:
234:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
235:                 "one mapped observer resolved to different bindings across domains",
236:                 request=request,
237:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
238:                 use_position=context.use_position,
239:                 evidence=evidence,
240:             )
241:         try:
242:             signature = resolved.sign_digest(digest)
243:         except CredentialBindingRefused as exc:
244:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
245:                 exc.reason,
246:                 request=request,
247:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
248:                 use_position=context.use_position,
249:                 evidence=exc.evidence or evidence,
250:             ) from exc
251:         except Exception as exc:
252:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
253:                 "credential signer failed",
254:                 request=request,
255:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
256:                 use_position=context.use_position,
257:                 evidence=evidence,
258:             ) from exc
259:         if not isinstance(signature, str) or not signature:
260:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
261:                 "mapped signer returned no signature",
262:                 request=request,
263:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
264:                 use_position=context.use_position,
265:                 evidence=evidence,
266:             )
267:         try:
268:             verified = verifier(
269:                 request.domain, evidence.public_key, signature, digest
270:             )
271:         except CredentialBindingRefused as exc:
272:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
273:                 exc.reason,
274:                 request=request,
275:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
276:                 use_position=context.use_position,
277:                 evidence=exc.evidence or evidence,
278:             ) from exc
279:         except Exception as exc:
280:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
281:                 "credential signature verifier failed",
282:                 request=request,
283:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
284:                 use_position=context.use_position,
285:                 evidence=evidence,
286:             ) from exc
287:         if not verified:
288:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
289:                 "mapped signature did not verify under its resolved public key",
290:                 request=request,
291:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
292:                 use_position=context.use_position,
293:                 evidence=evidence,
294:             )
295:         self._bindings[binding_key] = binding_value
296:         self._evidence.append(evidence)
297:         return signature
298: 
299: 
300: @dataclass(frozen=True)
301: class WriteCredentials:
302:     """The distinct domain-separated signers supplied for one write.
303: 
304:     ``tick_signer`` signs tick commitments, ``fact_signer`` signs inner fact
305:     commitments, and ``arrival_signer`` signs outer Arrival envelopes.  A
306:     missing signer means that axis is unsigned; one signer is never used as a
307:     fallback for another.
308:     """
309: 
310:     tick_signer: Callable[[str], str] | None = None
311:     fact_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None] | None = None
312:     arrival_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None] | None = None
313:     binding_namespace: str | None = None
314:     receipt_observer: str | None = None
315:     binding_resolver: BindingResolver | None = None
316:     signature_verifier: SignatureVerifier | None = None
317: 
318:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
319:         mapped_parts = (
320:             self.binding_namespace,
321:             self.binding_resolver,
322:             self.signature_verifier,
323:         )
324:         mapped = all(part is not None for part in mapped_parts)
325:         if any(part is not None for part in mapped_parts) and not mapped:
326:             raise ValueError(
327:                 "mapped credentials require namespace, resolver, and verifier together"
328:             )
329:         if self.receipt_observer is not None and not mapped:
330:             raise ValueError("receipt observer requires mapped credentials")
331:         if mapped and any(
332:             signer is not None
333:             for signer in (self.tick_signer, self.fact_signer, self.arrival_signer)
334:         ):
335:             raise ValueError("mapped credentials cannot include legacy signer callbacks")
336:         if self.binding_resolver is not None and not callable(self.binding_resolver):
337:             raise ValueError("credential binding resolver must be callable")
338:         if self.signature_verifier is not None and not callable(self.signature_verifier):
339:             raise ValueError("credential signature verifier must be callable")
340:         if self.binding_namespace is not None and (
341:             not isinstance(self.binding_namespace, str) or not self.binding_namespace
342:         ):
343:             raise ValueError("credential namespace must be an exact nonempty string")
344:         if self.receipt_observer is not None and (
345:             not isinstance(self.receipt_observer, str) or not self.receipt_observer
346:         ):
347:             raise ValueError("receipt observer must be an exact nonempty string")
348: 
349:     @property
350:     def mapped(self) -> bool:
351:         """Whether signing must use explicit mapped binding resolution."""
352:         return self.binding_namespace is not None
353: 
354: 
355: class CredentialProvider(Protocol):
356:     """Supply credentials at the moment of each write operation."""
357: 
358:     def for_write(self, vertex: Path) -> WriteCredentials: ...

94: @contextlib.contextmanager
95: def _open_arrival_read(
96:     resolved: tuple[Path, Any, Any],
97:     *,
98:     registry: BackendRegistry | None,
99:     continuation: Continuation | None = None,
100: ):
101:     """Open and close one descriptor-first read without exposing custody."""
102:     from engine.arrival_consumer import open_read
103: 
104:     _path, ast, descriptor = resolved
105:     opened = open_read(
106:         registry if registry is not None else BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends(),
107:         descriptor,
108:         requirement=ProjectionRequirement.CURRENT,
109:         continuation=continuation,
110:     )
111:     try:
112:         yield ast, descriptor, opened
113:     finally:
114:         opened.close()

124: def _fact_as_dict(fact: Fact) -> dict[str, Any]:
125:     return {
126:         "id": fact.id,
127:         "kind": fact.kind,
128:         "ts": fact.ts,
129:         "observer": fact.observer,
130:         "origin": fact.origin,
131:         "payload": dict(fact.payload),
132:         "arrival_ordinal": fact.arrival_ordinal,
133:         "arrival_seq": fact.arrival_seq,
134:     }

138:     return {

141:         "ts": tick.ts,
142:         "since": tick.since,
143:         "origin": tick.origin,
144:         "payload": dict(tick.payload),

149: 
150: def _latest_timestamp(kinds: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]]) -> float | None:
151:     latest: list[float] = []
152:     for stats in kinds.values():
153:         value = stats.get("latest")
154:         if isinstance(value, datetime):
155:             latest.append(value.timestamp())
156:         elif isinstance(value, (int, float)):
157:             latest.append(float(value))
158:         elif isinstance(value, str):
159:             with contextlib.suppress(ValueError, TypeError):
160:                 latest.append(datetime.fromisoformat(value).timestamp())
161:     return max(latest) if latest else None
162: 
163: 
164: def _arrival_generation(
165:     ast: Any,
166:     facts: tuple[Fact, ...],
167:     own_lineage: str,
168:     declaration_status: str,
169: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
170:     """Build the established declaration-generation review fields from one snapshot."""
171:     documents = vertex_to_documents(ast)
172:     canonical = json.dumps(
173:         [document.as_json() for document in documents],
174:         sort_keys=True,
175:         separators=(",", ":"),
176:     )
177:     declaration_head = None
178:     if declaration_status == "store":
179:         declaration_head = own_lineage
180:         for fact in sorted(facts, key=lambda item: (item.arrival_ordinal, item.arrival_seq)):
181:             if not fact.kind.startswith("_decl."):
182:                 continue
183:             if fact.id == own_lineage or fact.payload.get("lineage") == own_lineage:
184:                 declaration_head = fact.id
185:     return {
186:         "status": declaration_status,
187:         "lineage": own_lineage,
188:         "review_fingerprint": "sha256:"
189:         + hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
190:         "decl_head": declaration_head,
191:     }
192: 
193: 
194: def _arrival_declaration(
195:     locator_ast: Any,
196:     target_path: Path,
197:     opened: Any,
198:     *,
199:     include_user_facts: bool = False,
200:     allow_aggregate: bool = False,
201: ) -> tuple[Any, str, tuple[Fact, ...], str]:
202:     """Resolve effective declarations from the same bounded read snapshot."""
203:     facts = opened.snapshot.facts(
204:         FactRequest(
205:             limit=None,
206:             kind=None if include_user_facts else "_decl",
207:             include_internal=True,
208:             order="oldest",
209:         )
210:     ).items
211:     anchor = opened.snapshot.declaration_anchor
212:     documents = resolve_declaration_documents_from_snapshot(anchor, facts)
213:     if documents is None:
214:         raise SdkError(
215:             "the Arrival snapshot has no adopted declaration; refusing to "
216:             "substitute the current vertex file as store history"
217:         )
218:     status = "unhistorized" if isinstance(documents, Unhistorized) else "store"
219:     document_rows = documents.documents if isinstance(documents, Unhistorized) else documents
220:     effective_ast = documents_to_vertex(
221:         document_rows,
222:         path=target_path,
223:         store=locator_ast.store,
224:         store_backend=locator_ast.store_backend,
225:         store_location=locator_ast.store_location,
226:     )
227:     if not allow_aggregate and (
228:         effective_ast.combine is not None or effective_ast.discover is not None
229:     ):
230:         raise TargetUnsupported(
231:             "the bounded Arrival declaration is an aggregate; member-basis reads "
232:             "are not implemented in this single-store stage"
233:         )
234:     if anchor.own_lineage is None:
235:         raise SdkError("resolved declaration has no own lineage anchor")
236:     return effective_ast, status, facts, anchor.own_lineage
237: 
238: 
239: def _arrival_search_spec(locator_ast: Any, target_path: Path, opened: Any):
240:     """Derive one pin-checked search spec from this exact read snapshot."""
241:     from engine.arrival_search import derive_search_spec
242: 
243:     facts = opened.snapshot.facts(
244:         FactRequest(limit=None, kind="_decl", include_internal=True, order="oldest")
245:     ).items
246:     documents = resolve_declaration_documents_from_snapshot(
247:         opened.snapshot.declaration_anchor, facts
248:     )
249:     if documents is None:
250:         raise SdkError(
251:             "the Arrival snapshot has no adopted declaration; refusing to derive "
252:             "search fields from the locator file"
253:         )
254:     document_rows = documents.documents if isinstance(documents, Unhistorized) else documents
255:     effective = effective_declaration_from_documents(document_rows, locator_ast)
256:     if effective.combine is not None or effective.discover is not None:
257:         raise TargetUnsupported(
258:             "the bounded Arrival declaration is an aggregate; member-basis search "
259:             "is not implemented in this single-store stage"
260:         )
261:     return derive_search_spec(effective, target_path.parent)

264: def _refuse_unimplemented_arrival(
265:     target: Path | str, operation: str
266: ) -> None:
267:     if _arrival_descriptor(target) is not None:
268:         raise TargetUnsupported(
269:             f"{operation} is not yet supported for a descriptor-first Arrival target"
270:         )
271:     _refuse_arrival_aggregate_members(target)

273: 
274: def _check_declared_ordering(ordering: Ordering | None, *, single_store: bool) -> None:
275:     """Validate a declared read ordering against the axis this page path serves.
276: 
277:     ``None`` is the whole of today's behavior: the defaults are already what the
278:     paged read paths serve, so nothing reroutes. A DECLARED ordering must resolve
279:     to that same axis — ``Arrival()`` single-store, ``ByKey('ts')`` aggregate,
280:     with ``Arrival()`` on an aggregate refused by the one engine resolver. Any
281:     other declared ordering is refused rather than approximated: ordering-aware
282:     witness pagination is not this cut.
283: 
284:     Raises:
285:         SdkValueError: The ordering is refused, or names an axis paged reads do
286:             not serve this cut.
287:     """
288:     if ordering is None:
289:         return
290:     try:
291:         resolved = resolve_ordering(ordering, single_store=single_store)
292:     except OrderingError as exc:
293:         raise SdkValueError(str(exc)) from exc
294:     served = resolve_ordering(None, single_store=single_store)
295:     if resolved != served:
296:         raise SdkValueError(
297:             f"ordering {ordering!r} is not supported on a paged read this cut: "
298:             f"this path serves {served!r}. Declare that, or omit ordering."
299:         )
300: 
301: 
302: def _ensure_reader(canonical_path: Path, index_path: Path) -> tuple[StoreReader, bool]:
303:     """Ensure the target store index is current and return an open StoreReader.
304: 
305:     Returns (StoreReader, agreement_boolean).
306:     """
307:     preflight = read_preflight(canonical_path, mode=PreflightMode.RECOVER_THEN_OPEN)
308:     if preflight.store is not None:
309:         preflight.store.close()
310:     return StoreReader(index_path), preflight.agreed
