312: 
313: def _compute_summary_stats(
314:     reader: StoreReader,
315:     *,
316:     include_internal: bool = False,
317: ) -> tuple[int, int, float | None, dict[str, dict[str, Any]], tuple[int, int] | None]:
318:     """Extract factual totals, tick counts, time bounds, and attestation metrics."""
319:     raw_summary = reader.summary(include_internal=include_internal)
320:     fact_total = raw_summary.get("facts", {}).get("total", 0)
321:     tick_total = raw_summary.get("ticks", {}).get("total", 0)
322:     kinds_dict = raw_summary.get("facts", {}).get("kinds", {})
323: 
324:     all_latest: list[float] = []
325:     kinds: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
326:     for k, stats in kinds_dict.items():
327:         earliest_iso = stats.get("earliest")
328:         latest_iso = stats.get("latest")
329:         if latest_iso is not None:
330:             if isinstance(latest_iso, datetime):
331:                 all_latest.append(latest_iso.timestamp())
332:             elif isinstance(latest_iso, (int, float)):
333:                 all_latest.append(float(latest_iso))
334:             elif isinstance(latest_iso, str):
335:                 with contextlib.suppress(ValueError, TypeError):
336:                     all_latest.append(datetime.fromisoformat(latest_iso).timestamp())
337:         kinds[k] = {
338:             "count": stats.get("count", 0),
339:             "earliest": earliest_iso,
340:             "latest": latest_iso,
341:         }
342: 
343:     latest_ts = max(all_latest) if all_latest else None
344:     signed_counts = reader.signed_counts()
345:     return fact_total, tick_total, latest_ts, kinds, signed_counts
346: 
347: 
348: def _aggregate_summary(
349:     target_path: Path,
350:     *,
351:     include_internal: bool,
352:     registry: BackendRegistry | None,
353: ) -> ReadSummary:
354:     """Inventory retained member snapshots without manufacturing a common basis."""
355:     with open_aggregate_read(target_path, registry=registry) as aggregate:
356:         return _aggregate_summary_from(aggregate, target_path, include_internal=include_internal)
357: 
358: 
359: def _aggregate_summary_from(
360:     aggregate: Any, target_path: Path, *, include_internal: bool
361: ) -> ReadSummary:
362:     kinds: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
363:     ticks: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
364:     fact_total = tick_total = signed_count = unsigned_count = 0
365:     for member in aggregate.capture.members:
366:         raw = member.snapshot.summary(SummaryRequest(include_internal=include_internal))
367:         fact_total += raw.fact_total
368:         tick_total += raw.tick_total
369:         signed_count += raw.signed_count
370:         unsigned_count += raw.unsigned_count
371:         for name, stats in raw.fact_kinds.items():
372:             target = kinds.setdefault(name, {"count": 0, "earliest": None, "latest": None})
373:             target["count"] += stats.get("count", 0)
374:             for edge, reducer in (("earliest", min), ("latest", max)):
375:                 value = stats.get(edge)
376:                 if value is not None:
377:                     target[edge] = value if target[edge] is None else reducer(target[edge], value)
378:         for name, stats in raw.tick_names.items():
379:             target = ticks.setdefault(name, {"count": 0, "earliest": None, "latest": None})
380:             target["count"] += stats.get("count", 0)
381:             for edge, reducer in (("earliest", min), ("latest", max)):
382:                 value = stats.get(edge)
383:                 if value is not None:
384:                     target[edge] = value if target[edge] is None else reducer(target[edge], value)
385:     return ReadSummary(
386:             read_path="arrival-aggregate",
387:             target_type="vertex",
388:             target_path=str(target_path),
389:             fact_total=fact_total,
390:             tick_total=tick_total,
391:             latest_ts=_latest_timestamp(kinds),
392:             kinds=kinds,
393:             ticks=ticks,
394:             agreement=None,
395:             declaration_status="aggregate-captured",
396:             unfolded_kinds=[
397:                 name
398:                 for name in kinds
399:                 if name not in aggregate.specs() and not name.startswith("_decl.")
400:             ],
401:             signed_count=signed_count,
402:             unsigned_count=unsigned_count,
403:             aggregate_members=aggregate.member_evidence(),
404:             aggregate_definitions=aggregate.definition_evidence(),
405:     )
406: 
407: 
408: def _aggregate_state(
409:     target_path: Path, *, kind: str | None, observer: str | None, registry: BackendRegistry | None
410: ) -> FoldStateResult:
411:     """Replay frozen selected streams under recursively inherited fold specs."""
412:     with open_aggregate_read(target_path, registry=registry) as aggregate:
413:         return _aggregate_state_from(aggregate, target_path, kind=kind, observer=observer)
414: 
415: 
416: def _aggregate_state_from(
417:     aggregate: Any, target_path: Path, *, kind: str | None, observer: str | None
418: ) -> FoldStateResult:
419:     specs = aggregate.specs()
420:     sections: dict[str, Any] = {}
421:     for section_name, spec in specs.items():
422:         if kind is not None and section_name != kind:
423:             continue
424:         payloads: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
425:         for fact in aggregate.ordered_for_kind(section_name, observer=observer):
426:             payload = dict(fact.payload)
427:             payload.update(
428:                 {
429:                     "_ts": fact.ts,
430:                     "_observer": fact.observer,
431:                     "_origin": fact.origin,
432:                     "_id": fact.id,
433:                 }
434:             )
435:             payloads.append(payload)
436:         sections[section_name] = _serialize_fold_section(spec.replay(payloads))
437:     return FoldStateResult(
438:             read_path="arrival-aggregate",
439:             vertex_name=aggregate.root.effective_declaration.name,
440:             target_path=str(target_path),
441:             declaration_status="aggregate-captured",
442:             generation={"status": "aggregate", "member_count": len(aggregate.capture.members)},
443:             sections=sections,
444:             aggregate_members=aggregate.member_evidence(),
445:             aggregate_definitions=aggregate.definition_evidence(),
446:     )
447: 
448: 
449: def _aggregate_timeline(
450:     target_path: Path,
451:     *,
452:     start_ts: float | None,
453:     end_ts: float | None,
454:     limit: int,
455:     order: str,
456:     registry: BackendRegistry | None,
457: ) -> TimelineResult:
458:     """Timestamp lens over retained member snapshots; it does not fold rows."""
459:     with open_aggregate_read(target_path, registry=registry) as aggregate:
460:         return _aggregate_timeline_from(
461:             aggregate,
462:             start_ts=start_ts,
463:             end_ts=end_ts,
464:             limit=limit,
465:             order=order,
466:         )
467: 
468: 
469: def _aggregate_timeline_from(
470:     aggregate: Any,
471:     *,
472:     start_ts: float | None,
473:     end_ts: float | None,
474:     limit: int,
475:     order: str,
476: ) -> TimelineResult:
477:     since = start_ts if start_ts is not None else 0.0
478:     until = end_ts if end_ts is not None else float("inf")
479:     events = [
480:             TimelineEvent(
481:                 event_type=event_type,
482:                 id=row.id,
483:                 kind_or_name=row.kind if event_type == "fact" else row.name,
484:                 ts=row.ts,
485:                 observer=row.observer if event_type == "fact" else "",
486:                 origin=row.origin,
487:                 payload=dict(row.payload),
488:             )
489:         for event_type, _member, row in aggregate.all_events(start=since, end=until)
490:     ]
491:     if order == "newest":
492:         events.reverse()
493:     return TimelineResult(
494:             read_path="arrival-aggregate",
495:             events=events[:limit],
496:             start_ts=start_ts,
497:             end_ts=end_ts,
498:             total_events=len(events),
499:             truncated=len(events) > limit,
500:             order=order,
501:             aggregate_members=aggregate.member_evidence(),
502:             aggregate_definitions=aggregate.definition_evidence(),
503:     )
504: 
505: 
506: def read_summary(
507:     target: Path | str,
508:     *,
509:     include_internal: bool = False,
510:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
511: ) -> ReadSummary:
512:     """Read domain-neutral statistical inventory of a target artifact.
513: 
514:     Parameters:
515:         target: Path to .vertex, .jsonl, or .db artifact.
516:         include_internal: Whether to include reserved `_decl.*` kinds.
517: 
518:     Returns:
519:         ReadSummary containing fact/tick totals, kind distribution, and agreement.
520:     """
521:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
522:     if has_local_descriptor_aggregate(target_path):
523:         return _aggregate_summary(
524:             target_path, include_internal=include_internal, registry=registry
525:         )
526:     arrival = _aggregate_aware_arrival_descriptor(target_path)
527:     if arrival is not None:
528:         with _open_arrival_read(arrival, registry=registry) as (
529:             locator_ast,
530:             descriptor,
531:             opened,
532:         ):
533:             raw = opened.snapshot.summary(
534:                 SummaryRequest(include_internal=include_internal)
535:             )
536:             effective_ast, declaration_status, _facts, _own_lineage = _arrival_declaration(
537:                 locator_ast, target_path, opened, allow_aggregate=True
538:             )
539:             if effective_ast.combine is not None or effective_ast.discover is not None:
540:                 with open_aggregate_read(
541:                     target_path, registry=registry, opened_root=opened
542:                 ) as aggregate:
543:                     return _aggregate_summary_from(
544:                         aggregate, target_path, include_internal=include_internal
545:                     )
546:             kinds = {name: dict(stats) for name, stats in raw.fact_kinds.items()}
547:             return ReadSummary(
548:                 read_path="arrival",
549:                 basis=opened.basis,
550:                 store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
551:                 target_type="vertex",
552:                 target_path=str(target_path),
553:                 fact_total=raw.fact_total,
554:                 tick_total=raw.tick_total,
555:                 latest_ts=_latest_timestamp(raw.fact_kinds),
556:                 kinds=kinds,
557:                 ticks={name: dict(stats) for name, stats in raw.tick_names.items()},
558:                 agreement=None,
559:                 declaration_status=declaration_status,
560:                 unfolded_kinds=[
561:                     name
562:                     for name in kinds
563:                     if name not in effective_ast.loops and not name.startswith("_decl.")
564:                 ],
565:                 signed_count=raw.signed_count,
566:                 unsigned_count=raw.unsigned_count,
567:             )
568: 
569:     _refuse_arrival_aggregate_members(target_path)
570:     info = resolve_target(target)
571:     if info.target_type == "vertex":
572:         decl_ast, decl_status = load_declaration_status(target_path)
573:         is_aggregate = decl_ast is not None and (
574:             decl_ast.combine is not None or decl_ast.discover is not None
575:         )
576: 
577:         if not is_aggregate and (info.canonical_path is None or not info.canonical_path.exists()):
578:             return ReadSummary(
579:                 target_type="vertex",
580:                 target_path=str(target_path),
581:                 canonical_mode=info.canonical_mode or "unknown",
582:                 canonical_path=str(info.canonical_path) if info.canonical_path else None,
583:                 index_path=str(info.index_path) if info.index_path else None,
584:                 declaration_status=decl_status or "unknown",
585:                 fact_total=0,
586:                 tick_total=0,
587:                 latest_ts=None,
588:                 kinds={},
589:                 unfolded_kinds=[],
590:                 agreement=True,
591:                 signed_count=0,
592:                 unsigned_count=0,
593:             )
594: 
595:         if is_aggregate:
596:             raw_sum = vertex_summary(target_path, include_internal=include_internal)
597:             fact_total = raw_sum.get("facts", {}).get("total", 0)
598:             tick_total = raw_sum.get("ticks", {}).get("total", 0)
599:             kinds_raw = raw_sum.get("facts", {}).get("kinds", {})
600:             kinds = {
601:                 k: {
602:                     "count": v.get("count", 0),
603:                     "earliest": v.get("earliest"),
604:                     "latest": v.get("latest"),
605:                 }
606:                 for k, v in kinds_raw.items()
607:             }
608:             return ReadSummary(
609:                 target_type="vertex",
610:                 target_path=str(target_path),
611:                 canonical_mode=info.canonical_mode or "aggregate",
612:                 canonical_path=str(info.canonical_path) if info.canonical_path else None,
613:                 index_path=str(info.index_path) if info.index_path else None,
614:                 declaration_status=decl_status or "aggregate-head",
615:                 fact_total=fact_total,
616:                 tick_total=tick_total,
617:                 latest_ts=None,
618:                 kinds=kinds,
619:                 unfolded_kinds=[],
620:                 agreement=True,
621:                 signed_count=0,
622:                 unsigned_count=fact_total,
623:             )
624: 
625:         canonical = info.canonical_path or target_path
626:         index_path = info.index_path or canonical
627:         reader, agreed = _ensure_reader(canonical, index_path)
628:         try:
629:             fact_total, tick_total, latest_ts, kinds, signed_counts = _compute_summary_stats(
630:                 reader, include_internal=include_internal
631:             )
632:             unfolded = [
633:                 k
634:                 for k in kinds
635:                 if decl_ast is not None and k not in decl_ast.loops and not k.startswith("_decl.")
636:             ]
637:             # signed_counts is (signed, TOTAL) — unsigned is the difference
638:             signed_count = signed_counts[0] if signed_counts else 0
639:             unsigned_count = (signed_counts[1] - signed_counts[0]) if signed_counts else 0
640: 
641:             return ReadSummary(
642:                 target_type="vertex",
643:                 target_path=str(target_path),
644:                 canonical_mode=info.canonical_mode or "unknown",
645:                 canonical_path=str(canonical),
646:                 index_path=str(index_path),
647:                 declaration_status=decl_status or "unknown",
648:                 fact_total=fact_total,
649:                 tick_total=tick_total,
650:                 latest_ts=latest_ts,
651:                 kinds=kinds,
652:                 unfolded_kinds=unfolded,
653:                 agreement=agreed,
654:                 signed_count=signed_count,
655:                 unsigned_count=unsigned_count,
656:             )
657:         finally:
658:             reader.close()
659: 
660:     # Bare store (.jsonl or .db)
661:     canonical = info.canonical_path or target_path
662:     index_path = info.index_path or canonical
663:     reader, agreed = _ensure_reader(canonical, index_path)
664:     try:
665:         fact_total, tick_total, latest_ts, kinds, signed_counts = _compute_summary_stats(
666:             reader, include_internal=include_internal
667:         )
668:         # signed_counts is (signed, TOTAL) — unsigned is the difference
669:         signed_count = signed_counts[0] if signed_counts else 0
670:         unsigned_count = (signed_counts[1] - signed_counts[0]) if signed_counts else 0
671: 
672:         return ReadSummary(
673:             target_type=info.target_type,
674:             target_path=str(target_path),
675:             canonical_mode=info.canonical_mode or "unknown",
676:             canonical_path=str(canonical),
677:             index_path=str(index_path),
678:             declaration_status=None,
679:             fact_total=fact_total,
680:             tick_total=tick_total,
681:             latest_ts=latest_ts,
682:             kinds=kinds,
683:             unfolded_kinds=[],
684:             agreement=agreed,
685:             signed_count=signed_count,
686:             unsigned_count=unsigned_count,
687:         )
688:     finally:
689:         reader.close()
690: 
691: 
692: def read_facts(
693:     target: Path | str,
694:     *,
695:     limit: int = 50,
696:     kind: str | None = None,
697:     observer: str | None = None,
698:     order: str = "newest",
699:     ordering: Ordering | None = None,
700:     before: str | Continuation | None = None,
701:     after: str | Continuation | None = None,
702:     include_internal: bool = False,
703:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
704: ) -> FactPageResult:
705:     """Read a bounded page of facts with stable witness pagination cursors.
706: 
707:     Parameters:
708:         target: Path to .vertex, .jsonl, or .db artifact.
709:         limit: Maximum number of facts to return.
710:         kind: Optional kind filter.
711:         observer: Optional observer identity filter.
712:         order: Sort direction along the ordering axis — 'oldest' ascending,
713:             'newest' descending.
714:         ordering: Declared read ordering. ``None`` keeps today's axis: receipt
715:             order for a single-store vertex, the ``(ts, id)`` read lens for an
716:             aggregate. Declaring ``Arrival()`` on a multi-store aggregate is
717:             refused — see :func:`engine.vertex_reader.resolve_ordering`. Only
718:             the resolved default axis is supported this cut; any other declared
719:             ordering is refused rather than silently approximated.
720:         before: Cursor token to fetch rows before (older than) the cursor in newest order.
721:         after: Cursor token to fetch rows after (newer than) the cursor in oldest order.
722:         include_internal: Whether to include internal `_decl.*` facts.
723: 
724:     Returns:
725:         FactPageResult containing deserialized fact items and pagination metadata.
726:     """
727:     if order not in ("newest", "oldest"):
728:         raise SdkValueError(f"invalid order '{order}': expected 'newest' or 'oldest'")
729: 
730:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
731:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor(target_path)
732:     if arrival is not None:
733:         if before is not None and after is not None:
734:             raise SdkValueError("before and after are mutually exclusive")
735:         token = before if before is not None else after
736:         if token is not None and not isinstance(token, Continuation):
737:             raise SdkValueError(
738:                 "descriptor-first reads require the Continuation returned by a prior page"
739:             )
740:         if before is not None and order != "newest":
741:             raise SdkValueError("before continuation requires order='newest'")
742:         if after is not None and order != "oldest":
743:             raise SdkValueError("after continuation requires order='oldest'")
744:         request = FactRequest(
745:             limit=limit,
746:             kind=kind,
747:             observer=observer,
748:             order=order,
749:             include_internal=include_internal,
750:         )
751:         with _open_arrival_read(
752:             arrival, registry=registry, continuation=token
753:         ) as (locator_ast, descriptor, opened):
754:             if token is None:
755:                 _arrival_declaration(locator_ast, target_path, opened)
756:             page = opened.snapshot.facts(request)
757:             next_cursor = opened.continuation(request, page)
758:             return FactPageResult(
759:                 read_path="arrival",
760:                 basis=opened.basis,
761:                 store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
762:                 items=[_fact_as_dict(fact) for fact in page.items],
763:                 next_cursor=next_cursor,
764:                 prev_cursor=None,
765:                 truncated=page.truncated,
766:                 order=page.order,
767:             )
768: 
769:     _refuse_arrival_aggregate_members(target_path)
770:     info = resolve_target(target)
771:     if isinstance(before, Continuation) or isinstance(after, Continuation):
772:         raise SdkValueError(
773:             "Arrival Continuation values cannot be used on a legacy read path"
774:         )
775: 
776:     if info.target_type == "vertex":
777:         decl_ast, _ = load_declaration_status(target_path)
778:         is_aggregate = decl_ast is not None and (
779:             decl_ast.combine is not None or decl_ast.discover is not None
780:         )
781:         # read_facts keys on DECLARATION SHAPE, not member count: an aggregate's
782:         # page comes off the (ts, id) lens whatever its member count, so that is
783:         # the axis a declared ordering is checked against.
784:         _check_declared_ordering(ordering, single_store=not is_aggregate)
785: 
786:         if is_aggregate:
787:             # Multi-store aggregate vertex
788:             all_facts = vertex_facts(
789:                 target_path,
790:                 since_ts=0.0,
791:                 until_ts=float("inf"),
792:                 kind=kind,
793:                 observer=observer,
794:                 include_internal=include_internal,
795:             )
796:             if order == "newest":
797:                 all_facts = list(reversed(all_facts))
798: 
799:             capped = all_facts[:limit]
800:             truncated = len(all_facts) > len(capped)
801:             return FactPageResult(
802:                 items=capped,
803:                 next_cursor=None,
804:                 prev_cursor=None,
805:                 truncated=truncated,
806:                 order=order,
807:             )
808: 
809:         if info.canonical_path is None or not info.canonical_path.exists():
810:             return FactPageResult(
811:                 items=[],
812:                 next_cursor=None,
813:                 prev_cursor=None,
814:                 truncated=False,
815:                 order=order,
816:             )
817: 
818:         # Witness resolution reads the sqlite index, not the (possibly jsonl)
819:         # canonical log — passing the canonical raised DatabaseError on
820:         # jsonl-canonical vertices.
821:         witness_store = info.index_path or info.canonical_path
822:         before_pos = resolve_witness_position(witness_store, before) if before else None
823:         after_pos = resolve_witness_position(witness_store, after) if after else None
824: 
825:         page = vertex_query_facts(
826:             target_path,
827:             limit=limit,
828:             before=before_pos,
829:             after=after_pos,
830:             kind=kind,
831:             observer=observer,
832:             include_internal=include_internal,
833:             order=order,
834:         )
835:         next_tok = page.next.fact_id or f"seq:{page.next.seq}" if page.next is not None else None
836:         page_prev: Any = getattr(page, "prev", None)
837:         prev_tok = page_prev.fact_id or f"seq:{page_prev.seq}" if page_prev is not None else None
838:         return FactPageResult(
839:             items=page.items,
840:             next_cursor=next_tok,
841:             prev_cursor=prev_tok,
842:             truncated=page.truncated,
843:             order=order,
844:         )
845: 
846:     # A bare .db/.jsonl target is one store, so its axis is Arrival().
847:     _check_declared_ordering(ordering, single_store=True)
848: 
849:     canonical = info.canonical_path or target_path
850:     index_path = info.index_path or canonical
851: 
852:     reader, _ = _ensure_reader(canonical, index_path)
853:     try:
854:         # Same as the vertex branch: witness cursors resolve against the sqlite
855:         # index — after _ensure_reader, which builds it for bare jsonl targets.
856:         before_pos = resolve_witness_position(index_path, before) if before else None
857:         after_pos = resolve_witness_position(index_path, after) if after else None
858: 
859:         page = reader.query_facts(
860:             limit=limit,
861:             before=before_pos,
862:             after=after_pos,
863:             kind=kind,
864:             observer=observer,
865:             include_internal=include_internal,
866:             order=order,
867:         )
868:         next_tok = page.next.fact_id or f"seq:{page.next.seq}" if page.next is not None else None
869:         page_prev: Any = getattr(page, "prev", None)
870:         prev_tok = page_prev.fact_id or f"seq:{page_prev.seq}" if page_prev is not None else None
871: 
872:         return FactPageResult(
873:             items=page.items,
874:             next_cursor=next_tok,
875:             prev_cursor=prev_tok,
876:             truncated=page.truncated,
877:             order=order,
878:         )
879:     finally:
880:         reader.close()
881: 
882: 
883: def _tick_as_dict(t: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
884:     """Serialize a tick record: engine's Tick exposes to_dict(), not as_dict().
885: 
886:     The old ``dict(t)`` fallback raised TypeError on every real fired Tick.
887:     """
888:     if hasattr(t, "as_dict"):
889:         return t.as_dict()
890:     if hasattr(t, "to_dict"):
891:         return t.to_dict()
892:     return dict(t)
893: 
894: 
895: def _serialize_fold_item(item: Any) -> Any:
896:     """Serialize fold state item values cleanly."""
897:     if hasattr(item, "predicate") and hasattr(item, "address"):
898:         return {"predicate": item.predicate, "address": item.address}
899:     if hasattr(item, "as_dict"):
900:         return item.as_dict()
901:     if isinstance(item, Mapping):
902:         return {str(k): _serialize_fold_item(v) for k, v in item.items()}
903:     if hasattr(item, "__dict__"):
904:         return {str(k): _serialize_fold_item(v) for k, v in vars(item).items()}
905:     if isinstance(item, (list, tuple)):
906:         return [_serialize_fold_item(x) for x in item]
907:     return item
908: 
909: 
910: def _serialize_fold_section(section: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
911:     """Serialize a single kind fold state section."""
912:     if hasattr(section, "as_dict"):
913:         raw = section.as_dict()
914:     elif isinstance(section, Mapping):
915:         raw = dict(section)
916:     elif hasattr(section, "__dict__"):
917:         raw = vars(section)
918:     else:
919:         raw = {"value": section}
920: 
921:     serialized: dict[str, Any] = {}
922:     for k, v in raw.items():
923:         serialized[str(k)] = _serialize_fold_item(v)
924:     return serialized
925: 
926: 
927: def read_state(
928:     target: Path | str,
929:     *,
930:     kind: str | None = None,
931:     observer: str | None = None,
932:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
933: ) -> FoldStateResult:
934:     """Reconstruct live fold state by replaying facts through declared vertex folds.
935: 
936:     Parameters:
937:         target: Path to .vertex artifact.
938:         kind: Optional single-kind filter.
939:         observer: Optional observer view filter.
940: 
941:     Returns:
942:         FoldStateResult containing reconstructed state sections and generation info.
943:     """
944:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
945:     if has_local_descriptor_aggregate(target_path):
946:         return _aggregate_state(
947:             target_path, kind=kind, observer=observer, registry=registry
948:         )
949:     arrival = _aggregate_aware_arrival_descriptor(target_path)
950:     if arrival is not None:
951:         with _open_arrival_read(arrival, registry=registry) as (
952:             locator_ast,
953:             descriptor,
954:             opened,
955:         ):
956:             effective_ast, declaration_status, facts, own_lineage = _arrival_declaration(
957:                 locator_ast,
958:                 target_path,
959:                 opened,
960:                 include_user_facts=True,
961:                 allow_aggregate=True,
962:             )
963:             if effective_ast.combine is not None or effective_ast.discover is not None:
964:                 with open_aggregate_read(
965:                     target_path, registry=registry, opened_root=opened
966:                 ) as aggregate:
967:                     return _aggregate_state_from(
968:                         aggregate, target_path, kind=kind, observer=observer
969:                     )
970:             specs = compile_vertex(effective_ast)
971:             sections: dict[str, Any] = {}
972:             for section_name, spec in specs.items():
973:                 if kind is not None and section_name != kind:
974:                     continue
975:                 selected = [
976:                     fact
977:                     for fact in facts
978:                     if fact.kind == section_name
979:                     and (observer is None or observer_matches(fact.observer, observer))
980:                 ]
981:                 payloads: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
982:                 for fact in selected:
983:                     payload = dict(fact.payload)
984:                     payload.update(
985:                         {
986:                             "_ts": fact.ts,
987:                             "_observer": fact.observer,
988:                             "_origin": fact.origin,
989:                             "_id": fact.id,
990:                         }
991:                     )
992:                     payloads.append(payload)
993:                 sections[section_name] = _serialize_fold_section(spec.replay(payloads))
994: 
995:             return FoldStateResult(
996:                 read_path="arrival",
997:                 basis=opened.basis,
998:                 store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
999:                 vertex_name=effective_ast.name,
1000:                 target_path=str(target_path),
1001:                 declaration_status=declaration_status,
1002:                 generation=_arrival_generation(
1003:                     effective_ast, facts, own_lineage, declaration_status
1004:                 ),
1005:                 sections=sections,
1006:             )
1007: 
1008:     _refuse_arrival_aggregate_members(target_path)
1009:     info = resolve_target(target)
1010:     if info.target_type != "vertex":
1011:         raise TargetUnsupported(f"read_state requires a .vertex target, got {info.target_type}")
1012: 
1013:     decl_ast, decl_status = load_declaration_status(target_path)
1014:     vertex_path = target_path
1015: 
1016:     if info.canonical_path is None or not info.canonical_path.exists():
1017:         return FoldStateResult(
1018:             vertex_name=decl_ast.name if decl_ast else vertex_path.stem,
1019:             target_path=str(vertex_path),
1020:             declaration_status=decl_status or "unknown",
1021:             generation={},
1022:             sections={},
1023:         )
1024: 
1025:     state_dict = vertex_read(vertex_path, observer=observer)
1026:     if not isinstance(state_dict, dict):
1027:         state_dict = {}
1028: 
1029:     sections: dict[str, Any] = {}
1030:     for sec_name, sec_val in state_dict.items():
1031:         if kind is not None and sec_name != kind:
1032:             continue
1033:         sections[sec_name] = _serialize_fold_section(sec_val)
1034: 
1035:     gen = getattr(decl_ast, "generation", {}) if decl_ast else {}
1036: 
1037:     generation_as_dict = getattr(gen, "as_dict", None)
1038:     return FoldStateResult(
1039:         vertex_name=decl_ast.name if decl_ast else vertex_path.stem,
1040:         target_path=str(vertex_path),
1041:         declaration_status=decl_status or "unknown",
1042:         generation=generation_as_dict() if callable(generation_as_dict) else gen,
1043:         sections=sections,
1044:     )
1045: 
1046: 
1047: def read_ticks(
1048:     target: Path | str,
1049:     *,
1050:     name: str | None = None,
1051:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
1052: ) -> TickReadResult:
1053:     """Read chronological tick seals and cadence boundaries.
1054: 
1055:     Parameters:
1056:         target: Path to .vertex, .jsonl, or .db artifact.
1057:         name: Optional tick mark name filter.
1058: 
1059:     Returns:
1060:         TickReadResult with chronological records and the read basis when the
1061:         target uses a descriptor-first Arrival store.
1062:     """
1063:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
1064:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor(target_path)
1065:     if arrival is not None:
1066:         with _open_arrival_read(arrival, registry=registry) as (
1067:             locator_ast,
1068:             descriptor,
1069:             opened,
1070:         ):
1071:             _arrival_declaration(locator_ast, target_path, opened)
1072:             ticks = opened.snapshot.ticks(TickRequest(name=name))
1073:             return TickReadResult(
1074:                 read_path="arrival",
1075:                 basis=opened.basis,
1076:                 store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
1077:                 items=[_arrival_tick_as_dict(tick) for tick in ticks],
1078:             )
1079: 
1080:     _refuse_arrival_aggregate_members(target_path)
1081:     info = resolve_target(target)
1082: 
1083:     if info.target_type == "vertex":
1084:         decl_ast, _ = load_declaration_status(target_path)
1085:         is_aggregate = decl_ast is not None and (
1086:             decl_ast.combine is not None or decl_ast.discover is not None
1087:         )
1088:         if is_aggregate:
1089:             ticks = vertex_ticks(target_path, 0.0, float("inf"), name=name)
1090:             return TickReadResult(items=[_tick_as_dict(t) for t in ticks])
1091: 
1092:         if info.canonical_path is None or not info.canonical_path.exists():
1093:             return TickReadResult(items=[])
1094: 
1095:     canonical = info.canonical_path or target_path
1096:     index_path = info.index_path or canonical
1097:     reader, _ = _ensure_reader(canonical, index_path)
1098:     try:
1099:         ticks = reader.ticks_between(0.0, float("inf"), name=name)
1100:         return TickReadResult(items=[_tick_as_dict(t) for t in ticks])
1101:     finally:
1102:         reader.close()
1103: 
1104: 
1105: def read_fact_by_id(
1106:     target: Path | str,
1107:     fact_id: str,
1108:     *,
1109:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
1110: ) -> FactLookupResult:
1111:     """Locate and return a single fact by its ULID identifier.
1112: 
1113:     Parameters:
1114:         target: Path to .vertex, .jsonl, or .db artifact.
1115:         fact_id: ULID or ID string of the target fact.
1116: 
1117:     Returns:
1118:         FactLookupResult retaining the read basis even when no fact matched.
1119:     """
1120:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
1121:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor(target_path)
1122:     if arrival is not None:
1123:         request = FactRequest(limit=None, fact_id=fact_id)
1124:         with _open_arrival_read(arrival, registry=registry) as (
1125:             locator_ast,
1126:             descriptor,
1127:             opened,
1128:         ):
1129:             _arrival_declaration(locator_ast, target_path, opened)
1130:             matches = opened.snapshot.facts(request).items
1131:             if len(matches) > 1:
1132:                 raise SdkValueError(f"Ambiguous ID prefix: {fact_id!r}")
1133:             return FactLookupResult(
1134:                 read_path="arrival",
1135:                 basis=opened.basis,
1136:                 store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
1137:                 fact=None if not matches else _fact_as_dict(matches[0]),
1138:             )
1139: 
1140:     _refuse_arrival_aggregate_members(target_path)
1141:     info = resolve_target(target)
1142: 
1143:     if info.target_type == "vertex":
1144:         decl_ast, _ = load_declaration_status(target_path)
1145:         is_aggregate = decl_ast is not None and (
1146:             decl_ast.combine is not None or decl_ast.discover is not None
1147:         )
1148:         if is_aggregate:
1149:             return FactLookupResult(fact=vertex_fact_by_id(target_path, fact_id))
1150: 
1151:         if info.canonical_path is None or not info.canonical_path.exists():
1152:             return FactLookupResult(fact=None)
1153: 
1154:     canonical = info.canonical_path or target_path
1155:     index_path = info.index_path or canonical
1156:     reader, _ = _ensure_reader(canonical, index_path)
1157:     try:
1158:         return FactLookupResult(fact=reader.fact_by_id(fact_id))
1159:     finally:
1160:         reader.close()
1161: 
1162: 
1163: def search_facts(
1164:     target: Path | str,
1165:     query: str,
1166:     *,
1167:     kind: str | None = None,
1168:     observer: str | None = None,
1169:     since: float | None = None,
1170:     until: float | None = None,
1171:     limit: int = 50,
1172:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
1173: ) -> SearchResult:
1174:     """Execute full-text search (FTS5) over fact payloads.
1175: 
1176:     Parameters:
1177:         target: Path to .vertex, .jsonl, or .db artifact.
1178:         query: Full-text search expression.
1179:         kind: Optional kind-subtree filter.
1180:         observer: Optional exact observer filter.
1181:         since: Optional inclusive event-time lower bound.
1182:         until: Optional inclusive event-time upper bound.
1183:         limit: Maximum number of matching items.
1184: 
1185:     Returns:
1186:         SearchResult containing ranked SearchResultItem matches.
1187:     """
1188:     if limit < 1:
1189:         raise SdkValueError("search limit must be >= 1")
1190:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
1191:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor(target_path)
1192:     if arrival is not None:
1193:         from .errors import normalize_exception
1194: 
1195:         try:
1196:             with _open_arrival_read(arrival, registry=registry) as (
1197:                 locator_ast,
1198:                 descriptor,
1199:                 opened,
1200:             ):
1201:                 spec = _arrival_search_spec(locator_ast, target_path, opened)
1202:                 page = opened.snapshot.search(
1203:                     SearchRequest(
1204:                         expression=query,
1205:                         expected_fields_hash=spec.fields_hash,
1206:                         kind=kind,
1207:                         observer=observer,
1208:                         since=since,
1209:                         until=until,
1210:                         limit=limit,
1211:                     )
1212:                 )
1213:                 return SearchResult(
1214:                     read_path="arrival",
1215:                     basis=opened.basis,
1216:                     store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
1217:                     query=query,
1218:                     matches=[
1219:                         SearchResultItem(
1220:                             id=match.fact.id,
1221:                             kind=match.fact.kind,
1222:                             ts=match.fact.ts,
1223:                             observer=match.fact.observer,
1224:                             origin=match.fact.origin,
1225:                             payload=dict(match.fact.payload),
1226:                             rank=match.rank,
1227:                             snippet=match.snippet or "",
1228:                         )
1229:                         for match in page.matches
1230:                     ],
1231:                     total_matches=page.total_matches,
1232:                     truncated=page.truncated,
1233:                     ranking=page.ranking,
1234:                     ranking_through=page.ranking_through,
1235:                     fields_hash=page.fields_hash,
1236:                 )
1237:         except BaseException as exc:
1238:             normalized = normalize_exception(exc)
1239:             if normalized is exc:
1240:                 raise
1241:             raise normalized from exc
1242: 
1243:     _refuse_arrival_aggregate_members(target_path)
1244:     if observer is not None or since is not None or until is not None:
1245:         raise SdkValueError(
1246:             "observer and time filters are currently available only for an "
1247:             "explicit Arrival search target"
1248:         )
1249:     info = resolve_target(target)
1250: 
1251:     matches: list[SearchResultItem] = []
1252:     if info.target_type == "vertex":
1253:         try:
1254:             raw_matches = vertex_search(target_path, query, kind=kind, limit=limit)
1255:             for raw_match in raw_matches:
1256:                 m: Any = raw_match
1257:                 payload = m.payload if hasattr(m, "payload") else m.get("payload", {})
1258:                 if isinstance(payload, str):
1259:                     with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
1260:                         payload = json.loads(payload)
1261:                 m_ts = m.ts if hasattr(m, "ts") else m.get("ts", 0.0)
1262:                 m_ts_val = m_ts.timestamp() if hasattr(m_ts, "timestamp") else float(m_ts or 0.0)
1263:                 m_obs = str(m.observer if hasattr(m, "observer") else m.get("observer", ""))
1264:                 m_orig = str(m.origin if hasattr(m, "origin") else m.get("origin", ""))
1265:                 m_rank = float(m.rank if hasattr(m, "rank") else m.get("rank", 0.0))
1266:                 m_snip = str(m.snippet if hasattr(m, "snippet") else m.get("snippet", ""))
1267:                 matches.append(
1268:                     SearchResultItem(
1269:                         id=str(m.id if hasattr(m, "id") else m.get("id", "")),
1270:                         kind=str(m.kind if hasattr(m, "kind") else m.get("kind", "")),
1271:                         ts=m_ts_val,
1272:                         observer=m_obs,
1273:                         origin=m_orig,
1274:                         payload=dict(payload) if isinstance(payload, Mapping) else {},
1275:                         rank=m_rank,
1276:                         snippet=m_snip,
1277:                     )
1278:                 )
1279:             return SearchResult(
1280:                 query=query,
1281:                 matches=matches,
1282:                 total_matches=len(matches),
1283:             )
1284:         except Exception as exc:
1285:             raise SdkError(f"full-text search failed on {target_path}: {exc}") from exc
1286: 
1287:     canonical = info.canonical_path or target_path
1288:     index_path = info.index_path or canonical
1289:     reader, _ = _ensure_reader(canonical, index_path)
1290:     try:
1291:         if reader.fts_generation() is None:
1292:             return SearchResult(
1293:                 query=query,
1294:                 matches=[],
1295:                 total_matches=0,
1296:             )
1297: 
1298:         raw_matches = reader.search_facts(query, kind=kind, limit=limit)
1299:         for raw_match in raw_matches:
1300:             m: Any = raw_match
1301:             payload = m.payload if hasattr(m, "payload") else m.get("payload", {})
1302:             if isinstance(payload, str):
1303:                 with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
1304:                     payload = json.loads(payload)
1305:             m_ts = m.ts if hasattr(m, "ts") else m.get("ts", 0.0)
1306:             m_ts_val = m_ts.timestamp() if hasattr(m_ts, "timestamp") else float(m_ts or 0.0)
1307:             m_obs = str(m.observer if hasattr(m, "observer") else m.get("observer", ""))
1308:             m_orig = str(m.origin if hasattr(m, "origin") else m.get("origin", ""))
1309:             m_rank = float(m.rank if hasattr(m, "rank") else m.get("rank", 0.0))
1310:             m_snip = str(m.snippet if hasattr(m, "snippet") else m.get("snippet", ""))
1311:             matches.append(
1312:                 SearchResultItem(
1313:                     id=str(m.id if hasattr(m, "id") else m.get("id", "")),
1314:                     kind=str(m.kind if hasattr(m, "kind") else m.get("kind", "")),
1315:                     ts=m_ts_val,
1316:                     observer=m_obs,
1317:                     origin=m_orig,
1318:                     payload=dict(payload) if isinstance(payload, Mapping) else {},
1319:                     rank=m_rank,
1320:                     snippet=m_snip,
1321:                 )
1322:             )
1323:         return SearchResult(
1324:             query=query,
1325:             matches=matches,
1326:             total_matches=len(matches),
1327:         )
1328:     except Exception as exc:
1329:         raise SdkError(f"full-text search failed on {target_path}: {exc}") from exc
1330:     finally:
1331:         reader.close()
1332: 
1333: 
1334: def resolve_entity(
1335:     target: Path | str,
1336:     kind: str,
1337:     key: str,
1338:     value: Any,
1339:     *,
1340:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
1341: ) -> EntityResolutionResult:
1342:     """Resolve an entity key to its latest fact ID.
1343: 
1344:     Parameters:
1345:         target: Path to .vertex, .jsonl, or .db artifact.
1346:         kind: Target kind name.
1347:         key: Primary key field name.
1348:         value: Entity key value.
1349:         registry: Optional Arrival backend registry used for descriptor opens.
1350: 
1351:     Returns:
1352:         A uniform entity-resolution result; ``found`` and ``fact_id`` describe
1353:         the match while ``address`` always preserves the queried top-level key.
1354:     """
1355:     def result(
1356:         fact_id: str | None,
1357:         *,
1358:         store: StoreDescriptorInfo | None,
1359:         basis: Any = None,
1360:         read_path: str = "legacy",
1361:     ) -> EntityResolutionResult:
1362:         return EntityResolutionResult(
1363:             found=fact_id is not None,
1364:             fact_id=fact_id,
1365:             address={"kind": kind, "key": key, "value": value},
1366:             read_path=read_path,
1367:             store=store,
1368:             basis=basis,
1369:         )
1370: 
1371:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
1372:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor(target)
1373:     if arrival is not None:
1374:         with _open_arrival_read(arrival, registry=registry) as (
1375:             locator,
1376:             descriptor,
1377:             opened,
1378:         ):
1379:             _arrival_declaration(locator, target_path, opened, include_user_facts=True)
1380:             facts = opened.snapshot.facts(
1381:                 FactRequest(kind=kind, limit=None, include_internal=False, order="newest")
1382:             ).items
1383:             matched = next(
1384:                 (
1385:                     fact for fact in facts
1386:                     if key in fact.payload
1387:                     and type(fact.payload[key]) is type(value)
1388:                     and fact.payload[key] == value
1389:                 ),
1390:                 None,
1391:             )
1392:             return result(
1393:                 None if matched is None else matched.id,
1394:                 store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
1395:                 basis=opened.basis, read_path="arrival",
1396:             )
1397:     _refuse_arrival_aggregate_members(target)
1398:     info = resolve_target(target)
1399: 
1400:     if info.target_type == "vertex":
1401:         decl_ast, _ = load_declaration_status(target_path)
1402:         is_aggregate = decl_ast is not None and (
1403:             decl_ast.combine is not None or decl_ast.discover is not None
1404:         )
1405:         if is_aggregate:
1406:             # Entity resolution must agree with the fold, so it resolves the
1407:             # SAME ordering _combined_read does, off the same member count.
1408:             #
1409:             # Arrival() (ONE member): that member's arrival axis IS the
1410:             # aggregate's, so the fold replays on it and resolution must too —
1411:             # a backdated re-assertion wins the fold, and the lens walk below
1412:             # would hand back the row it superseded.
1413:             #
1414:             # ByKey (TWO OR MORE): no cross-store arrival axis exists, so the
1415:             # combined read is an event-time lens projection and every other
1416:             # combined surface reads through that same lens — the lens walk
1417:             # below is the coherent answer.
1418:             store_paths = _resolve_stores(decl_ast, target_path)
1419:             fold_ordering = resolve_ordering(None, single_store=len(store_paths) == 1)
1420:             if isinstance(fold_ordering, Arrival) and store_paths[0].exists():
1421:                 member_reader = StoreReader(store_paths[0])
1422:                 try:
1423:                     fact_id = member_reader.resolve_entity_id(kind, key, value)
1424:                     return result(fact_id, store=None)
1425:                 finally:
1426:                     member_reader.close()
1427: 
1428:             all_facts = vertex_facts(target_path, since_ts=0.0, until_ts=float("inf"), kind=kind)
1429:             for f in reversed(all_facts):
1430:                 p = f.get("payload", {})
1431:                 if str(p.get(key)) == str(value):
1432:                     fact_id = f.get("id")
1433:                     return result(fact_id, store=None)
1434:             return result(None, store=None)
1435: 
1436:         if info.canonical_path is None or not info.canonical_path.exists():
1437:             return result(None, store=None)
1438: 
1439:     canonical = info.canonical_path or target_path
1440:     index_path = info.index_path or canonical
1441:     reader, _ = _ensure_reader(canonical, index_path)
1442:     try:
1443:         fact_id = reader.resolve_entity_id(kind, key, value)
1444:         return result(fact_id, store=None)
1445:     finally:
1446:         reader.close()
1447: 
1448: 
1449: def read_timeline(
1450:     target: Path | str,
1451:     *,
1452:     start_ts: float | None = None,
1453:     end_ts: float | None = None,
1454:     limit: int = 100,
1455:     order: str = "oldest",
1456:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
1457: ) -> TimelineResult:
1458:     """Read an interleaved, chronological stream of both facts and sealed ticks.
1459: 
1460:     Parameters:
1461:         target: Path to .vertex, .jsonl, or .db artifact.
1462:         start_ts: Optional lower timestamp bound (inclusive).
1463:         end_ts: Optional upper timestamp bound (inclusive).
1464:         limit: Maximum number of events to return.
1465:         order: Sort order ('oldest' for chronological, 'newest' for reverse chronological).
1466:         registry: Optional Arrival backend registry used for root and member opens.
1467: 
1468:     Ordering is by event time (``ts``) — facts and ticks are interleaved on the
1469:     only axis they share, so the timeline is an event-time read lens, not the
1470:     store's fold order. A backdated fact appears where its ``ts`` puts it here
1471:     and folds last; ``read_facts`` is the receipt-ordered view.
1472: 
1473:     Returns:
1474:         TimelineResult containing merged events with honest total counts and truncation markers.
1475:     """
1476:     if order not in ("oldest", "newest"):
1477:         raise SdkValueError(f"invalid order '{order}': expected 'oldest' or 'newest'")
1478: 
1479:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
1480:     if has_local_descriptor_aggregate(target_path):
1481:         return _aggregate_timeline(
1482:             target_path,
1483:             start_ts=start_ts,
1484:             end_ts=end_ts,
1485:             limit=limit,
1486:             order=order,
1487:             registry=registry,
1488:         )
1489:     arrival = _aggregate_aware_arrival_descriptor(target_path)
1490:     if arrival is not None:
1491:         since = start_ts if start_ts is not None else 0.0
1492:         until = end_ts if end_ts is not None else float("inf")
1493:         with _open_arrival_read(arrival, registry=registry) as (
1494:             locator_ast,
1495:             descriptor,
1496:             opened,
1497:         ):
1498:             effective_ast, _status, _facts, _lineage = _arrival_declaration(
1499:                 locator_ast, target_path, opened, allow_aggregate=True
1500:             )
1501:             if effective_ast.combine is not None or effective_ast.discover is not None:
1502:                 with open_aggregate_read(
1503:                     target_path, registry=registry, opened_root=opened
1504:                 ) as aggregate:
1505:                     return _aggregate_timeline_from(
1506:                         aggregate,
1507:                         start_ts=start_ts,
1508:                         end_ts=end_ts,
1509:                         limit=limit,
1510:                         order=order,
1511:                     )
1512:             facts = opened.snapshot.facts(
1513:                 FactRequest(limit=None, include_internal=False, order="oldest")
1514:             ).items
1515:             ticks = opened.snapshot.ticks(TickRequest(since=since, until=until))
1516:             events: list[tuple[TimelineEvent, int, int]] = []
1517:             for fact in facts:
1518:                 if since <= fact.ts <= until:
1519:                     events.append(
1520:                         (
1521:                             TimelineEvent(
1522:                                 event_type="fact",
1523:                                 id=fact.id,
1524:                                 kind_or_name=fact.kind,
1525:                                 ts=fact.ts,
1526:                                 observer=fact.observer,
1527:                                 origin=fact.origin,
1528:                                 payload=dict(fact.payload),
1529:                             ),
1530:                             fact.arrival_ordinal,
1531:                             fact.arrival_seq,
1532:                         )
1533:                     )
1534:             for tick in ticks:
1535:                 events.append(
1536:                     (
1537:                         TimelineEvent(
1538:                             event_type="tick",
1539:                             id=tick.id,
1540:                             kind_or_name=tick.name,
1541:                             ts=tick.ts,
1542:                             origin=tick.origin,
1543:                             payload=dict(tick.payload),
1544:                         ),
1545:                         tick.arrival_ordinal,
1546:                         tick.arrival_seq,
1547:                     )
1548:                 )
1549:             events.sort(
1550:                 key=lambda item: (item[0].ts, item[1], item[2], item[0].id),
1551:                 reverse=(order == "newest"),
1552:             )
1553:             total_events = len(events)
1554:             capped = events[:limit]
1555:             return TimelineResult(
1556:                 read_path="arrival",
1557:                 basis=opened.basis,
1558:                 store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
1559:                 events=[event for event, _ordinal, _seq in capped],
1560:                 start_ts=start_ts,
1561:                 end_ts=end_ts,
1562:                 total_events=total_events,
1563:                 truncated=total_events > len(capped),
1564:                 order=order,
1565:             )
1566: 
1567:     _refuse_arrival_aggregate_members(target_path)
1568:     info = resolve_target(target)
1569:     events: list[TimelineEvent] = []
1570: 
1571:     since = start_ts if start_ts is not None else 0.0
1572:     until = end_ts if end_ts is not None else float("inf")
1573: 
1574:     if info.target_type == "vertex":
1575:         decl_ast, _ = load_declaration_status(target_path)
1576:         is_aggregate = decl_ast is not None and (
1577:             decl_ast.combine is not None or decl_ast.discover is not None
1578:         )
1579: 
1580:         if is_aggregate or (info.canonical_path is not None and info.canonical_path.exists()):
1581:             raw_facts = vertex_facts(target_path, since_ts=since, until_ts=until)
1582:             for f in raw_facts:
1583:                 f_ts = (
1584:                     f["ts"].timestamp()
1585:                     if hasattr(f.get("ts"), "timestamp")
1586:                     else float(f.get("ts", 0.0))
1587:                 )
1588:                 events.append(
1589:                     TimelineEvent(
1590:                         event_type="fact",
1591:                         id=f.get("id", ""),
1592:                         kind_or_name=f.get("kind", ""),
1593:                         ts=f_ts,
1594:                         observer=f.get("observer", ""),
1595:                         origin=f.get("origin", ""),
1596:                         payload=dict(f.get("payload", {})),
1597:                     )
1598:                 )
1599: 
1600:             raw_ticks = vertex_ticks(target_path, since, until)
1601:             for raw_tick in raw_ticks:
1602:                 t: Any = raw_tick
1603:                 t_ts = (
1604:                     t.ts.timestamp()
1605:                     if hasattr(t, "ts") and hasattr(t.ts, "timestamp")
1606:                     else float(getattr(t, "ts", 0.0) if hasattr(t, "ts") else t.get("ts", 0.0))
1607:                 )
1608:                 t_id = getattr(t, "tick_id", getattr(t, "id", ""))
1609:                 t_name = getattr(t, "name", "")
1610:                 events.append(
1611:                     TimelineEvent(
1612:                         event_type="tick",
1613:                         id=t_id,
1614:                         kind_or_name=t_name,
1615:                         ts=t_ts,
1616:                         observer="",
1617:                         origin="",
1618:                         payload=dict(t.payload)
1619:                         if hasattr(t, "payload") and t.payload
1620:                         else dict(t.get("payload", {})),
1621:                     )
1622:                 )
1623: 
1624:             # Event-time lens: ts is the only axis facts and ticks share.
1625:             events.sort(key=lambda e: e.ts, reverse=(order == "newest"))
1626:             total_events = len(events)
1627:             capped = events[:limit]
1628:             truncated = total_events > len(capped)
1629: 
1630:             return TimelineResult(
1631:                 events=capped,
1632:                 start_ts=start_ts,
1633:                 end_ts=end_ts,
1634:                 total_events=total_events,
1635:                 truncated=truncated,
1636:                 order=order,
1637:             )
1638: 
1639:         return TimelineResult(
1640:             events=[],
1641:             start_ts=start_ts,
1642:             end_ts=end_ts,
1643:             total_events=0,
1644:             truncated=False,
1645:             order=order,
1646:         )
1647: 
1648:     canonical = info.canonical_path or target_path
1649:     index_path = info.index_path or canonical
1650:     reader, _ = _ensure_reader(canonical, index_path)
1651:     try:
1652:         raw_facts = reader.facts_between(since_ts=since, until_ts=until)
1653:         ticks = reader.ticks_between(since, until)
1654: 
1655:         for f in raw_facts:
1656:             f_ts = (
1657:                 f["ts"].timestamp()
1658:                 if hasattr(f.get("ts"), "timestamp")
1659:                 else float(f.get("ts", 0.0))
1660:             )
1661:             events.append(
1662:                 TimelineEvent(
1663:                     event_type="fact",
1664:                     id=f.get("id", ""),
1665:                     kind_or_name=f.get("kind", ""),
1666:                     ts=f_ts,
1667:                     observer=f.get("observer", ""),
1668:                     origin=f.get("origin", ""),
1669:                     payload=dict(f.get("payload", {})),
1670:                 )
1671:             )
1672: 
1673:         for raw_tick in ticks:
1674:             t: Any = raw_tick
1675:             t_ts = (
1676:                 t.ts.timestamp()
1677:                 if hasattr(t, "ts") and hasattr(t.ts, "timestamp")
1678:                 else float(getattr(t, "ts", 0.0) if hasattr(t, "ts") else t.get("ts", 0.0))
1679:             )
1680:             t_id = getattr(t, "tick_id", getattr(t, "id", ""))
1681:             t_name = getattr(t, "name", "")
1682:             events.append(
1683:                 TimelineEvent(
1684:                     event_type="tick",
1685:                     id=t_id,
1686:                     kind_or_name=t_name,
1687:                     ts=t_ts,
1688:                     observer="",
1689:                     origin="",
1690:                     payload=dict(t.payload)
1691:                     if hasattr(t, "payload") and t.payload
1692:                     else dict(t.get("payload", {})),
1693:                 )
1694:             )
1695: 
1696:         # Event-time lens: ts is the only axis facts and ticks share.
1697:         events.sort(key=lambda e: e.ts, reverse=(order == "newest"))
1698:         total_events = len(events)
1699:         capped = events[:limit]
1700:         truncated = total_events > len(capped)
1701: 
1702:         return TimelineResult(
1703:             events=capped,
1704:             start_ts=start_ts,
1705:             end_ts=end_ts,
1706:             total_events=total_events,
1707:             truncated=truncated,
1708:             order=order,
1709:         )
1710:     finally:
1711:         reader.close()
1712: 
1713: 
1714: def _search_coverage_as_dict(coverage: Any | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
1715:     if coverage is None:
1716:         return None
1717:     through = getattr(coverage, "through", None)
1718:     if through is None:
1719:         return None
1720:     return {
1721:         "through": {
1722:             "lineage": through.lineage,
1723:             "ordinal": through.ordinal,
1724:             "record_hash": through.record_hash,
1725:         },
1726:         "fields_hash": coverage.fields_hash,
1727:         "schema_version": coverage.schema_version,
1728:     }
1729: 
1730: 
1731: def sync_search_index(
1732:     target: Path | str,
1733:     *,
1734:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
1735: ) -> SearchIndexResult:
1736:     """Explicitly build exact-prefix Arrival FTS coverage.
1737: 
1738:     The declaration/spec capture is separate from the serialized adapter build.
1739:     The engine receives the first capture's exact head, so an append or a later
1740:     declaration cannot make the second coordinator open index a different corpus.
1741:     This operation is not projection synchronization and reads never call it.
1742:     """
1743:     from engine.arrival_search import sync_search_index as sync_engine_search_index
1744: 
1745:     from .errors import normalize_exception
1746: 
1747:     resolved = _arrival_descriptor(target)
1748:     if resolved is None:
1749:         raise TargetUnsupported(
1750:             "sync_search_index requires an explicit Arrival descriptor"
1751:         )
1752:     try:
1753:         with _open_arrival_read(resolved, registry=registry) as (
1754:             locator_ast,
1755:             descriptor,
1756:             opened,
1757:         ):
1758:             spec = _arrival_search_spec(locator_ast, Path(target).resolve(), opened)
1759:             basis = opened.basis
1760:         result = sync_engine_search_index(
1761:             registry if registry is not None else BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends(),
1762:             descriptor,
1763:             spec,
1764:             through=basis.captured_head,
1765:         )
1766:         return SearchIndexResult(
1767:             store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
1768:             basis=basis,
1769:             coordinator_captured_head=result.captured_head,
1770:             target=result.target,
1771:             coverage_before=_search_coverage_as_dict(result.coverage_before),
1772:             coverage_after=_search_coverage_as_dict(result.coverage_after),
1773:             changed=result.changed,
1774:         )
1775:     except BaseException as exc:
1776:         normalized = normalize_exception(exc)
1777:         if normalized is exc:
1778:             raise
1779:         raise normalized from exc
1780: 
1781: 
1782: def sync_target(
1783:     target: Path | str,
1784:     *,
1785:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
1786: ) -> SyncResult:
1787:     """Explicitly synchronize a target's derived query projection.
1788: 
1789:     Parameters:
1790:         target: Path to target .vertex, .jsonl, or .db artifact.
1791: 
1792:     Returns:
1793:         A basis-bearing ``SyncResult``. Descriptor-first Arrival targets use
1794:         attested bounded catch-up; legacy targets retain their existing
1795:         reindex/preflight behavior and carry no Arrival heads.
1796:     """
1797:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
1798:     t0 = time.perf_counter()
1799:     arrival = _arrival_descriptor(target_path)
1800:     if arrival is not None:
1801:         from engine.arrival_maintenance import sync_projection
1802: 
1803:         from .errors import normalize_exception
1804: 
1805:         _path, _ast, descriptor = arrival
1806:         try:
1807:             result = sync_projection(
1808:                 registry if registry is not None else BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends(),
1809:                 descriptor,
1810:             )
1811:         except BaseException as exc:
1812:             normalized = normalize_exception(exc)
1813:             if normalized is exc:
1814:                 raise
1815:             raise normalized from exc
1816:         t1 = time.perf_counter()
1817:         return SyncResult(
1818:             read_path="arrival",
1819:             store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
1820:             target_path=str(target_path),
1821:             status="caught-up" if result.changed else "current",
1822:             captured_head=result.captured_head,
1823:             target=result.target,
1824:             projected_before=result.projected_before,
1825:             projected_after=result.projected_after,
1826:             view_generation=result.view_generation,
1827:             changed=result.changed,
1828:             rebuilt=result.rebuilt,
1829:             indexed_facts=None,
1830:             agreement=result.projected_after.ordinal >= result.target.ordinal,
1831:             duration_ms=(t1 - t0) * 1000.0,
1832:         )
1833: 
1834:     _refuse_arrival_aggregate_members(target_path)
1835:     info = resolve_target(target)
1836: 
1837:     if info.target_type == "vertex":
1838:         res = vertex_reindex(target_path)
1839:         # Same agreement check as the bare-store branch below — the old
1840:         # hardcoded agreement=True silently hid canonical/index drift.
1841:         # Aggregates carry no canonical/index pair, so there the claim is
1842:         # vacuously true.
1843:         agreed = True
1844:         if info.canonical_path is not None and info.canonical_path.exists():
1845:             preflight = read_preflight(info.canonical_path, mode=PreflightMode.RECOVER_THEN_OPEN)
1846:             if preflight.store is not None:
1847:                 preflight.store.close()
1848:             agreed = preflight.agreed
1849:         t1 = time.perf_counter()
1850:         return SyncResult(
1851:             target_path=str(target_path),
1852:             status="synced" if res.get("reindexed", False) else "unindexed",
1853:             indexed_facts=res.get("facts_indexed", 0),
1854:             agreement=agreed,
1855:             duration_ms=(t1 - t0) * 1000.0,
1856:         )
1857: 
1858:     # Bare store (.jsonl or .db)
1859:     canonical = info.canonical_path or target_path
1860:     preflight = read_preflight(canonical, mode=PreflightMode.RECOVER_THEN_OPEN)
1861:     if preflight.store is not None:
1862:         preflight.store.close()
1863: 
1864:     idx_path = info.index_path or canonical
1865:     fact_count = 0
1866:     if idx_path.exists():
1867:         reader = StoreReader(idx_path)
1868:         try:
1869:             total_val: Any = reader.fact_total
1870:             fact_count = total_val() if callable(total_val) else total_val
1871:         finally:
1872:             reader.close()
1873: 
1874:     t1 = time.perf_counter()
1875:     return SyncResult(
1876:         target_path=str(target_path),
1877:         status="synced",
1878:         indexed_facts=fact_count,
1879:         agreement=preflight.agreed,
1880:         duration_ms=(t1 - t0) * 1000.0,
1881:     )

===== libs/sdk/src/sdk/errors.py =====
1: """Normalize engine boundary failures for SDK process callers.
2: 
3: Engine deliberately keeps backend and witness refusal families separate.  The
4: SDK is the process boundary, so it translates those families into stable SDK
5: errors while retaining the identity evidence an operator needs to reconcile a
6: refusal or an outcome that may already have committed.
7: """
8: 
9: from __future__ import annotations
10: 
11: from collections.abc import Mapping
12: from pathlib import PurePath
13: from typing import Any
14: 
15: from engine.admission import AdmissionError
16: from engine.arrival import ArrivalError
17: from engine.arrival_contract import ContractRefusal
18: from engine.arrival_declarations import (
19:     DeclarationCommittedIncomplete,
20:     DeclarationOutcomeUnknown,
21:     DeclarationStale,
22:     DeclarationUnwitnessed,
23: )
24: from engine.arrival_head_attestation import AttestationRefusal
25: from engine.arrival_head_seam import NotWitnessed
26: from engine.arrival_initialization import (
27:     InitializationCommittedIncomplete,
28:     InitializationOutcomeUnknown,
29:     InitializationUnwitnessed,
30: )
31: from engine.arrival_maintenance import ProjectionSyncError
32: from engine.arrival_restore import RestoreForwardIncomplete, RestoreForwardUnknown
33: from engine.arrival_search import SearchIndexSyncError
34: from engine.credentials import CredentialBindingRefused
35: from engine.runtime_write import (
36:     BatchPostCommitProjectionFailed,
37:     BatchWriteCommitUnknown,
38:     BatchWritePreparationRefused,
39:     PostCommitProjectionFailed,
40:     RuntimeWriteRefused,
41:     WriteCommitUnknown,

44: from .types import AdmissionFailed, SdkError
