46: __all__ = [
47:     "ArrivalRefusal",
48:     "AdmissionRefusal",
49:     "CommittedOutcome",
50:     "CommittedProjectionFailed",
51:     "CommittedIncomplete",
52:     "CommittedUnwitnessed",
53:     "CommittedOutcomeUnknown",
54:     "ProjectionOutcomeUnknown",
55:     "normalize_exception",
56: ]
57: 
58: 
59: def _json_value(value: Any) -> Any:
60:     if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
61:         return value
62:     if isinstance(value, PurePath):
63:         return str(value)
64:     # Identity diagnostics must never stringify arbitrary record/payload objects.
65:     return None
66: 
67: 
68: def _head_dict(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
69:     fields = ("lineage", "ordinal", "record_hash")
70:     if not all(hasattr(value, field) for field in fields):
71:         return None
72:     return {field: _json_value(getattr(value, field)) for field in fields}
73: 
74: 
75: def _coverage_dict(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
76:     """Serialize explicit search-index coverage without backend row bodies."""
77:     through = _head_dict(getattr(value, "through", None))
78:     fields_hash = getattr(value, "fields_hash", None)
79:     schema_version = getattr(value, "schema_version", None)
80:     if through is None or not isinstance(fields_hash, str) or not isinstance(schema_version, str):
81:         return None
82:     return {
83:         "through": through,
84:         "fields_hash": fields_hash,
85:         "schema_version": schema_version,
86:     }
87: 
88: 
89: def _identity_details(exc: BaseException) -> dict[str, Any]:
90:     """Copy stable coordinates without serializing mutable record payloads."""
91:     details: dict[str, Any] = {}
92:     for name in (
93:         "fact_id",
94:         "tick_id",
95:         "ordinal",
96:         "observer",
97:         "kind",
98:         "vertex",
99:         "item_index",
100:         "source_type",
101:         "lineage",
102:         "intent_path",
103:         "phase",
104:         "fields_hash",
105:         "view_generation",
106:         "tier_index",
107:     ):
108:         value = getattr(exc, name, None)
109:         if value is not None:
110:             coordinate = _json_value(value)
111:             if coordinate is not None:
112:                 details[name] = coordinate
113:     for name in ("fact_ids", "tick_ids"):
114:         value = getattr(exc, name, None)
115:         if value is not None:
116:             details[name] = [_json_value(item) for item in value]
117:     items = getattr(exc, "items", None)
118:     if items is not None:
119:         details["items"] = [
120:             {
121:                 "fact_id": _json_value(item.fact_id),
122:                 "tick_id": _json_value(item.tick_id),
123:                 "tick_name": _json_value(item.tick_name),
124:                 "already_present": bool(item.already_present),
125:             }
126:             for item in items
127:         ]
128:     for name in (
129:         "head",
130:         "captured_head",
131:         "presented",
132:         "before",
133:         "after",
134:         "target",
135:         "projected_before",
136:         "observed_after",
137:         "projected_head",
138:     ):
139:         value = getattr(exc, name, None)
140:         if value is None:
141:             continue
142:         head = _head_dict(value)
143:         if head is not None:
144:             details[name] = head
145:     for name in ("coverage_before", "coverage_after", "observed_after"):
146:         value = getattr(exc, name, None)
147:         coverage = _coverage_dict(value)
148:         if coverage is not None:
149:             details[name] = coverage
150:     # Preserve an explicitly unavailable coordinate without claiming a head or
151:     # coverage. Missing attributes on older wrappers still remain omitted.
152:     for name in (
153:         "captured_head", "projected_head", "projected_before", "observed_after",
154:         "coverage_before", "coverage_after",
155:     ):
156:         if hasattr(exc, name) and getattr(exc, name) is None:
157:             details[name] = None
158:     commit = getattr(exc, "commit", None)
159:     if commit is not None:
160:         before = _head_dict(getattr(commit, "before", None))
161:         after = _head_dict(getattr(commit, "after", None))
162:         commit_details: dict[str, Any] = {}
163:         if before is not None:
164:             commit_details["before"] = before
165:         if after is not None:
166:             commit_details["after"] = after
167:         if commit_details:
168:             details["commit"] = commit_details
169:     return details
170: 
171: 
172: def _credential_binding_details(exc: BaseException) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
173:     """Extract public mapped-binding evidence from an explicit causal chain."""
174:     seen: set[int] = set()
175:     current: BaseException | None = exc
176:     for _ in range(3):
177:         if current is None or id(current) in seen:
178:             return None
179:         seen.add(id(current))
180:         if isinstance(current, CredentialBindingRefused):
181:             result: dict[str, Any] = {"reason": current.reason}
182:             request = current.request
183:             if request is not None:
184:                 result["request"] = {
185:                     "namespace": request.namespace,
186:                     "observer": request.observer,
187:                     "domain": request.domain.value,
188:                     "purpose": request.purpose.value,
189:                 }
190:             if current.captured_head is not None:
191:                 head = _head_dict(current.captured_head)
192:                 if head is not None:
193:                     result["captured_head"] = head
194:             if current.use_position is not None:
195:                 result["use_position"] = current.use_position
196:             binding = current.evidence
197:             if binding is not None:
198:                 # A malformed provider response can be the reason for refusal.
199:                 # Do not serialize arbitrary objects retained as that evidence.
200:                 result["binding"] = {
201:                     name: value if isinstance(value, str) else None
202:                     for name in ("key_ref", "algorithm", "public_key", "provenance")
203:                     for value in (getattr(binding, name, None),)
204:                 }
205:             return result
206:         current = _causal_exception(current)
207:     return None
208: 
209: 
210: def _causal_exception(exc: BaseException) -> BaseException | None:
211:     cause = getattr(exc, "cause", None)
212:     if isinstance(cause, BaseException):
213:         return cause
214:     return exc.__cause__ if isinstance(exc.__cause__, BaseException) else None
215: 
216: 
217: def _cause_details(exc: BaseException) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
218:     """Two explicit causal nodes, never implicit context or record bodies."""
219:     seen = {id(exc)}
220:     result: dict[str, Any] | None = None
221:     parent: dict[str, Any] | None = None
222:     for _ in range(2):
223:         cause = _causal_exception(exc)
224:         if cause is None or id(cause) in seen:
225:             break
226:         seen.add(id(cause))
227:         try:
228:             message = str(cause)
229:         except Exception:
230:             message = "<message unavailable>"
231:         node: dict[str, Any] = {
232:             "type": type(cause).__name__,
233:             "message": message[:512],
234:             "details": _identity_details(cause),
235:         }
236:         if len(message) > 512:
237:             node["truncated"] = True
238:         if parent is None:
239:             result = node
240:         else:
241:             parent["cause"] = node
242:         parent = node
243:         exc = cause
244:     return result
245: 
246: 
247: def _evidence_details(exc: BaseException, details: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
248:     """Expose coordinator proofs without deriving effects from error classes."""
249:     evidence: dict[str, Any] = {}
250:     phase = getattr(exc, "coordinator_phase", None)
251:     if isinstance(phase, str):
252:         evidence["phase"] = phase
253:     effects = getattr(exc, "effects", None)
254:     if isinstance(effects, Mapping):
255:         safe_effects = {}
256:         for resource in ("custody", "witness", "derived", "cache", "artifact", "dispatch"):
257:             effect = effects.get(resource)
258:             if not isinstance(effect, Mapping):
259:                 continue
260:             state = effect.get("state")
261:             attempt = effect.get("attempt")
262:             if state not in (
263:                 "not-attempted", "known-none", "committed", "unknown", "completed", "incomplete"
264:             ):
265:                 continue
266:             if attempt == "not-entered" and state != "not-attempted":
267:                 continue
268:             if attempt == "entered" and state == "not-attempted":
269:                 continue
270:             safe_effect = {"state": state}
271:             if attempt in ("not-entered", "entered"):
272:                 safe_effect["attempt"] = attempt
273:             safe_effects[resource] = safe_effect
274:         if safe_effects:
275:             evidence["effects"] = safe_effects
276:     cause = _cause_details(exc)
277:     if cause is not None:
278:         evidence["cause"] = cause
279:     if not evidence:
280:         return None
281:     evidence["schema"] = "loops.sdk/evidence/v1"
282:     basis_fields = (
283:         "head", "captured_head", "presented", "target", "projected_before", "projected_head",
284:         "observed_after", "coverage_before", "coverage_after", "fields_hash", "view_generation",
285:     )
286:     identity_fields = (
287:         "fact_id", "tick_id", "fact_ids", "tick_ids", "items", "item_index", "tier_index",
288:         "ordinal", "observer", "kind", "vertex", "lineage", "commit",
289:     )
290:     basis = {name: details[name] for name in basis_fields if name in details}
291:     identities = {name: details[name] for name in identity_fields if name in details}
292:     if basis:
293:         evidence["basis"] = basis
294:     if identities:
295:         evidence["identities"] = identities
296:     return evidence
297: 
298: 
299: def _context_detail(name: str, value: Any) -> Any:
300:     """Serialize only stable identity fields supplied by an SDK operation."""
301:     if name in {"fact_ids", "tick_ids"}:
302:         return [_json_value(item) for item in value]
303:     if name == "items":
304:         return [
305:             {
306:                 "fact_id": _json_value(item.fact_id),
307:                 "tick_id": _json_value(item.tick_id),
308:                 "tick_name": _json_value(item.tick_name),
309:                 "already_present": bool(item.already_present),
310:             }
311:             for item in value
312:         ]
313:     head = _head_dict(value)
314:     return head if head is not None else _json_value(value)
315:

328:             "type": type(self).__name__,
329:             "message": str(self),

331:             "outcome": "refused",

415:         super().__init__(message)
416:         self.source_type = source_type
417:         self.details = dict(details)

420:         return {
421:             "schema": "loops.sdk/error/v1",
422:             "type": type(self).__name__,
423:             "message": str(self),
424:             "source_type": self.source_type,
425:             "outcome": self.outcome,
426:             "details": dict(self.details),

429: 
430: def normalize_exception(
431:     exc: BaseException, *, context: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None
432: ) -> BaseException:
433:     """Return a stable SDK exception for engine boundary failures.
434: 
435:     Existing SDK errors are returned unchanged.  Classification is by the
436:     engine's public exception types, never by message text.
437:     """
438:     if not isinstance(exc, Exception) or isinstance(exc, SdkError):
439:         return exc
440:     details = _identity_details(exc)
441:     context_fields = {
442:         "captured_head",
443:         "fact_id",
444:         "tick_id",
445:         "fact_ids",
446:         "tick_ids",
447:         "items",
448:         "item_index",
449:     }
450:     for name, value in (context or {}).items():
451:         if name in details or name not in context_fields:
452:             continue
453:         coordinate = _context_detail(name, value)
454:         if coordinate is not None or value is None:
455:             details[name] = coordinate
456:     details["source_type"] = type(exc).__name__
457:     credential = _credential_binding_details(exc)
458:     if credential is not None:
459:         details["credential_binding"] = credential
460:     evidence = _evidence_details(exc, details)
461:     if evidence is not None:
462:         details["evidence"] = evidence
463:     if isinstance(exc, (BatchWriteCommitUnknown, WriteCommitUnknown)):
464:         return CommittedOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
465:     if isinstance(exc, InitializationOutcomeUnknown):
466:         return CommittedOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
467:     if isinstance(exc, (DeclarationOutcomeUnknown, RestoreForwardUnknown)):
468:         return CommittedOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
469:     if isinstance(exc, InitializationUnwitnessed):
470:         return CommittedUnwitnessed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
471:     if isinstance(exc, DeclarationUnwitnessed):
472:         return CommittedUnwitnessed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
473:     if isinstance(exc, InitializationCommittedIncomplete):
474:         return CommittedIncomplete(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
475:     if isinstance(exc, (DeclarationCommittedIncomplete, RestoreForwardIncomplete)):
476:         return CommittedIncomplete(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
477:     if isinstance(exc, NotWitnessed):
478:         return CommittedUnwitnessed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
479:     if isinstance(exc, DeclarationStale):
480:         return ArrivalRefusal(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
481:     if isinstance(exc, BatchPostCommitProjectionFailed):
482:         return CommittedProjectionFailed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
483:     if isinstance(exc, PostCommitProjectionFailed):
484:         return CommittedProjectionFailed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
485:     if isinstance(exc, ProjectionSyncError):
486:         return ProjectionOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
487:     if isinstance(exc, SearchIndexSyncError):
488:         return ProjectionOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
489:     if isinstance(exc, BatchWritePreparationRefused):
490:         fact = exc.item.fact
491:         if exc.item.fact_id is not None:
492:             details.setdefault("fact_id", exc.item.fact_id)
493:         details.setdefault("observer", fact.observer)
494:         details.setdefault("kind", fact.kind)
495:         details.setdefault("vertex", exc.effective_declaration.name)
496:         if exc.admission_refused:
497:             return AdmissionRefusal(
498:                 str(exc),
499:                 source_type=type(exc).__name__,
500:                 details=details,
501:                 observer=fact.observer,
502:                 kind=fact.kind,
503:                 vertex=exc.effective_declaration.name,
504:             )
505:         return ArrivalRefusal(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
506:     if isinstance(exc, AdmissionError):
507:         return AdmissionRefusal(
508:             str(exc),
509:             source_type=type(exc).__name__,
510:             details=details,
511:             observer=getattr(exc, "observer", None),
512:             kind=getattr(exc, "kind", None),
513:             vertex=getattr(exc, "vertex", None),
514:         )
515:     if isinstance(exc, CredentialBindingRefused):
516:         return ArrivalRefusal(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
517:     if isinstance(
518:         exc,
519:         (
520:             AttestationRefusal,
521:             ContractRefusal,
522:             ArrivalError,
523:             RuntimeWriteRefused,
524:         ),
525:     ):
526:         return ArrivalRefusal(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
527:     return exc

===== libs/engine/src/engine/arrival_registry.py =====
1: """arrival_registry — which adapter opens which artifact.
2: 
3: The declaration and registry halves remain deliberately separable:
4: 
5: * :func:`descriptor_for` reads a parsed ``.vertex`` and answers what
6:   :class:`engine.arrival_contract.StoreDescriptor` — if any — describes its
7:   store. It can answer about a ``backend="duckdb"`` vertex on a host with no
8:   DuckDB adapter installed, which is the point of separating naming from
9:   opening. Only the built-in file location is path-interpreted here; every
10:   other adapter receives the declared string unchanged.
11: 
12:   **"No adapter import" is an IMPORT-TIME claim, scoped deliberately**
13:   (``finding:s2wp4-gate-f1-descriptor-for-calltime-import``): importing this
14:   module drags in neither ``sqlite3`` nor the adapter, which is the property
15:   ``test_importing_the_registry_does_not_drag_the_adapter_in`` pins. CALLING
16:   :func:`descriptor_for` reaches :mod:`engine.residence` only for the named
17:   file adapter, whose relative paths are resolved against the vertex. The
18:   adapter surface stays out either way; nothing here constructs a ledger.
19: * :class:`BackendRegistry` maps a backend NAME to an opener. This is the half
20:   that can fail with :class:`~engine.arrival_contract.UnknownBackend`, because
21:   this is the half that knows what is registered.
22: 
23: The registry is not a singleton and does not register anything at import.
24: Construct one with :meth:`BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends`. A
25: process-wide instance remains a consumer decision.
26: """
27: 
28: from __future__ import annotations
29: 
30: import contextlib
31: from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator, Mapping, Sequence
32: from pathlib import Path
33: from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
34: 
35: from .arrival_binding import (
36:     BindingIdentity,
37:     BindingProbeError,
38:     BindingProvider,
39:     file_binding,
40:     is_opaque_key_for,
41:     opaque_binding,
42: )
43: from .arrival_contract import (
44:     ArrivalLedger,
45:     ArrivalQuery,
46:     Capabilities,
47:     Commit,
48:     ExportedPrefix,
49:     Head,
50:     NotAuthority,
51:     NotSupported,
52:     Open,
53:     Profile,
54:     RecordDraft,
55:     StoreDescriptor,
56:     UnknownBackend,
57:     VerifyScope,
58:     Watermark,
59: )
60: from .arrival_head_seam import AttestedLedger, BindingProbeUnanswered
61: 
62: if TYPE_CHECKING:  # pragma: no cover - typing only
63:     from lang.ast import VertexFile
64: 
65:     from .arrival_maintenance import MaintenanceOpener, ProjectionMaintenance
66:     from .arrival_restore import RestoreForwardResult
67:     from .arrival_search import SearchIndexMaintenance, SearchMaintenanceOpener
68: 
69: __all__ = ["BackendOpener", "BackendRegistry", "descriptor_for"]
70: 
71: 
72: #: What a registered adapter must be: given a descriptor, hand back the
73: #: custody half and the reads half. The pair is the contract's own §03 split,
74: #: so an adapter cannot register a single object that does both.
75: BackendOpener = Callable[[StoreDescriptor], "tuple[ArrivalLedger, ArrivalQuery]"]
76: 
77: 
78: def _file_binding(descriptor: StoreDescriptor) -> BindingIdentity:
79:     """The built-in file adapter's binding policy."""
80: 
81:     return file_binding(descriptor.location)
82: 
83: 
84: def _validate_binding(
85:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor, binding: BindingIdentity
86: ) -> None:
87:     """Reject a provider identity before its opener can create a handle."""
88: 
89:     if not isinstance(binding, BindingIdentity):
90:         raise TypeError(
91:             f"binding provider for backend {descriptor.backend!r} returned "
92:             f"{type(binding).__name__}, not BindingIdentity"
93:         )
94:     if binding.backend != descriptor.backend:
95:         raise ValueError(
96:             f"binding provider for backend {descriptor.backend!r} returned "
97:             f"identity for backend {binding.backend!r}"
98:         )
99:     if descriptor.backend == FILE_BACKEND:
100:         if not binding.filesystem_aliases or binding.key.startswith("backend:"):
101:             raise ValueError(
102:                 "the file backend binding must retain its canonical-path "
103:                 "identity and filesystem alias policy"
104:             )
105:         return
106:     if binding.filesystem_aliases or binding.filesystem_identity is not None:
107:         raise ValueError(
108:             f"non-file backend {descriptor.backend!r} supplied filesystem "
109:             "identity; non-file binding keys are opaque"
110:         )
111:     if not is_opaque_key_for(descriptor.backend, binding.key):
112:         raise ValueError(
113:             f"non-file backend {descriptor.backend!r} supplied an invalid "
114:             "binding key; use opaque_binding(backend, normalized_locator)"
115:         )
116: 
117: #: The explicit name the built-in file adapter registers under.
118: FILE_BACKEND = "file"
119: 
120: 
121: def descriptor_for(
122:     ast: VertexFile, vertex_path: Path | None = None
123: ) -> StoreDescriptor | None:
124:     """Which adapter opens this vertex's store, or None if no adapter does.
125: 
126:     Two answers, and only the first describes a ledger:
127: 
128:     **Explicit** — the vertex declared ``backend=``. The declared name wins,
129:     and there is NO cross-check against the location's suffix. A
130:     ``backend="file"`` on a ``.duckdb``-named path is legal, because under §02
131:     the suffix carries no meaning once the backend is declared; adding a "do
132:     they agree?" check would re-admit inference through the back door on the
133:     very change that removes it. An unregistered name is not this function's
134:     to refuse — see :meth:`BackendRegistry.open`.
135: 
136:     **No descriptor** — a bare legacy store, an aggregate (``combine``), or a
137:     loops-only vertex has no explicit adapter declaration. Suffix inference
138:     ends here: a ``.arrival`` suffix does not synthesize ``backend="file"``.
139: 
140:     ``lineage`` and ``role`` are declaration claims, not grants. The registry
141:     checks them against the presented genesis and backend capabilities, then
142:     keeps enforcing them on the returned custody handle.
143:     """
144:     if ast.store is None or ast.store_backend is None:
145:         return None
146: 
147:     declaration = ast.store_backend
148:     if declaration.name == FILE_BACKEND:
149:         # This is the built-in file adapter's interpretation of a location:
150:         # relative paths are relative to the declaring vertex, never cwd.
151:         from .residence import canonical_store_path
152: 
153:         location = str(canonical_store_path(ast.store, vertex_path))
154:     else:
155:         # No pathlib round-trip for another adapter. A DSN or service URL is
156:         # opaque, including repeated slashes and query text.
157:         if ast.store_location is None:
158:             raise ValueError(
159:                 f"non-file backend {declaration.name!r} has no opaque store "
160:                 "location; construct it by parsing a descriptor declaration"
161:             )
162:         location = ast.store_location
163: 
164:     role = None if declaration.role is None else Profile(declaration.role)
165:     return StoreDescriptor(
166:         backend=declaration.name,
167:         location=location,
168:         lineage=declaration.lineage,
169:         role=role,
170:     )
171: 
172: 
173: def _open_file_backend(
174:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
175: ) -> tuple[ArrivalLedger, ArrivalQuery]:
176:     """Open the ``.arrival`` log and the projection beside it.
177: 
178:     Imported lazily, inside the opener: ``import engine.arrival_registry``
179:     must not drag sqlite3 and the whole arrival codec behind it for a caller
180:     that only wants to NAME a descriptor. That is the same reason the contract
181:     module is separate from the adapter, held one level up.
182:     """
183:     from .arrival import ArrivalLog
184:     from .arrival_file_backend import FileLedger, FileQuery, file_projection_path
185: 
186:     location = Path(descriptor.location)
187:     return FileLedger(ArrivalLog(location)), FileQuery(file_projection_path(location))
188: 
189: 
190: def _open_file_search_maintenance(descriptor: StoreDescriptor):
191:     """Build the file adapter's explicit FTS authority lazily."""
192:     from .arrival_file_backend import FileSearchMaintenance, file_projection_path
193: 
194:     location = Path(descriptor.location)
195:     return FileSearchMaintenance(location, file_projection_path(location))
196: 
197: 
198: def _open_file_maintenance(descriptor: StoreDescriptor) -> ProjectionMaintenance:
199:     """Build the file adapter's derived-state authority lazily."""
200:     from .arrival_file_backend import FileProjectionMaintenance, file_projection_path
201: 
202:     location = Path(descriptor.location)
203:     return FileProjectionMaintenance(location, file_projection_path(location))
204: 
205: 
206: def _close_handle(handle: object) -> None:
207:     """Best-effort release of an adapter half after a refused registry open."""
208:     close = getattr(handle, "close", None)
209:     if callable(close):
210:         with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
211:             close()
212: 
213: 
214: class _DescriptorLedger:
215:     """Keep descriptor lineage and role claims true after open.
216: 
217:     Capability checks at open are insufficient on their own: the file adapter
218:     supports both Authority and Replica, so handing its full custody surface
219:     through a ``role=replica`` descriptor would let operator intent evaporate
220:     after one check. This wrapper gates every mutation and every operation
221:     carrying a lineage before delegating.
222: 
223:     ``role=None`` remains the transitional behavior for existing direct
224:     ``StoreDescriptor`` callers. It is not an Authority designation; the SDK
225:     integration cut must make its supported declaration explicit.
226:     """
227: 
228:     def __init__(self, ledger: ArrivalLedger, descriptor: StoreDescriptor) -> None:
229:         self._ledger = ledger
230:         self._lineage = descriptor.lineage
231:         self._role = descriptor.role
232: 
233:     def _require_role(self, operation: str, *allowed: Profile) -> None:
234:         if self._role is not None and self._role not in allowed:
235:             choices = ", ".join(profile.value for profile in allowed)
236:             raise NotAuthority(
237:                 f"descriptor role {self._role.value!r} does not permit "
238:                 f"{operation}; permitted roles: {choices}"
239:             )
240: 
241:     def _require_lineage(self, lineage: str, operation: str) -> None:
242:         if self._lineage is not None and lineage != self._lineage:
243:             raise NotAuthority(
244:                 f"descriptor names lineage {self._lineage}, but {operation} "
245:                 f"names {lineage}; a descriptor is checked against genesis "
246:                 "and never replaces it"
247:             )
248: 
249:     def mint(self, options: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Head:
250:         self._require_role("mint", Profile.AUTHORITY)
251:         if self._lineage is not None:
252:             proposed = options.get("lineage")
253:             if not isinstance(proposed, str):
254:                 raise NotAuthority(
255:                     f"descriptor pins lineage {self._lineage}; mint must name "
256:                     "that lineage explicitly"
257:                 )
258:             self._require_lineage(proposed, "mint")
259:         # The backend contract requires mint(options) to use the offered
260:         # lineage. Do not turn a backend breach discovered after durable mint
261:         # into a precommit-looking descriptor refusal that suppresses the
262:         # attestation wrapper's bootstrap receipt.
263:         return self._ledger.mint(options)
264: 
265:     def append(
266:         self, expected: Head | None, drafts: Sequence[RecordDraft]
267:     ) -> Commit:
268:         self._require_role("append", Profile.AUTHORITY)
269:         if expected is not None:
270:             self._require_lineage(expected.lineage, "append expectation")
271:         return self._ledger.append(expected, drafts)
272: 
273:     def replicate(
274:         self, expected: Head | None, records: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]
275:     ) -> Commit:
276:         self._require_role("replicate", Profile.AUTHORITY, Profile.REPLICA)
277:         if expected is not None:
278:             self._require_lineage(expected.lineage, "replication expectation")
279:         if self._lineage is not None:
280:             for index, record in enumerate(records):
281:                 lineage = record.get("lin")
282:                 if not isinstance(lineage, str):
283:                     raise NotAuthority(
284:                         f"descriptor pins lineage {self._lineage}; replication "
285:                         f"record {index} must name a string lineage before "
286:                         "the target is modified"
287:                     )
288:                 self._require_lineage(lineage, f"replication record {index}")
289:         # Every carried record was pinned before delegation. A post-commit
290:         # mismatch would be a backend conformance failure, and raising here
291:         # would prevent the attestation wrapper from witnessing durable data.
292:         return self._ledger.replicate(expected, records)
293: 
294:     def head(self, lineage: str | None = None) -> Head:
295:         self._require_role("head", Profile.AUTHORITY, Profile.REPLICA)
296:         requested = self._lineage if lineage is None else lineage
297:         if requested is not None:
298:             self._require_lineage(requested, "head request")
299:         head = self._ledger.head(requested)
300:         self._require_lineage(head.lineage, "presented genesis")
301:         return head
302: 
303:     def head_at(self, watermark: Watermark) -> Head:
304:         self._require_role("head_at", Profile.AUTHORITY, Profile.REPLICA)
305:         self._require_lineage(watermark.lineage, "watermark")
306:         head = self._ledger.head_at(watermark)
307:         self._require_lineage(head.lineage, "watermark result")
308:         return head
309: 
310:     def read(self, coordinate: int) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
311:         self._require_role("read", Profile.AUTHORITY, Profile.REPLICA)
312:         record = self._ledger.read(coordinate)
313:         lineage = record.get("lin")
314:         if isinstance(lineage, str):
315:             self._require_lineage(lineage, "read result")
316:         return record
317: 
318:     def scan(
319:         self, *, after: int | None = None, through: Head | None = None
320:     ) -> Iterator[Mapping[str, Any]]:
321:         self._require_role("scan", Profile.AUTHORITY, Profile.REPLICA)
322:         if through is not None:
323:             self._require_lineage(through.lineage, "scan bound")
324:         for record in self._ledger.scan(after=after, through=through):
325:             lineage = record.get("lin")
326:             if isinstance(lineage, str):
327:                 self._require_lineage(lineage, "scan result")
328:             yield record
329: 
330:     def verify(self, scope: VerifyScope) -> Head:
331:         self._require_role(
332:             "verify", Profile.AUTHORITY, Profile.REPLICA, Profile.ARCHIVE
333:         )
334:         through = getattr(scope, "through", None)
335:         if isinstance(through, Head):
336:             self._require_lineage(through.lineage, "verification bound")
337:         head = self._ledger.verify(scope)
338:         self._require_lineage(head.lineage, "presented genesis")
339:         return head
340: 
341:     def export(self, *, through: Head, codec: str) -> ExportedPrefix:
342:         self._require_role("export", Profile.AUTHORITY, Profile.REPLICA)
343:         self._require_lineage(through.lineage, "export bound")
344:         exported = self._ledger.export(through=through, codec=codec)
345:         self._require_lineage(exported.head.lineage, "export result")
346:         return exported
347: 
348:     def capabilities(self) -> Capabilities:
349:         return self._ledger.capabilities()
350: 
351:     def close(self) -> None:
352:         """Release the adapter custody handle, when it owns one."""
353:         close = getattr(self._ledger, "close", None)
354:         if callable(close):
355:             close()
356: 
357: 
358: #: The opener performs no recovery, and that is what makes the seam above it
359: #: honest rather than merely early. ``ArrivalLog`` construction does no I/O at
360: #: all, and ``FileQuery`` now builds its read handle lazily (§0.4), so nothing
361: #: on this path can catch up, truncate a torn tail, or rebuild an index before
362: #: the comparison has seen the store as it actually is.
363: 
364: 
365: class BackendRegistry:
366:     """Backend name → opener, and nothing more.
367: 
368:     A lookup table, not a cache: it holds no open handles and opening the
369:     same descriptor twice yields two independent pairs. Custody lifetime
370:     belongs to whoever asked for it.
371:     """
372: 
373:     def __init__(self) -> None:
374:         self._openers: dict[str, BackendOpener] = {}
375:         self._bindings: dict[str, BindingProvider] = {}
376:         self._maintenance_openers: dict[str, MaintenanceOpener] = {}
377:         self._search_maintenance_openers: dict[str, SearchMaintenanceOpener] = {}
378: 
379:     @classmethod
380:     def with_builtin_backends(cls) -> BackendRegistry:
381:         """A registry holding the adapters that ship in this package."""
382:         registry = cls()
383:         registry.register(
384:             FILE_BACKEND,
385:             _open_file_backend,
386:             binding_provider=_file_binding,
387:             maintenance_opener=_open_file_maintenance,
388:             search_maintenance_opener=_open_file_search_maintenance,
389:         )
390:         return registry
391: 
392:     def register(
393:         self,
394:         name: str,
395:         opener: BackendOpener,
396:         *,
397:         binding_provider: BindingProvider | None = None,
398:         maintenance_opener: MaintenanceOpener | None = None,
399:         search_maintenance_opener: SearchMaintenanceOpener | None = None,
400:     ) -> None:
401:         """Bind a backend name to its opener.
402: 
403:         Re-registering a name refuses. A silent overwrite would let import
404:         order decide which adapter answers to "file", and the failure would
405:         surface as a store opening through the wrong codec rather than as an
406:         error at the point of the mistake. ``binding_provider`` and the
407:         private ``maintenance_opener`` are separate metadata so the opener's
408:         tuple remains stable. Binding is checked before the opener runs, and
409:         maintenance remains reachable only through the attested coordinator.
410:         """
411:         if name in self._openers:
412:             raise ValueError(f"backend {name!r} is already registered")
413:         self._openers[name] = opener
414:         self._bindings[name] = (
415:             binding_provider
416:             if binding_provider is not None
417:             else lambda descriptor, backend=name: opaque_binding(
418:                 backend, descriptor.location
419:             )
420:         )
421:         if maintenance_opener is not None:
422:             self._maintenance_openers[name] = maintenance_opener
423:         if search_maintenance_opener is not None:
424:             self._search_maintenance_openers[name] = search_maintenance_opener
425: 
426:     def registered(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
427:         """The backend names this registry answers to, sorted."""
428:         return tuple(sorted(self._openers))
429: 
430:     def _maintenance_for(self, descriptor: StoreDescriptor) -> ProjectionMaintenance:
431:         """Private adapter factory used only after an attested registry open.
432: 
433:         The public maintenance entry point is
434:         :func:`engine.arrival_maintenance.sync_projection`. Keeping this
435:         factory private prevents a raw derived-state authority from becoming a
436:         second registry open path that omits head attestation.
437:         """
438:         opener = self._maintenance_openers.get(descriptor.backend)
439:         if opener is None:
440:             raise NotSupported(
441:                 f"backend {descriptor.backend!r} does not provide projection maintenance"
442:             )
443:         return opener(descriptor)
444: 
445:     def _search_maintenance_for(self, descriptor: StoreDescriptor) -> SearchIndexMaintenance:
446:         """Private FTS provider reached only by the attested coordinator."""
447:         opener = self._search_maintenance_openers.get(descriptor.backend)
448:         if opener is None:
449:             raise NotSupported(
450:                 f"backend {descriptor.backend!r} does not provide search maintenance"
451:             )
452:         return opener(descriptor)
453: 
454:     def open(
455:         self, descriptor: StoreDescriptor
456:     ) -> tuple[ArrivalLedger, ArrivalQuery]:
457:         """The custody half and the reads half for this descriptor.
458: 
459:         Refuses an unregistered name with
460:         :class:`~engine.arrival_contract.UnknownBackend`, naming what IS
461:         registered. Refusing is the whole point: §02 forbids inferring a
462:         backend from a suffix, so an unregistered name has no fallback to
463:         degrade into — and a registry that guessed would reintroduce
464:         inference at the one seam built to remove it.
465: 
466:         **The custody half comes back wrapped, always, with no way to ask for
467:         it unwrapped** (slice 3 §D.1). :class:`~engine.arrival_head_seam.AttestedLedger`
468:         compares the head this store presents against the one this machine last
469:         accepted, and journals every commit made through it. A configuration
470:         switch for a safety property is a shape the arrival vocabulary
471:         ratchet's own denylist rejects: custody is structural rather than
472:         configured, so there is no flag here and no second opener that skips
473:         the comparison.
474: 
475:         The comparison happens at construction, which means **this method
476:         raises what the comparison raises**: a rollback, a fork, a rewrite, a
477:         replacement or a lost store refuses the open rather than being reported
478:         afterwards by a caller who might not ask. This is also why the seam
479:         sits here rather than deeper — the opener above performs no recovery,
480:         so the comparison sees the store as it is rather than as a catch-up
481:         just repaired it.
482:         """
483:         ledger, query, binding = self._open_components(descriptor)
484:         try:
485:             attested = AttestedLedger(
486:                 ledger, location=descriptor.location, query=query, binding=binding
487:             )
488:         except BaseException:
489:             _close_handle(query)
490:             _close_handle(ledger)
491:             raise
492:         return attested, query
493: 
494: 
495:     def _open_components(
496:         self, descriptor: StoreDescriptor
497:     ) -> tuple[ArrivalLedger, ArrivalQuery, BindingIdentity]:
498:         """Private resources for attested open or the bounded restore procedure.
499: 
500:         Never returned to consumers. Both callers own comparison, witnessing,
501:         and closure; restoration exposes only its completed receipt.
502:         """
503:         opener = self._openers.get(descriptor.backend)
504:         if opener is None:
505:             known = ", ".join(self.registered()) or "(none)"
506:             raise UnknownBackend(
507:                 f"no adapter registered for backend {descriptor.backend!r} "
508:                 f"(registered: {known})"
509:             )
510:         provider = self._bindings[descriptor.backend]
511:         try:
512:             binding = provider(descriptor)
513:         except BindingProbeError as exc:
514:             raise BindingProbeUnanswered(
515:                 f"{descriptor.location} exists and storage would not establish "
516:                 f"its file identity ({exc}); nothing has been accepted or "
517:                 "written — repair the location or retry"
518:             ) from exc
519:         _validate_binding(descriptor, binding)
520:         ledger, query = opener(descriptor)
521:         try:
522:             capabilities = ledger.capabilities()
523:             if (
524:                 descriptor.role is not None
525:                 and descriptor.role not in capabilities.profiles
526:             ):
527:                 offered = ", ".join(
528:                     sorted(profile.value for profile in capabilities.profiles)
529:                 ) or "(none)"
530:                 raise NotSupported(
531:                     f"backend {descriptor.backend!r} cannot open location "
532:                     f"{descriptor.location!r} as {descriptor.role.value}: "
533:                     f"capabilities advertise {offered}"
534:                 )
535: 
536:             # Check a presented genesis before AttestedLedger can turn a
537:             # first-contact head into a bootstrap journal entry. An absent or
538:             # not-yet-minted ledger has no genesis to compare; its later mint
539:             # remains pinned by _DescriptorLedger.
540:             if descriptor.lineage is not None:
541:                 try:
542:                     presented = ledger.verify(Open())
543:                 except Exception:  # backend-specific absence/corruption
544:                     presented = None
545:                 if (
546:                     presented is not None
547:                     and presented.lineage != descriptor.lineage
548:                 ):
549:                     raise NotAuthority(
550:                         f"descriptor names lineage {descriptor.lineage}, but "
551:                         f"the genesis at {descriptor.location!r} opens "
552:                         f"{presented.lineage}; the declaration is a check, "
553:                         "not a replacement for genesis"
554:                     )
555: 
556:             return _DescriptorLedger(ledger, descriptor), query, binding
557:         except BaseException:
558:             _close_handle(query)
559:             _close_handle(ledger)
560:             raise
561: 
562:     def restore_forward(
563:         self,
564:         source: StoreDescriptor,
565:         receiver: StoreDescriptor,
566:         *,
567:         through: Head | None = None,
568:     ) -> RestoreForwardResult:
569:         """Restore an existing exact prefix without granting an open handle."""
570:         from .arrival_restore import _restore_forward
571: 
572:         return _restore_forward(self, source, receiver, through=through)

@@ -218,6 +218,26 @@ signs nothing and does not require private custody to remain available.
 Mapped providers are supported on Arrival targets; legacy writers explicitly
 refuse them. The default `CustodyCredentialProvider` remains unchanged.
 
+The runnable [two-observer mapped workload](tests/test_arrival_mapped_workload.py)
+shows the complete public lifecycle: create Alice and Bob bindings under one
+persisted provider root and exact namespace; initialize with Alice; grant Bob
+using Bob's pre-created public key; then emit with both authors. For a locator
+move that keeps the same physical file-backed store, initialize with an
+**absolute** `location` and move only the `.vertex` file while retaining its
+store clause, lineage, and role. Recreate `MappedCredentialProvider` with the
+same provider root, namespace, and receipt observer before continuing work.
+The absolute store location keeps the same store residence, while
+mapped key selection comes only from the provider namespace and exact observer
+label; neither the vertex filename nor its new directory chooses a key.
+
+After relocation, `verify_target(relocated_vertex)` provides a `Full`
+verification of the captured prefix's grammar, dense ordinals, lineage, and
+record-hash chain. It does not establish projection agreement, signature
+authorship, key trust, or the identity of an observer across lineages. The
+mapped workload exercises public writes with captured authorization; the
+[mapped signing tests](tests/test_arrival_mapped_credentials.py) separately
+verify domain-specific signatures and key-history behavior.
+
 `import_legacy(vertex_path, observer, token=...)` explicitly copies a verified
 legacy key into managed storage without changing the original. It preserves
 flat/nested ambiguity and alias refusals. After import, locator moves do not

+"""Public mapped-custody workflow conformance for an Arrival vertex."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+from engine.credentials import CredentialPurpose, CredentialRequest, SigningDomain
+
+from sdk import (
+    AdmissionFailed,
+    ArrivalRefusal,
+    MappedCredentialProvider,
+    emit_batch,
+    emit_fact,
+    grant_observer,
+    init_vertex,
+    read_facts,
+    verify_target,
+)
+
+
+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def _isolated_process_roots(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
+    monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_STATE_HOME", str(tmp_path / "state"))
+    monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", str(tmp_path / "config"))
+    monkeypatch.setenv("LOOPS_HOME", str(tmp_path / "loops-home"))
+
+
+def test_mapped_two_observer_workload_survives_descriptor_relocation(
+    tmp_path: Path,
+) -> None:
+    custody_root = tmp_path / "custody"
+    namespace = "workload-tenant"
+    provider = MappedCredentialProvider(
+        custody_root,
+        namespace=namespace,
+        receipt_observer="alice",
+    )
+    alice = provider.create_binding("alice", token="create-alice")
+    bob = provider.create_binding("bob", token="create-bob")
+    alice_case_alias = provider.create_binding("Alice", token="create-uppercase-alice")
+    assert alice_case_alias.key_ref != alice.key_ref
+
+    vertex = tmp_path / "original" / "workload.vertex"
+    store = (tmp_path / "data" / "workload.arrival").resolve()
+    initialized = init_vertex(
+        vertex,
+        name="workload",
+        store_type="arrival",
+        location=store,
+        observer="alice",
+        credentials=provider,
+    )
+    assert initialized.lineage is not None
+    assert initialized.store is not None
+    assert initialized.store.location == str(store)
+
+    before_grant = verify_target(vertex).verified_through
+    assert before_grant is not None
+    assert initialized.head == {
+        "lineage": before_grant.lineage,
+        "ordinal": before_grant.ordinal,
+        "record_hash": before_grant.record_hash,
+    }
+    with pytest.raises(AdmissionFailed) as ungranted:
+        emit_fact(
+            vertex,
+            "item",
+            {"owner": "bob", "phase": "before-grant"},
+            observer="bob",
+            origin="workload-test",
+            ts=9.0,
+            id_override="bob-before-grant",
+            credentials=provider,
+        )
+    assert ungranted.value.observer == "bob"
+    assert ungranted.value.kind == "item"
+    assert verify_target(vertex).verified_through == before_grant
+
+    granted = grant_observer(
+        vertex,
+        "bob",
+        grants=["item"],
+        key=bob.public_key,
+        observer="alice",
+        credentials=provider,
+    )
+    assert granted.status == "applied"
+    assert granted.commit is not None
+    assert granted.commit.before == before_grant
+
+    batch = emit_batch(
+        vertex,
+        [
+            {
+                "id": "alice-item",
+                "kind": "item",
+                "payload": {"owner": "alice"},
+                "observer": "alice",
+                "origin": "workload-test",
+                "ts": 10.0,
+            },
+            {
+                "id": "bob-item",
+                "kind": "item",
+                "payload": {"owner": "bob"},
+                "observer": "bob",
+                "origin": "workload-test",
+                "ts": 11.0,
+            },
+        ],
+        credentials=provider,
+    )
+    assert batch.atomic is True
+    assert batch.atomicity == "single-append"
+    assert batch.commit is not None
+    assert batch.commit.before == granted.commit.after
+    assert batch.captured_head == batch.commit.before
+    assert batch.witnessed is True
+    assert batch.projection == "synced"
+    assert [(item.id, item.observer, item.stored, item.signed) for item in batch.items] == [
+        ("alice-item", "alice", True, True),
+        ("bob-item", "bob", True, True),
+    ]
+    assert all(item.captured_head == batch.captured_head for item in batch.items)
+    assert all(item.commit is None for item in batch.items)
+    assert all(item.witnessed is True for item in batch.items)
+    assert all(item.projection == "synced" for item in batch.items)
+
+    first_read = read_facts(vertex, limit=20, order="oldest")
+    assert first_read.basis is not None
+    assert first_read.basis.lineage == initialized.lineage
+    assert first_read.basis.captured_head == batch.commit.after
+    assert first_read.basis.projected_through == first_read.basis.captured_head
+    assert [
+        (item["id"], item["observer"], item["payload"])
+        for item in first_read.items
+    ] == [
+        ("alice-item", "alice", {"owner": "alice"}),
+        ("bob-item", "bob", {"owner": "bob"}),
+    ]
+    first_verified = verify_target(vertex)
+    assert first_verified.captured_head == batch.commit.after
+    assert first_verified.verified_through == first_read.basis.captured_head
+    assert first_verified.claims == ("grammar", "density", "lineage", "hash-chain")
+    assert "signature-authorship" in first_verified.excludes
+
+    relocated = tmp_path / "relocated" / "renamed.vertex"
+    relocated.parent.mkdir()
+    original_declaration = vertex.read_bytes()
+    vertex.rename(relocated)
+    assert relocated.read_bytes() == original_declaration
+
+    reopened = MappedCredentialProvider(
+        custody_root,
+        namespace=namespace,
+        receipt_observer="alice",
+    )
+    for observer, created in (("alice", alice), ("bob", bob)):
+        resolved = reopened.resolve(
+            CredentialRequest(
+                namespace,
+                observer,
+                SigningDomain.FACT,
+                CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP,
+            )
+        )
+        assert resolved is not None
+        assert resolved.evidence.key_ref == created.key_ref
+        assert resolved.evidence.public_key == created.public_key
+    resolved_case_alias = reopened.resolve(
+        CredentialRequest(
+            namespace,
+            "Alice",
+            SigningDomain.FACT,
+            CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP,
+        )
+    )
+    assert resolved_case_alias is not None
+    assert resolved_case_alias.evidence.key_ref == alice_case_alias.key_ref
+    assert resolved_case_alias.evidence.key_ref != alice.key_ref
+
+    relocated_verified = verify_target(relocated)
+    assert relocated_verified.verified_through == first_verified.verified_through
+    relocated_read = read_facts(relocated, limit=20, order="oldest")
+    assert relocated_read.basis == first_read.basis
+    assert relocated_read.items == first_read.items
+    assert relocated_read.store is not None
+    assert relocated_read.store.location == str(store)
+
+    other_namespace = MappedCredentialProvider(
+        custody_root,
+        namespace="other-tenant",
+        receipt_observer="alice",
+    )
+    other_namespace.create_binding("alice", token="create-other-tenant-alice")
+    with pytest.raises(ArrivalRefusal) as wrong_namespace:
+        emit_fact(
+            relocated,
+            "item",
+            {"owner": "alice", "phase": "wrong-namespace"},
+            observer="alice",
+            origin="workload-test",
+            ts=11.5,
+            id_override="alice-wrong-namespace",
+            credentials=other_namespace,
+        )
+    assert wrong_namespace.value.source_type == "CredentialBindingRefused"
+    assert wrong_namespace.value.details["captured_head"] == {
+        "lineage": first_verified.verified_through.lineage,
+        "ordinal": first_verified.verified_through.ordinal,
+        "record_hash": first_verified.verified_through.record_hash,
+    }
+    assert verify_target(relocated).verified_through == first_verified.verified_through
+
+    with pytest.raises(AdmissionFailed) as wrong_case:
+        emit_fact(
+            relocated,
+            "item",
+            {"owner": "Alice", "phase": "case-alias"},
+            observer="Alice",
+            origin="workload-test",
+            ts=11.75,
+            id_override="uppercase-alice",
+            credentials=reopened,
+        )
+    assert wrong_case.value.observer == "Alice"
+    assert wrong_case.value.kind == "item"
+    assert verify_target(relocated).verified_through == first_verified.verified_through
+
+    continued = emit_fact(
+        relocated,
+        "item",
+        {"owner": "bob", "phase": "relocated"},
+        observer="bob",
+        origin="workload-test",
+        ts=12.0,
+        id_override="bob-after-relocation",
+        credentials=reopened,
+    )
+    assert continued.stored is True
+    assert continued.signed is True
+    assert continued.commit is not None
+    assert continued.commit.before == first_verified.verified_through
+    assert continued.witnessed is True
+    assert continued.projection == "synced"
+
+    final_read = read_facts(relocated, limit=20, order="oldest")
+    assert final_read.basis is not None
+    assert final_read.basis.lineage == initialized.lineage
+    assert final_read.basis.captured_head == continued.commit.after
+    assert final_read.basis.projected_through == final_read.basis.captured_head
+    assert [
+        (item["id"], item["observer"], item["payload"])
+        for item in final_read.items
+    ] == [
+        ("alice-item", "alice", {"owner": "alice"}),
+        ("bob-item", "bob", {"owner": "bob"}),
+        (
+            "bob-after-relocation",
+            "bob",
+            {"owner": "bob", "phase": "relocated"},
+        ),
+    ]
+    final_verified = verify_target(relocated)
+    assert final_verified.captured_head == continued.commit.after
+    assert final_verified.verified_through == final_read.basis.captured_head
